123D: Unifying Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving Data at Scale
Authors: Daniel Dauner, Valentin Charraut, Bastian Berle, Tianyu Li, Long Nguyen, Jiabao Wang, Changhui Jing, Maximilian Igl, Holger Caesar, Boris Ivanovic, Yiyi Liao, Andreas Geiger, Kashyap Chitta
First: 2026-05-08T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:59:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The pursuit of autonomous driving has produced one of the richest sensor data collections in all of robotics. However, its scale and diversity remain largely untapped. Each dataset adopts different 2D and 3D modalities, such as cameras, lidar, ego states, annotations, traffic lights, and HD maps, with different rates and synchronization schemes. They come in fragmented formats requiring complex dependencies that cannot natively coexist in the same development environment. Further, major inconsistencies in annotation conventions prevent training or measuring generalization across multiple datasets. We present 123D, an open-source framework that unifies such multi-modal driving data through a single API. To handle synchronization, we store each modality as an independent timestamped event stream with no prescribed rate, enabling synchronous or asynchronous access across arbitrary datasets. Using 123D, we consolidate eight real-world driving datasets spanning 3,300 hours and 90,000 kilometers, together with a synthetic dataset with configurable collection scripts, and provide tools for data analysis and visualization. We conduct a systematic study comparing annotation statistics and assessing each dataset's pose and calibration accuracy. Further, we showcase two applications 123D enables: cross-dataset 3D object detection transfer and reinforcement learning for planning, and offer recommendations for future directions. Code and documentation are available at https://github.com/kesai-labs/py123d.
中文标题/摘要
标题：123D：大规模统一多模态自动驾驶数据
自动驾驶研究产生了机器人领域最丰富的传感器数据集，但其规模和多样性尚未被充分挖掘。各数据集采用不同的2D和3D模态（如摄像头、激光雷达、自身状态、标注、交通灯和高精地图），具有不同的采样率和同步方案，格式碎片化，难以在单一开发环境中原生共存。此外，标注规范的重大不一致阻碍了跨数据集的训练与泛化评估。我们提出123D，一个开源框架，通过单一API统一多模态驾驶数据。为处理同步问题，我们以独立的时间戳事件流形式存储每种模态，无固定采样率，支持任意数据集的同步或异步访问。使用123D，我们整合了8个真实世界驾驶数据集（总计3300小时、9万公里），以及一个支持自定义采集脚本的合成数据集，并提供数据分析与可视化工具。我们系统性地比较了标注统计信息，并评估各数据集的姿态与标定精度。此外，我们展示了123D支持的两个应用：跨数据集3D目标检测迁移与规划强化学习，并提出未来发展方向建议。代码与文档见https://github.com/kesai-labs/py123d。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to unify fragmented, multi-modal autonomous driving datasets with inconsistent annotations and synchronization schemes, 123D introduces an open-source framework that treats each sensor modality as an independent timestamped event stream, enabling flexible synchronous or asynchronous access. The system consolidates eight real-world datasets totaling 3,300 hours and 90,000 kilometers, plus a configurable synthetic dataset, and provides tools for analysis and visualization. Experimental results show consistent annotation statistics across datasets and reveal varying pose and calibration accuracies, while demonstrating successful cross-dataset 3D object detection transfer and reinforcement learning for planning.
为解决自动驾驶多模态数据因格式分散、标注不一致和同步机制各异而难以整合的问题，123D提出一个开源框架，将各传感器模态作为独立的时间戳事件流存储，支持灵活的同步或异步访问。该框架整合了8个真实世界数据集（总计3300小时、9万公里）及一个可配置的合成数据集，并提供分析与可视化工具。实验表明，各数据集标注统计与标定精度具有一致性，且成功实现了跨数据集3D目标检测迁移与强化学习规划应用，验证了123D在推动自动驾驶研究规模化与互操作性方面的有效性。
LLMs Improving LLMs: Agentic Discovery for Test-Time Scaling
Authors: Tong Zheng, Haolin Liu, Chengsong Huang, Huiwen Bao, Sheng Zhang, Rui Liu, Runpeng Dai, Ruibo Chen, Chenxi Liu, Tianyi Xiong, Xidong Wu, Hongming Zhang, Heng Huang
First: 2026-05-08T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:59:40+00:00
Comments: 25 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Test-time scaling (TTS) has become an effective approach for improving large language model performance by allocating additional computation during inference. However, existing TTS strategies are largely hand-crafted: researchers manually design reasoning patterns and tune heuristics by intuition, leaving much of the computation-allocation space unexplored. We propose an environment-driven framework, AutoTTS, that changes what researchers design: from individual TTS heuristics to environments where TTS strategies can be discovered automatically. The key to AutoTTS lies in environment construction: the discovery environment must make the control space tractable and provide cheap, frequent feedback for TTS search. As a concrete instantiation, we formulate width--depth TTS as controller synthesis over pre-collected reasoning trajectories and probe signals, where controllers decide when to branch, continue, probe, prune, or stop and can be evaluated cheaply without repeated LLM calls. We further introduce beta parameterization to make the search tractable and fine-grained execution trace feedback to improve discovery efficiency by helping the agent diagnose why a TTS program fails. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that the discovered strategies improve the overall accuracy--cost tradeoff over strong manually designed baselines. The discovered strategies generalize to held-out benchmarks and model scales, while the entire discovery costs only $39.9 and 160 minutes. Our data, and code will be open-source at https://github.com/zhengkid/AutoTTS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过代理发现实现测试时扩展：LLMs改进LLMs
测试时扩展（TTS）已成为通过在推理期间分配额外计算来提升大语言模型性能的有效方法。然而，现有TTS策略大多由人工设计：研究人员凭直觉手动设计推理模式并调整启发式方法，导致大量计算分配空间未被探索。我们提出一种环境驱动框架AutoTTS，改变研究人员设计的内容：从单个TTS启发式策略转变为可自动发现TTS策略的环境。AutoTTS的核心在于环境构建：发现环境必须使控制空间可处理，并提供廉价、频繁的TTS搜索反馈。作为具体实现，我们将宽度-深度TTS形式化为基于预收集推理轨迹和探测信号的控制器合成问题，其中控制器决定何时分支、继续、探测、剪枝或停止，且无需重复调用LLM即可低成本评估。我们进一步引入beta参数化以使搜索可处理且精细，并通过执行轨迹反馈提升发现效率，帮助代理诊断TTS程序失败的原因。在数学推理基准上的实验表明，发现的策略在整体准确率-成本权衡上优于强人工设计基线。这些策略可泛化至未见基准和模型规模，而整个发现过程仅花费39.9美元和160分钟。我们的数据和代码将在https://github.com/zhengkid/AutoTTS开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manually designed test-time scaling (TTS) strategies for large language models, the authors propose AutoTTS, an environment-driven framework that automates the discovery of TTS heuristics. The method constructs a controllable environment where TTS strategies are synthesized as controllers over pre-collected reasoning trajectories and probe signals, enabling cheap evaluation without repeated LLM calls. Enhanced by beta parameterization and fine-grained execution trace feedback, the framework allows agents to diagnose failures and refine strategies. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AutoTTS discovers TTS strategies that outperform hand-crafted baselines in accuracy-cost tradeoff, generalize across benchmarks and model scales, and achieve this with minimal cost: $39.9 and 160 minutes.
为解决现有大语言模型测试时扩展策略依赖人工设计、效率低下的问题，本文提出AutoTTS框架，通过构建可控搜索环境自动发现最优扩展策略。该方法利用预收集的推理轨迹与探针信号，将扩展策略建模为控制器合成问题，控制器决定分支、继续、探针、剪枝或停止等操作，且评估成本低廉无需重复调用大模型。引入beta参数化与细粒度执行轨迹反馈机制提升搜索效率。实验表明，所发现策略在数学推理基准上优于人工设计基线，具备跨基准与模型规模的泛化能力，且仅需39.9美元和160分钟的发现成本。
Privately Estimating Black-Box Statistics
Authors: Günter F. Steinke, Thomas Steinke
First: 2025-09-30T22:28:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:57:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard techniques for differentially private estimation, such as Laplace or Gaussian noise addition, require guaranteed bounds on the sensitivity of the estimator in question. But such sensitivity bounds are often large or simply unknown. Thus we seek differentially private methods that can be applied to arbitrary black-box functions. A handful of such techniques exist, but all are either inefficient in their use of data or require evaluating the function on exponentially many inputs. In this work we present a scheme that trades off between statistical efficiency (i.e., how much data is needed) and oracle efficiency (i.e., the number of evaluations). We also present lower bounds showing the near-optimality of our scheme.
中文标题/摘要
标题：私有化估计黑盒统计量
差分隐私估计的标准技术（如拉普拉斯或高斯噪声添加）需要对所涉估计量的敏感度有确定的界。但此类敏感度界往往较大或根本未知。因此我们寻求可应用于任意黑盒函数的差分隐私方法。目前已存在少量此类技术，但均要么数据使用效率低下，要么需要在指数级数量的输入上评估函数。本文提出一种在统计效率（所需数据量）与查询效率（评估次数）之间进行权衡的方案，并给出下界证明该方案近乎最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of applying differential privacy to black-box functions where sensitivity bounds are either unknown or too large, this work proposes a new method that balances statistical efficiency (data usage) and oracle efficiency (function evaluations). The approach avoids the inefficiencies of existing techniques that require exponentially many evaluations or excessive data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves near-optimal performance, with theoretical lower bounds confirming its efficiency.
由于标准差分隐私估计方法需要已知且常较大的敏感度界，而这类界在黑盒函数中往往未知或过大，本文提出了一种适用于任意黑盒函数的差分隐私方案。该方法在统计效率（最小化所需数据量）与查询效率（最小化函数调用次数）之间进行权衡，通过自适应采样估计函数行为。实验结果表明该方案性能接近最优，理论下界证明其相对于需大量数据或指数级调用的现有方法具有高效性。
Normalizing Trajectory Models
Authors: Jiatao Gu, Tianrong Chen, Ying Shen, David Berthelot, Shuangfei Zhai, Josh Susskind
First: 2026-05-08T17:57:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:57:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based models decompose sampling into many small Gaussian denoising steps -- an assumption that breaks down when generation is compressed to a few coarse transitions. Existing few-step methods address this through distillation, consistency training, or adversarial objectives, but sacrifice the likelihood framework in the process. We introduce Normalizing Trajectory Models (NTM), which models each reverse step as an expressive conditional normalizing flow with exact likelihood training. Architecturally, NTM combines shallow invertible blocks within each step with a deep parallel predictor across the trajectory, forming an end-to-end network trainable from scratch or initializable from pretrained flow-matching models. Its exact trajectory likelihood further enables self-distillation: a lightweight denoiser trained on the model's own score produces high-quality samples in four steps. On text-to-image benchmarks, NTM matches or outperforms strong image generation baselines in just four sampling steps while uniquely retaining exact likelihood over the generative trajectory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：归一化轨迹模型
基于扩散的模型将采样分解为许多小的高斯去噪步骤——这一假设在生成被压缩为少数粗略过渡时失效。现有少数步骤方法通过蒸馏、一致性训练或对抗目标解决此问题，但在此过程中牺牲了似然框架。我们引入归一化轨迹模型（NTM），将每个反向步骤建模为具有精确似然训练的表达性条件归一化流。架构上，NTM在每一步内结合浅层可逆块，并在轨迹上使用深层并行预测器，形成可从零训练或从预训练流匹配模型初始化的端到端网络。其精确轨迹似然进一步支持自蒸馏：一个轻量级去噪器在模型自身分数上训练，可在四步内生成高质量样本。在文本到图像基准测试中，NTM仅用四步采样即可匹配或超越强图像生成基线，同时独特地保留生成轨迹的精确似然。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of diffusion models when compressed to few steps, this paper proposes Normalizing Trajectory Models (NTM) that model each reverse step as a conditional normalizing flow with exact likelihood training. NTM combines shallow invertible blocks per step with a deep parallel predictor across the trajectory, enabling end-to-end training from scratch or initialization from pretrained models. Experiments show NTM matches or surpasses strong baselines on text-to-image benchmarks using only four sampling steps while preserving exact trajectory likelihood, and its self-distillation capability further enhances sample quality.
为了解决扩散模型在压缩采样步数时性能下降的问题，本文提出归一化轨迹模型（NTM），将每个逆向去噪步骤建模为带精确似然训练的条件归一化流。NTM在每一步使用浅层可逆块，并通过深度并行预测器跨轨迹建模，支持从零训练或初始化预训练模型。实验表明，NTM仅用四步即可达到或超越主流图像生成基线，在保持轨迹精确似然的同时，其自蒸馏机制还能进一步提升样本质量。
Conformal Path Reasoning: Trustworthy Knowledge Graph Question Answering via Path-Level Calibration
Authors: Shuhang Lin, Chuhao Zhou, Xiao Lin, Zihan Dong, Kuan Lu, Zhencan Peng, Jie Yin, Dimitris N. Metaxas
First: 2026-05-08T17:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:57:13+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables;
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) has shown promise for grounded and interpretable reasoning, yet existing approaches often fail to provide reliable coverage guarantees over retrieved answers. While Conformal Prediction (CP) offers a principled framework for producing prediction sets with statistical guarantees, prior methods suffer from critical limitations in both calibration validity and score discriminability, resulting in violated coverage guarantees and excessively large prediction sets. To address these pitfalls, we propose Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR), a trustworthy KGQA framework with two key innovations. First, we perform query-level conformal calibration over path-level scores, preserving the exchangeability while generating path prediction sets. Second, we introduce the Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet), a lightweight module trained via PUCT-guided exploration to learn discriminative path-level nonconformity scores. Experiments on benchmarks show that CPR significantly improves the Empirical Coverage Rate by 34% while reducing average prediction set size by 40% compared to conformal baselines. These results validate the efficacy of CPR in satisfying coverage guarantees with substantially more compact answer sets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符合性路径推理：通过路径级校准实现可信知识图谱问答
知识图谱问答（KGQA）在接地和可解释推理方面展现出潜力，但现有方法往往无法为检索到的答案提供可靠的覆盖保证。虽然符合性预测（CP）提供了一个具有统计保证的预测集生成框架，但先前方法在校准有效性与得分区分性方面存在关键局限，导致覆盖保证被违反且预测集过大。为解决这些问题，我们提出符合性路径推理（CPR），一种可信的KGQA框架，包含两项关键创新：首先，在路径级得分上进行查询级符合性校准，保持交换性的同时生成路径预测集；其次，引入残差符合性值网络（RCVNet），一种轻量级模块，通过PUCT引导探索训练，学习具有区分性的路径级非符合性得分。在基准测试中，CPR相比符合性基线方法将经验覆盖率提升34%，平均预测集大小减少40%。这些结果验证了CPR在满足覆盖保证的同时，能显著压缩答案集的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of reliable coverage guarantees in existing Knowledge Graph Question Answering systems, this paper introduces Conformal Path Reasoning (CPR), a framework that calibrates path-level scores using conformal prediction to ensure statistical coverage while maintaining interpretability. CPR employs query-level conformal calibration over path scores to preserve exchangeability and generates calibrated prediction sets, complemented by a lightweight Residual Conformal Value Network (RCVNet) trained via PUCT-guided exploration to enhance score discriminability. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate that CPR improves empirical coverage rate by 34% and reduces average prediction set size by 40% compared to prior conformal methods, validating its effectiveness in delivering trustworthy, compact answer sets with guaranteed coverage.
为解决现有知识图谱问答系统缺乏可靠覆盖保证的问题，本文提出Conformal Path Reasoning（CPR）框架，通过路径级分数校准确保统计有效性并保持可解释性。CPR在查询级别对路径分数进行符合性校准，生成校准预测集，并引入轻量级神经模块RCVNet，通过PUCT引导探索学习判别性非符合性分数。实验表明，与现有符合性方法相比，CPR将经验覆盖率提升34%，平均预测集大小减少40%，验证了其在提供可信且紧凑答案集方面的有效性。
Zero-Shot Imagined Speech Decoding via Imagined-to-Listened MEG Mapping
Authors: Maryam Maghsoudi, Shihab Shamma
First: 2026-05-08T17:56:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:56:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decoding imagined speech from non-invasive brain recordings is challenging because imagined datasets are scarce and difficult to align temporally across subjects and sessions In this work, we propose a new approach to the decoding of imagined speech that leverages the richer and more reliably labeled recordings during listening to speech. We collected paired listened and imagined MEG recordings to rhythmic melodic and spoken stimuli from trained musicians. Using trained musicians helped improve temporal alignment across conditions. We then developed a three-stage decoding pipeline that revealed consistent and meaningful relationships between neural activity evoked by imagining and listening to the same stimuli. First, we trained six linear and neural models to map imagined MEG responses to listened responses. We evaluated these models against a null baseline from unseen subjects to validate that the predicted-listening responses preserve stimulus-specific information. In the second stage, we trained a contrastive word decoder exclusively on the listened MEG responses, and evaluated it using four embedding strategies including semantic, acoustic, and phonetic representations. In the third stage, we process the imagined MEG responses from held-out subjects through the mapping pipeline to compute the corresponding listening responses that are then decoded by the listened decoder. Using rank-based analysis, we show that the imagined words are decodable significantly above chance. We shall report here the results of a proof-of-concept implementation to decode imagined speech, where all evaluations are performed on held-out subjects. We also demonstrate that performance improves with training data size, suggesting that this approach is scalable and can directly be made applicable to realistic brain-computer interface scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过想象到聆听的MEG映射实现零样本想象语音解码
从非侵入性脑记录中解码想象语音具有挑战性，因为想象语音数据稀缺且难以在不同受试者和会话间进行时间对齐。本研究提出了一种新的想象语音解码方法，利用聆听语音时更丰富且标注更可靠的记录。我们收集了受过训练的音乐家在节奏旋律和口语刺激下的配对聆听与想象MEG记录，使用受过训练的音乐家有助于改善不同条件下的时间对齐。随后，我们开发了一个三阶段解码流程，揭示了想象与聆听相同刺激所引发的神经活动之间的一致且有意义的关系。第一阶段，我们训练了六个线性和神经模型，将想象MEG响应映射到聆听响应，并通过未见受试者的零基线验证预测聆听响应保留了刺激特异性信息。第二阶段，我们仅使用聆听MEG响应训练了一个对比词解码器，并采用四种嵌入策略（语义、声学和语音）进行评估。第三阶段，我们将未见受试者的想象MEG响应通过映射流程处理，计算对应的聆听响应，再由聆听解码器进行解码。通过基于排名的分析，我们证明想象词汇的解码显著高于随机水平。本文报告了该概念验证实现的解码想象语音结果，所有评估均在未见受试者上进行。我们还表明，随着训练数据量的增加，性能提升，说明该方法具有可扩展性，可直接应用于现实的脑机接口场景。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of decoding imagined speech from non-invasive MEG recordings, where imagined speech datasets are sparse and poorly aligned across subjects. The authors leverage paired imagined and listened MEG data from trained musicians to improve temporal alignment and develop a three-stage decoding pipeline: first, they train models to map imagined MEG to listened MEG responses; second, they train a contrastive word decoder on listened data using semantic, acoustic, and phonetic embeddings; third, they apply the mapping to imagined data from unseen subjects and decode the resulting simulated listening responses. Results show imagined words are decodable significantly above chance using rank-based analysis, and performance improves with more training data, indicating scalability for real-world brain-computer interfaces.
该研究旨在解决从非侵入式MEG记录中解码想象语音的难题，因想象语音数据稀少且跨被试和会话难以对齐。作者利用受过训练的音乐家提供的想象与聆听配对MEG数据，提升时间对齐，并构建三阶段解码流程：首先，用六种模型将想象MEG映射至聆听MEG；其次，仅用聆听MEG训练对比词解码器，采用四种嵌入策略（语义、声学、语音）评估；最后，将未见被试的想象MEG经映射后解码。结果表明，想象语音可显著高于随机水平被解码，且随训练数据量增加性能提升，显示该方法可扩展应用于真实脑机接口场景。
GRAPHLCP: Structure-Aware Localized Conformal Prediction on Graphs
Authors: Peyman Baghershahi, Fangxin Wang, Debmalya Mandal, Sourav Medya
First: 2026-05-08T17:56:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:56:09+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 9 Figures, 8 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal prediction (CP) provides a distribution-free approach to uncertainty quantification with finite-sample guarantees. However, applying CP to graph neural networks (GNNs) remains challenging as the combinatorial nature of graphs often leads to insufficiently certain predictions and indiscriminative embeddings. Existing methods primarily rely on embedding-space proximity for localization, which can be unreliable for graphs and yield inefficient prediction sets. We propose GRAPHLCP, a proximity-based localized CP framework that explicitly incorporates graph topology and inter-node dependencies into localization and weighting. Our approach introduces a feature-aware densification step to mitigate locality bias in sparse graphs, followed by a Personalized PageRank-based kernel computation to model structural proximity. This enables topology-dependent anchor sampling and calibration weighting that captures both local and long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments on several regression and classification datasets demonstrate that GRAPHLCP guarantees marginal coverage with finite samples while efficiently attaining favorable test conditional coverage across various conditioning scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRAPHLCP：图结构感知的局部置信预测
置信预测（CP）提供了一种无需分布假设的不确定性量化方法，并具有有限样本保证。然而，将CP应用于图神经网络（GNN）仍具挑战性，因为图的组合特性常导致预测不够确定且嵌入无区分度。现有方法主要依赖嵌入空间邻近性进行局部化，这对图结构不可靠，且预测集效率低下。我们提出GRAPHLCP，一种基于邻近性的局部化CP框架，显式将图拓扑与节点间依赖关系纳入局部化和加权过程。该方法引入特征感知稠密化步骤以缓解稀疏图中的局部性偏差，随后通过个性化PageRank核计算建模结构邻近性，从而实现依赖拓扑的锚点采样与校准加权，捕捉局部与长程依赖关系。在多个回归与分类数据集上的广泛实验表明，GRAPHLCP在有限样本下保证边际覆盖，同时在多种条件场景下高效实现有利的测试条件覆盖。

Motivated by the challenge of applying conformal prediction to graph neural networks due to their combinatorial structure and unreliable embedding-based localization, the authors propose GRAPHLCP, a framework that integrates graph topology into localized conformal prediction. The method first densifies sparse graph features to reduce locality bias, then computes a personalized PageRank-based kernel to model structural proximity, enabling topology-aware anchor selection and calibration weighting. Experimental results across multiple graph datasets show that GRAPHLCP achieves finite-sample marginal coverage while maintaining favorable conditional coverage under diverse conditioning scenarios, outperforming baseline methods in both efficiency and accuracy.
为了解决图神经网络因图结构组合性导致的预测不确定性与嵌入空间局部化不可靠的问题，作者提出GRAPHLCP框架，通过显式融合图拓扑结构进行局部化校准。该方法首先通过特征感知的稠密化缓解稀疏图的局部偏差，再利用基于个性化PageRank的核函数建模结构邻近度，指导锚点采样与加权校准。实验表明，GRAPHLCP在多个图数据集上能保证有限样本下的边际覆盖，同时在多种条件场景下实现更优的条件覆盖率，优于现有基线方法。
EmambaIR: Efficient Visual State Space Model for Event-guided Image Reconstruction
Authors: Wei Yu, Yunhang Qian
First: 2026-05-08T17:56:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:56:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent event-based image reconstruction methods predominantly rely on Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) to process complementary event information. However, these architectures face fundamental limitations: CNNs often fail to capture global feature correlations, whereas ViTs incur quadratic computational complexity (e.g., $O(n^2)$), hindering their application in high-resolution scenarios. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce EmambaIR, an Efficient visual State Space Model designed for image reconstruction using spatially sparse and temporally continuous event streams. Our framework introduces two key components: the cross-modal Top-k Sparse Attention Module (TSAM) and the Gated State-Space Module (GSSM). TSAM efficiently performs pixel-level top-k sparse attention to guide cross-modal interactions, yielding rich yet sparse fusion features. Subsequently, GSSM utilizes a nonlinear gated unit to enhance the temporal representation of vanilla linear-complexity ($O(n)$) SSMs, effectively capturing global contextual dependencies without the typical computational overhead. Extensive experiments on six datasets across three diverse image reconstruction tasks - motion deblurring, deraining, and High Dynamic Range (HDR) enhancement - demonstrate that EmambaIR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while offering substantial reductions in memory consumption and computational cost. The source code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/YunhangWickert/EmambaIR
中文标题/摘要
标题：EmambaIR：面向事件引导图像重建的高效视觉状态空间模型
近期基于事件的图像重建方法主要依赖卷积神经网络（CNN）和视觉Transformer（ViT）处理互补事件信息。然而，这些架构存在根本性局限：CNN常难以捕捉全局特征关联，而ViT则面临二次计算复杂度（如O(n²)），限制其在高分辨率场景中的应用。为解决这些瓶颈，我们提出EmambaIR，一种专为利用空间稀疏、时间连续事件流进行图像重建设计的高效视觉状态空间模型。该框架包含两个关键组件：跨模态Top-k稀疏注意力模块（TSAM）和门控状态空间模块（GSSM）。TSAM通过像素级Top-k稀疏注意力高效引导跨模态交互，生成丰富而稀疏的融合特征；随后，GSSM利用非线性门控单元增强传统线性复杂度（O(n)）状态空间模型的时间表示能力，有效捕捉全局上下文依赖关系，且无典型计算开销。在涵盖运动去模糊、除雨和高动态范围（HDR）增强三大图像重建任务的六个数据集上的大量实验表明，EmambaIR显著优于现有最先进方法，同时大幅降低内存消耗和计算成本。源代码与数据已公开：https://github.com/YunhangWickert/EmambaIR

Motivated by the computational inefficiencies of CNNs and ViTs in event-based image reconstruction, EmambaIR introduces a novel state space model that combines a Top-k Sparse Attention Module for efficient cross-modal fusion and a Gated State-Space Module to enhance temporal modeling with linear complexity. Evaluated across six datasets in motion deblurring, deraining, and HDR enhancement tasks, EmambaIR consistently outperforms existing methods while reducing memory and computational demands significantly.
为解决CNN和ViT在事件驱动图像重建中的计算瓶颈，EmambaIR提出一种新型视觉状态空间模型，结合Top-k稀疏注意力模块实现高效跨模态融合，以及门控状态空间模块增强时序建模并保持线性复杂度。在六个数据集上的运动去模糊、去雨和HDR增强任务中，该方法显著优于现有方法，同时大幅降低内存消耗与计算开销。
A Note on Non-Negative $L_1$-Approximating Polynomials
Authors: Jane H. Lee, Anay Mehrotra, Manolis Zampetakis
First: 2026-05-08T17:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:55:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
$L_1$-Approximating polynomials, i.e., polynomials that approximate indicator functions in $L_1$-norm under certain distributions, are widely used in computational learning theory. We study the existence of \textit{non-negative} $L_1$-approximating polynomials with respect to Gaussian distributions. This is a stronger requirement than $L_1$-approximation but weaker than sandwiching polynomials (which themselves have many applications). These non-negative approximating polynomials have recently found uses in smoothed learning from positive-only examples. In this short note, we prove that every class of sets with Gaussian surface area (GSA) at most $Γ$ under the standard Gaussian admits degree-$k$ non-negative polynomials that $\eps$-approximate its indicator functions in $L_1$-norm, for $k=\tilde{O}(Γ^2/\varepsilon^2)$. Equivalently, finite GSA implies $L_1$-approximation with the stronger pointwise guarantee that the approximating polynomial has range contained in $[0,\infty)$. Up to a constant-factor, this matches the degree of the best currently known Gaussian $L_1$-approximation degree bound without the non-negativity constraint.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于非负 $L_1$-逼近多项式的注记
$L_1$-逼近多项式，即在特定分布下以 $L_1$-范数逼近指示函数的多项式，在计算学习理论中广泛应用。我们研究了在高斯分布下非负 $L_1$-逼近多项式的存在性。这一要求比 $L_1$-逼近更强，但弱于具有广泛应用的夹逼多项式。这些非负逼近多项式最近在仅正例的平滑学习中找到了应用。 在本短文里，我们证明：在标准高斯分布下，表面面积（GSA）至多为 $Γ$ 的任意集合类，均存在度数为 $k$ 的非负多项式，以 $\eps$-精度在 $L_1$-范数下逼近其指示函数，其中 $k=\tilde{O}(Γ^2/\varepsilon^2)$。等价地，有限 GSA 意味着 $L_1$-逼近具有更强的逐点保证：逼近多项式的取值范围包含于 $[0,\infty)$。在常数因子范围内，这与当前已知的无非负约束的高斯 $L_1$-逼近度数界相匹配。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by applications in smoothed learning from positive-only examples, this paper investigates the existence of non-negative polynomials that approximate indicator functions in L1-norm under Gaussian distributions. The authors establish that any class of sets with Gaussian surface area at most Γ admits a degree-k non-negative polynomial that ε-approximates its indicator function in L1, where k is approximately Γ²/ε². This result provides a pointwise non-negative approximation guarantee, matching up to constant factors the best known degree bound for Gaussian L1-approximation without non-negativity constraints.
为解决正例学习中的平滑学习问题，本文研究在高斯分布下非负多项式对指示函数的L1逼近问题。作者证明，任意具有高斯表面积不超过Γ的集合类，均存在一个度数为k的非负多项式，能在L1范数下以ε误差逼近其指示函数，其中k约为Γ²/ε²。该结果提供点态非负逼近保证，其度数与当前无非负约束的最优高斯L1逼近度数相差仅常数因子。
Vibe Econometrics and the Analysis Contract
Authors: Lydia Ashton
First: 2026-05-08T17:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:54:47+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 2 figures. Appendices A-C (fillable templates) provided as ancillary file. Companion materials: https://github.com/lydiaashton/vibe-econometrics-supp . Also posted on SSRN: https://doi.org/10.2139/ssrn.6699999
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
"Vibe coding" and "vibe analytics" have been framed as a democratization of technical capability. This paper argues that AI-assisted methodology more broadly, or what I call "vibe methodology," also democratizes the failure modes specific to each domain. When AI assists with methods whose validity depends on assumptions that cannot be verified from the output alone (a class I call "vibe inference"), the failure surface is structurally different: the output does not reliably signal invalidity, and when it does, recognizing the signal requires the expertise the workflow bypasses. I focus on "vibe econometrics," the subset of AI-assisted causal analysis where identification can be named faster than it can be audited. The claim of this paper is not that AI invents inferential failures that did not previously exist, but that it changes their incidence, observability, and persuasive force enough to create a practically distinct governance problem. This results in three failure modes: method-data mismatch, where AI bypasses expertise at execution; confidence laundering, where AI amplifies the credibility of formatted output; and invisible forking, which spans both. What is new is not the failure modes but AI's industrialization of their packaging. The barrier between naming a method and executing it has collapsed, and weak foundations, dressed as rigorous analysis, now reach audiences at a scale, speed, and polish that previously required expertise. I propose the Analysis Contract, a pre-commitment framework that adapts the logic of pre-analysis plans and the Causal Roadmap to the AI-assisted setting. The contract imposes three conditions before a causal claim is made: a method-data contract, a data audit, and a pre-commitment statement defining what would count as a disconfirming result. The framework generalizes across domains of vibe inference through domain-specific instantiation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：氛围计量经济学与分析契约
"氛围编码"和"氛围分析"被描述为技术能力的民主化。本文认为，更广泛的AI辅助方法论，即我所称的"氛围方法论"，也使各领域特有的失败模式民主化。当AI辅助那些其有效性依赖于无法仅从输出中验证的假设的方法（我称之为"氛围推断"）时，失败模式的结构不同：输出不能可靠地指示无效性，且当它能指示时，识别该信号需要被工作流绕过的专业知识。我聚焦于"氛围计量经济学"，即AI辅助因果分析中识别速度快于审计速度的子集。本文主张并非AI创造了此前不存在的推断失败，而是它改变了这些失败的发生率、可观察性与说服力，从而产生一个实际不同的治理问题。这导致三种失败模式：方法-数据不匹配，AI绕过执行时的专业知识；信心洗白，AI放大格式化输出的可信度；以及隐形分叉，涵盖前两者。新意不在于失败模式本身，而在于AI对其包装的工业化。命名方法与执行方法之间的壁垒已崩溃，薄弱基础伪装成严谨分析，如今以此前需专业知识才能达到的规模、速度与精致度触达受众。我提出"分析契约"，一种预承诺框架，将预分析计划与因果路线图的逻辑适配于AI辅助情境。该契约在提出因果主张前施加三个条件：方法-数据契约、数据审计、以及预承诺声明，定义何为否定性结果。该框架通过领域特定实例化，可推广至所有氛围推断领域。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that AI-assisted methodologies, termed 'vibe methodology,' democratize not only technical access but also the failure modes inherent in inference, particularly in 'vibe econometrics' where causal identification outpaces auditability. The author identifies three novel governance challenges: method-data mismatch (AI bypasses expert execution), confidence laundering (AI enhances output credibility), and invisible forking (a combination of both). These failures arise because AI obscures invalidity signals and amplifies the persuasive force of flawed analysis. To address this, the paper proposes the 'Analysis Contract'—a pre-commitment framework requiring a method-data contract, data audit, and pre-defined disconfirming conditions before causal claims are made. The framework is designed to be adaptable across domains of vibe inference through domain-specific instantiation.
本文指出，AI辅助方法（称为‘vibe方法论’）不仅普及了技术能力，也使原本依赖不可验证假设的领域（如计量经济学）中的失效模式得以广泛传播。作者识别出三种新失效模式——方法-数据错配、信心洗牌和隐形分支——当AI绕过专家执行、放大输出可信度并掩盖方法缺陷时，这些失效模式便显现。这些失效本身并非新创，但AI将其工业化、规模化，使薄弱基础伪装成严谨分析。为此，作者提出‘分析契约’框架，要求在提出因果结论前完成方法-数据契约、数据审计及预设反证结果，以在各领域恢复问责制。
VecCISC: Improving Confidence-Informed Self-Consistency with Reasoning Trace Clustering and Candidate Answer Selection
Authors: James Petullo, Sonny George, Dylan Cashman, Nianwen Xue
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-08T17:54:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:54:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Findings of ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A standard technique for scaling inference-time reasoning is Self-Consistency, whereby multiple candidate answers are sampled from an LLM and the most common answer is selected. More recently, it has been shown that weighted majority voting (e.g. Confidence-Informed Self Consistency (CISC)), which assigns a confidence value to each candidate answer and chooses the answer with the largest accumulated score, tends to be more accurate on a wide range of popular benchmarks. In practice, weighted majority voting necessitates calling a critic LLM on each candidate's reasoning trace to produce the answer's confidence score. This secondary series of LLM calls greatly increases the overhead and cost of weighted majority voting, despite its potential performance benefits. To reduce this expense, we propose VecCISC, a lightweight, adaptive framework that uses a measure of semantic similarity to filter reasoning traces that are semantically equivalent to others, degenerate, or hallucinated, thus decreasing the number of candidate answers that must be evaluated by the critic. To ensure adequate experimental thoroughness, we evaluate VecCISC on five challenging, widely-adopted datasets spanning the domains of mathematics, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and the humanities. Our results demonstrate that VecCISC reduces the total token usage by 47%, while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of CISC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VecCISC：通过推理轨迹聚类与候选答案选择提升置信度引导的自一致性
一种在推理时扩展推理的标准技术是自一致性，即从大语言模型（LLM）中采样多个候选答案并选择最常见的答案。最近研究表明，加权多数投票（如置信度引导的自一致性CISC），即为每个候选答案分配置信度值并选择累计得分最高的答案，在多个流行基准测试中表现更优。然而，加权多数投票需要为每个候选答案的推理轨迹调用一个批评者LLM以生成置信度分数，这大大增加了开销和成本。为降低该开销，我们提出VecCISC，一种轻量级自适应框架，利用语义相似度度量过滤语义等价、退化或幻觉的推理轨迹，从而减少需由批评者评估的候选答案数量。为确保实验充分性，我们在涵盖数学、化学、生物、常识推理和人文学科的五个具有挑战性的广泛采用数据集上评估VecCISC。结果表明，VecCISC将总token使用量减少47%，同时保持或超越CISC的准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of confidence-informed self-consistency (CISC), which requires evaluating each candidate answer with a critic LLM, VecCISC introduces a lightweight framework that clusters reasoning traces by semantic similarity to eliminate redundant, degenerate, or hallucinated candidates before critic evaluation. By filtering out semantically equivalent responses, VecCISC reduces the number of LLM calls needed, cutting total token usage by 47% while preserving or improving accuracy across five diverse benchmarks including math, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and humanities.
为降低置信度引导自一致性（CISC）中因需逐个调用批评者LLM评估候选答案而导致的高昂计算开销，VecCISC提出一种轻量级框架，通过语义相似度聚类推理轨迹，提前过滤掉语义重复、退化或幻觉的候选答案，从而减少需提交给批评者评估的候选数量。实验表明，该方法在数学、化学、生物、常识推理和人文五大基准数据集上，总token使用量减少47%，同时保持或超越CISC的准确性。
Surgical Visual Understanding (SurgVU) Dataset
Authors: Aneeq Zia, Max Berniker, Rogerio Nespolo, Xiaorui Zhang, Conor Perreault, Ziheng Wang, Benjamin Mueller, Ryan Schmidt, Kiran Bhattacharyya, Xi Liu, Anthony Jarc
First: 2025-01-16T00:03:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:54:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Owing to recent advances in machine learning and the ability to harvest large amounts of data during robotic-assisted surgeries, surgical data science is ripe for foundational work. We present a large dataset of surgical videos and their accompanying labels for this purpose. We describe how the data was collected and some of its unique attributes. Multiple example problems are outlined. Although the dataset was curated for a particular set of scientific challenges (in an accompanying paper), it is general enough to be used for a broad range machine learning questions. Our hope is that this dataset exposes the larger machine learning community to the challenging problems within surgical data science, and becomes a touch-stone for future research. The videos are available at https://storage.googleapis.com/isi-surgvu/surgvu24_videos_only.zip, the labels at https://storage.googleapis.com/isi-surgvu/surgvu24_labels_updated_v2.zip, a validation set for tool detection problem at https://storage.googleapis.com/isi-surgvu/cat1_test_set_public.zip, and a sample set of question & answer pairs dataset for surgical visual question answering at https://storage.googleapis.com/isi-surgvu/SURGVU25_cat_2_sample_set_public.zip.

DReS: Dual Reconstruction Smoothing for Functional Regularization
Authors: Parsa Moradi, Tayyebeh Jahaninezhad, Hanzaleh Akbarinodehi, Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali
First: 2025-09-30T20:24:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Smoothness is a key inductive bias in machine learning and is closely related to generalization. Existing smoothness-inducing methods typically rely either on explicit gradient regularization, which often incurs substantial computational and memory overhead, or on data-mixing strategies, which are less naturally applicable to unsupervised and self-supervised settings. In this work, we propose $\textit{Dual Reconstruction Smoothing}$ (DReS), a nonparametric regularization framework that induces smoothness through a spline-based auxiliary branch with shared model parameters. The method introduces no additional trainable parameters and can be applied to arbitrary submodules, making it suitable for unsupervised, self-supervised, and supervised regimes. We show theoretically that the discrepancy between the target function and its DReS approximation is controlled by higher-order smoothness quantities of the function, establishing the method as an implicit higher-order smoothness regularizer. Empirically, DReS improves representation learning across several self-supervised methods, improves generation quality in generative modeling, and achieves strong performance relative to competitive baselines in supervised learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DReS：用于函数正则化的双重重建平滑
平滑性是机器学习中的关键归纳偏置，与泛化能力密切相关。现有平滑诱导方法通常依赖显式梯度正则化，常带来显著的计算和内存开销，或依赖数据混合策略，难以自然应用于无监督和自监督场景。本文提出DReS（双重重建平滑），一种非参数正则化框架，通过基于样条的辅助分支（共享模型参数）诱导平滑性。该方法不引入额外可训练参数，可应用于任意子模块，适用于无监督、自监督和监督学习场景。理论分析表明，目标函数与其DReS近似之间的差异由函数的高阶平滑量控制，使该方法成为隐式高阶平滑正则化器。实验表明，DReS在多种自监督方法中提升表征学习能力，在生成建模中提升生成质量，并在监督学习中相对于竞争基线取得优异性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient smoothness regularization that avoids computational overhead and data-mixing limitations, the authors propose DReS, a nonparametric method using a spline-based auxiliary branch sharing parameters with the main model. DReS induces smoothness implicitly without extra parameters, making it adaptable to supervised, self-supervised, and unsupervised settings. Experiments show DReS enhances representation learning in self-supervised tasks, improves generative quality, and outperforms or matches baselines in supervised learning while maintaining computational efficiency.
为解决现有平滑正则化方法计算开销大或难以应用于无监督与自监督场景的问题，作者提出DReS方法，通过共享参数的样条辅助分支隐式诱导平滑性，无需额外可训练参数，适用于任意子模块，兼容无监督、自监督与监督学习。实验表明，DReS可提升自监督表示学习效果、改善生成模型质量，并在监督学习任务中达到或超越主流基线性能，同时保持计算效率。
Proxy3D: Efficient 3D Representations for Vision-Language Models via Semantic Clustering and Alignment
Authors: Jerry Jiang, Haowen Sun, Denis Gudovskiy, Yohei Nakata, Tomoyuki Okuno, Kurt Keutzer, Wenzhao Zheng
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-08T17:50:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:50:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://wzzheng.net/Proxy3D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatial intelligence in vision-language models (VLMs) attracts research interest with the practical demand to reason in the 3D world.Despite promising results, most existing methods follow the conventional 2D pipeline in VLMs and use pixel-aligned representations for the vision modality. However, correspondence-based models with implicit 3D scene understanding often fail to achieve spatial consistency, and representation-based models with 3D geometric priors lack efficiency in vision sequence serialization. To address this, we propose a Proxy3D method with compact yet comprehensive 3D proxy representations for the vision modality. Given only video frames as input, we employ semantic and geometric encoders to extract scene features and then perform their semantic-aware clustering to obtain a set of proxies in the 3D space. For representation alignment, we further curate the SpaceSpan dataset and apply multi-stage training to adopt the proposed 3D proxy representations with the VLM. When using shorter sequences for vision information, our method achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance in 3D visual question answering, visual grounding and general spatial intelligence benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Proxy3D：通过语义聚类与对齐实现视觉-语言模型的高效3D表示
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中的空间智能因实际需求（在3D世界中进行推理）而备受研究关注。尽管现有方法取得一定成果，但多数仍沿用VLMs传统的2D流程，使用像素对齐的视觉表示。然而，基于对应关系的模型因隐式3D场景理解常难以保持空间一致性，而基于表示的模型虽引入3D几何先验，但在视觉序列序列化时效率不足。为此，我们提出Proxy3D方法，为视觉模态构建紧凑且全面的3D代理表示。仅输入视频帧时，我们采用语义与几何编码器提取场景特征，并进行语义感知聚类以获得3D空间中的代理集合。为实现表示对齐，我们进一步构建SpaceSpan数据集，并通过多阶段训练使VLM采纳所提出的3D代理表示。当使用较短序列传递视觉信息时，本方法在3D视觉问答、视觉定位及通用空间智能基准测试中达到竞争性或最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for VLMs to reason effectively in 3D environments, this work addresses limitations of existing 2D-centric methods that lack spatial consistency or computational efficiency. Proxy3D introduces compact 3D proxy representations derived from semantic and geometric clustering of scene features extracted from video frames. By aligning these proxies with vision-language models via multi-stage training on the SpaceSpan dataset, the method achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance on 3D visual question answering, visual grounding, and spatial intelligence benchmarks, even when using shorter video sequences.
为提升视觉语言模型在三维环境中的推理能力，Proxy3D提出通过语义与几何编码对视频帧进行聚类，生成紧凑且全面的三维代理表示。结合SpaceSpan数据集与多阶段训练，该方法将三维代理与视觉语言模型对齐，无需像素级对齐即可实现空间一致性。实验表明，即使使用较短视频序列，该方法在三维视觉问答、视觉定位及空间智能基准测试中达到竞争性或最先进性能。
UNCOM: Zero-shot Context-Aware Command Understanding for Tabletop Scenarios
Authors: Antonio Galiza Cerdeira Gonzalez, Paweł Gajewski, Bipin Indurkhya
First: 2024-10-08T20:46:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:50:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents UNCOM, a novel hybrid framework for interpreting natural human commands in tabletop scenarios. The system integrates multiple sources of information -- speech, gestures, and scene context -- to extract structured, actionable instructions for robots. Addressing the need for general-purpose human-robot interaction in domestic environments, UNCOM is designed for zero-shot operation, without reliance on predefined object models or training data specific to a given task. Using foundational and task-specific deep learning models, it allows out-of-the-box speech recognition, natural language understanding, gesture detection, and object segmentation. The modular architecture enhances transparency and explainability by explicitly parsing commands into object-action-target representations, enabling integration with symbolic robotic frameworks. We demonstrate the system in a TIAGo++ robot and provide an evaluation on a real-world data set of human-robot interaction scenarios; achieving an 82.39\% success rate over our benchmark data set, highlighting the robustness of the system to diversity, noise, and communication ambiguity. The data set, evaluation scenarios, and the code are publicly available to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UNCOM：面向桌面场景的零样本上下文感知指令理解
本文提出UNCOM，一种用于桌面场景中理解自然人类指令的新型混合框架。该系统整合语音、手势和场景上下文等多种信息源，提取结构化、可执行的机器人指令。为满足家庭环境中通用人机交互的需求，UNCOM支持零样本操作，无需依赖预定义物体模型或特定任务的训练数据。通过基础与任务特定的深度学习模型，系统可直接实现语音识别、自然语言理解、手势检测和物体分割。其模块化架构通过显式解析指令为物体-动作-目标表示，增强透明性和可解释性，并支持与符号化机器人框架集成。我们在TIAGo++机器人上演示该系统，并在真实的人机交互场景数据集上进行评估，成功率达82.39%，凸显系统对多样性、噪声和沟通模糊性的鲁棒性。数据集、评估场景及代码均已公开，以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, zero-shot human-robot interaction in domestic tabletop settings, UNCOM integrates speech, gesture, and scene context to generate structured robot commands without predefined object models or task-specific training data. Its modular hybrid architecture combines foundational and task-specific deep learning models to enable real-time speech recognition, natural language understanding, gesture detection, and object segmentation, explicitly parsing commands into object-action-target representations for symbolic robotic frameworks. Evaluated on a real-world human-robot interaction dataset using a TIAGo++ robot, UNCOM achieved an 82.39% success rate, demonstrating robustness to environmental diversity, noise, and communication ambiguity.
为实现家庭环境中通用的人机交互，UNCOM 是一种零样本混合框架，通过融合语音、手势和场景上下文来解析自然指令并生成结构化机器人操作。它利用基础和任务特定的深度学习模型，支持语音识别、自然语言理解、手势检测和物体分割，无需预定义物体模型或任务特定训练数据。在 TIAGo++ 机器人上基于真实人机交互数据集评估，系统成功率达 82.39%，展现出对环境多样性、噪声和沟通模糊性的鲁棒性。
Flow-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Flow Matching Models
Authors: Zhen Fang, Wenxuan Huang, Yu Zeng, Yiming Zhao, Shuang Chen, Kaituo Feng, Yunlong Lin, Lin Chen, Zehui Chen, Shaosheng Cao, Feng Zhao
First: 2026-05-08T17:50:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:50:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing Flow Matching (FM) text-to-image models suffer from two critical bottlenecks under multi-task alignment: the reward sparsity induced by scalar-valued rewards, and the gradient interference arising from jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives, which together give rise to a 'seesaw effect' of competing metrics and pervasive reward hacking. Inspired by the success of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) in the large language model community, we propose Flow-OPD, the first unified post-training framework that integrates on-policy distillation into Flow Matching models. Flow-OPD adopts a two-stage alignment strategy: it first cultivates domain-specialized teacher models via single-reward GRPO fine-tuning, allowing each expert to reach its performance ceiling in isolation; it then establishes a robust initial policy through a Flow-based Cold-Start scheme and seamlessly consolidates heterogeneous expertise into a single student via a three-step orchestration of on-policy sampling, task-routing labeling, and dense trajectory-level supervision. We further introduce Manifold Anchor Regularization (MAR), which leverages a task-agnostic teacher to provide full-data supervision that anchors generation to a high-quality manifold, effectively mitigating the aesthetic degradation commonly observed in purely RL-driven alignment. Built upon Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, Flow-OPD raises the GenEval score from 63 to 92 and the OCR accuracy from 59 to 94, yielding an overall improvement of roughly 10 points over vanilla GRPO, while preserving image fidelity and human-preference alignment and exhibiting an emergent 'teacher-surpassing' effect. These results establish Flow-OPD as a scalable alignment paradigm for building generalist text-to-image models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Flow-OPD：面向流匹配模型的在线策略蒸馏
现有流匹配（FM）文本到图像模型在多任务对齐中面临两大关键瓶颈：标量奖励导致的奖励稀疏性，以及联合优化异构目标引发的梯度干扰，共同导致竞争指标的‘跷跷板效应’和普遍的奖励黑客现象。受大语言模型社区中在线策略蒸馏（OPD）成功启发，我们提出Flow-OPD，这是首个将在线策略蒸馏集成到流匹配模型中的统一后训练框架。Flow-OPD采用两阶段对齐策略：首先通过单奖励GRPO微调培育领域专用教师模型，使每个专家独立达到性能上限；随后通过基于流的冷启动方案建立稳健初始策略，并通过三步协同——在线采样、任务路由标注与密集轨迹级监督——无缝整合异构专家知识至单一学生模型。我们进一步引入流形锚定正则化（MAR），利用任务无关教师提供全数据监督，锚定生成至高质量流形，有效缓解纯强化学习驱动对齐中常见的美学退化问题。基于Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium构建，Flow-OPD将GenEval分数从63提升至92，OCR准确率从59提升至94，相较基础GRPO整体提升约10分，同时保持图像保真度与人类偏好对齐，并展现出‘教师超越’效应。这些结果确立了Flow-OPD作为构建通用文本到图像模型的可扩展对齐范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the reward sparsity and gradient interference issues in multi-task Flow Matching text-to-image models that cause a 'seesaw effect' and reward hacking, Flow-OPD introduces an on-policy distillation framework inspired by large language model techniques. It employs a two-stage strategy: first, it trains domain-specialized teacher models via single-reward GRPO fine-tuning to maximize individual task performance; then, it uses a Flow-based Cold-Start to initialize a student model and integrates heterogeneous expertise through on-policy sampling, task-routing labeling, and trajectory-level supervision. Manifold Anchor Regularization further stabilizes generation by anchoring outputs to a high-quality manifold. Experiments on Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium show GenEval scores rising from 63 to 92 and OCR accuracy from 59 to 94, achieving a 10-point overall gain over vanilla GRPO while preserving image fidelity and human preference, and even demonstrating teacher-surpassing behavior.
为解决流匹配文本到图像模型在多任务对齐中因奖励稀疏和梯度干扰导致的‘跷跷板效应’与奖励滥用问题，作者提出Flow-OPD框架，首次将在线策略蒸馏引入流匹配模型。该方法首先通过单奖励GRPO微调训练各领域专家教师模型，再借助基于流的冷启动建立稳健初始策略，最后通过在线采样、任务路由标注与轨迹级监督将异构专家知识整合至单一学生模型。引入流形锚定正则化以锚定生成至高质量流形，有效缓解纯强化学习对齐中的美学退化。在Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium上，Flow-OPD将GenEval分数从63提升至92，OCR准确率从59提升至94，整体提升约10分，同时保持图像保真度与人类偏好对齐，并展现出超越教师模型的能力。
Rubric-Grounded RL: Structured Judge Rewards for Generalizable Reasoning
Authors: Manish Bhattarai, Ismael Boureima, Nishath Rajiv Ranasinghe, Scott Pakin, Dan O'Malley
First: 2026-05-08T17:48:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:48:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We argue that decomposing reward into weighted, verifiable criteria and using an LLM judge to score them provides a partial-credit optimization signal: instead of a binary outcome or a single holistic score, each response is graded along multiple task-specific criteria. We formalize \emph{rubric-grounded reinforcement learning (RL)}: a framework in which the policy is optimized against a structured, multi-criterion reward produced by a frozen LLM judge that conditions on auxiliary grounding the policy never sees. We instantiate the framework by deriving rubrics from an Office of Scientific and Technical Information (OSTI)-derived corpus of roughly 100,000 scientific and technical documents and training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). With GRPO-based training, the model achieves $71.7\%$ normalized reward on held-out rubric evaluation. The GRPO-tuned policy also improves over the base model on four reasoning benchmarks not derived from the training corpus -- GSM8K, MATH, GPQA Main, and GPQA Diamond. These results provide evidence that structured, document-grounded rewards can improve held-out rubric performance and induce transferable reasoning behaviors beyond the corpus used to construct the training environment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于评分标准的强化学习：用于可泛化推理的结构化评判奖励
我们认为，将奖励分解为加权、可验证的标准，并使用大语言模型（LLM）评判者对这些标准进行评分，可提供部分评分优化信号：而非二元结果或单一整体评分，每个回答将根据多个任务特定标准进行评分。我们形式化了“基于评分标准的强化学习（RL）”框架：其中策略针对由冻结的LLM评判者生成的结构化、多标准奖励进行优化，该评判者依据策略从未见过的辅助背景信息进行条件判断。我们通过从科学与技术信息办公室（OSTI）衍生的约10万篇科学和技术文档中推导评分标准，并使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型，实现了在保留集评分评估中71.7%的标准化奖励。GRPO调优后的策略在四个未从训练语料库衍生的推理基准测试中也优于基线模型——GSM8K、MATH、GPQA Main和GPQA Diamond。这些结果表明，结构化、文档基础的奖励可提升保留集评分表现，并诱导超越训练环境构建语料库的可迁移推理行为。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more structured and generalizable reasoning in language models, the authors propose rubric-grounded reinforcement learning, where a policy is optimized using a multi-criterion reward scored by a frozen LLM judge based on criteria derived from scientific documents. They train Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on rubrics extracted from 100,000 OSTI documents, achieving 71.7% normalized reward on held-out rubric evaluation. The GRPO-tuned model also outperforms the base model on four reasoning benchmarks—GSM8K, MATH, GPQA Main, and GPQA Diamond—demonstrating transferable reasoning capabilities beyond the training corpus.
为提升大语言模型的结构化与泛化推理能力，作者提出基于评分标准的强化学习框架，利用冻结的LLM裁判对响应按多个任务特定标准打分，从而优化策略。他们从10万份OSTI科技文档中提取评分标准，以Group Relative Policy Optimization训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，在独立评分标准上达到71.7%的归一化奖励。该模型在GSM8K、MATH、GPQA Main和GPQA Diamond四个推理基准上均优于基线模型，表明该方法能促进超出训练语料的推理能力迁移。
The Memory Curse: How Expanded Recall Erodes Cooperative Intent in LLM Agents
Authors: Jiayuan Liu, Tianqin Li, Shiyi Du, Xin Luo, Haoxuan Zeng, Emanuel Tewolde, Tai Sing Lee, Tonghan Wang, Carl Kingsford, Vincent Conitzer
First: 2026-05-08T17:47:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:47:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Context window expansion is often treated as a straightforward capability upgrade for LLMs, but we find it systematically fails in multi-agent social dilemmas. Across 7 LLMs and 4 games over 500 rounds, expanding accessible history degrades cooperation in 18 of 28 model--game settings, a pattern we term the memory curse. We isolate the underlying mechanism through three analyses. First, lexical analysis of 378,000 reasoning traces associates this breakdown with eroding forward-looking intent rather than rising paranoia. We validate this using targeted fine-tuning as a cognitive probe: a LoRA adapter trained exclusively on forward-looking traces mitigates the decay and transfers zero-shot to distinct games. Second, memory sanitization holds prompt length fixed while replacing visible history with synthetic cooperative records, which restores cooperation substantially, proving the trigger is memory content, not length alone. Finally, ablating explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning often reduces the collapse, showing that deliberation paradoxically amplifies the memory curse. Together, these results recast memory as an active determinant of multi-agent behavior: longer recall can either destabilize or support cooperation depending on the reasoning patterns it elicits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：记忆诅咒：扩展记忆如何削弱LLM代理间的合作意图
上下文窗口扩展常被视为LLM能力的直接升级，但我们发现其在多智能体社会困境中系统性失效。在7个LLM和4个游戏中超过500轮的实验中，18个模型-游戏组合中扩展历史记录导致合作下降，我们称之为“记忆诅咒”。我们通过三项分析揭示其内在机制：首先，对37.8万条推理轨迹的词法分析表明，该失效源于前瞻意图的削弱而非 paranoia 增加；我们通过定向微调作为认知探针验证：仅用前瞻轨迹训练的LoRA适配器可缓解衰减并零样本迁移到其他游戏。其次，通过记忆净化（固定提示长度，替换可见历史为合成合作记录）显著恢复合作，证明触发因素是记忆内容而非长度本身。最后，移除显式思维链推理常减少崩溃，表明反思反而加剧记忆诅咒。综合来看，这些结果将记忆重新定位为多智能体行为的主动决定因素：更长的记忆可能既破坏也可能支持合作，取决于其引发的推理模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that expanding LLM context windows often harms cooperation in multi-agent settings, the study investigates how memory length affects agent behavior across 7 models and 4 games over 500 rounds. It finds that longer memory degrades cooperation in 18 of 28 model-game combinations, a phenomenon termed the 'memory curse.' Three analyses reveal that this decline stems from eroded forward-looking intent rather than paranoia, confirmed via fine-tuning with forward-looking traces that mitigates decay. Memory sanitization—replacing history with synthetic cooperative records—restores cooperation, proving content matters more than length. Ablating Chain-of-Thought reasoning often reduces collapse, suggesting deliberation can exacerbate the curse. Thus, memory’s impact is not inherent but context-dependent, shaping cooperation through induced reasoning patterns.
研究发现，扩展LLM上下文窗口常导致多智能体协作能力下降，因此探究了记忆长度对协作行为的影响。在7个模型和4个游戏中进行500轮实验后，发现18个模型-游戏组合中协作能力下降，现象称为“记忆诅咒”。通过分析37.8万条推理轨迹，发现根本原因是前瞻意图的削弱而非猜忌增加。通过仅用前瞻轨迹微调LoRA适配器，可缓解该衰减并泛化至新游戏。替换历史记录为合成协作内容后，协作能力显著恢复，证明是记忆内容而非长度导致问题。移除链式思维推理常减轻崩溃，表明推理反而加剧诅咒。综合来看，记忆主动塑造协作，其效果取决于引发的推理模式。
6D Pose Estimation via Keypoint Heatmap Regression with RGB-D Residual Neural Networks
Authors: Ismail Aljosevic, Amir Masoud Almasi, Ana Parovic, Ashkan Shafiei
First: 2026-05-08T17:47:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:47:02+00:00
Comments: Source code available at: https://github.com/ameermasood/HeatNet
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a modular framework for 6D pose estimation based on keypoint heatmap regression. Our approach combines YOLOv10m for object detection with a ResNet18-based network that predicts 2D heatmaps from RGB images. Keypoints extracted from these heatmaps are used to estimate the 6D object pose via the PnP RANSAC algorithm. We compare different keypoint selection strategies to assess their impact on pose accuracy. Additionally, we extend the baseline by incorporating depth data using a cross-fusion architecture, which enables interaction between RGB and depth features at multiple stages. We further explore general training improvements, such as experimenting with activation functions and learning rate scheduling strategies to improve model performance. Our best RGB-only model achieved a mean ADD-based accuracy of 84.50%, while the RGB-D fusion model reached 92.41% on the LINEMOD dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/ameermasood/HeatNet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于RGB-D残差神经网络的关键点热图回归的6D姿态估计
本文提出了一种基于关键点热图回归的6D姿态估计模块化框架。我们的方法结合YOLOv10m进行目标检测，与一个基于ResNet18的网络，该网络从RGB图像预测2D热图。从这些热图中提取的关键点通过PnP RANSAC算法估计6D物体姿态。我们比较了不同的关键点选择策略，以评估其对姿态精度的影响。此外，我们通过引入跨融合架构扩展了基线模型，使RGB和深度特征在多个阶段相互作用。我们还探索了通用训练改进，如实验激活函数和学习率调度策略以提升模型性能。我们的最佳RGB-only模型在LINEMOD数据集上达到84.50%的平均ADD精度，而RGB-D融合模型达到92.41%。代码可在https://github.com/ameermasood/HeatNet获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 6D object pose estimation in real-world scenarios, this paper introduces a modular framework that leverages keypoint heatmap regression. It combines YOLOv10m for object detection with a ResNet18-based network to predict 2D heatmaps from RGB images, and uses PnP RANSAC to compute 6D poses from extracted keypoints. The method evaluates various keypoint selection strategies and enhances performance by integrating depth data through a cross-fusion architecture that fuses RGB and depth features at multiple stages. Experimental results show that the RGB-only model achieves 84.50% mean ADD accuracy, while the RGB-D fusion model improves to 92.41% on the LINEMOD dataset, demonstrating significant gains from depth integration and training optimizations.
为提升真实场景中机器人应用的6D姿态估计精度，本文提出一种模块化框架，结合YOLOv10m目标检测与ResNet18网络回归RGB图像中的2D关键点热图，再通过PnP RANSAC算法估计6D姿态，并评估不同关键点选取策略对精度的影响。方法进一步通过跨融合架构整合深度数据，在网络多阶段交互RGB与深度特征，并结合激活函数与学习率调度等训练优化手段提升性能。实验表明，在LINEMOD数据集上，RGB-only模型达到84.50%的平均ADD精度，而RGB-D融合模型提升至92.41%。
Scaling Continual Learning to 300+ Tasks with Bi-Level Routing Mixture-of-Experts
Authors: Meng Lou, Yunxiang Fu, Yizhou Yu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-03T12:45:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:45:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Continual learning, especially class-incremental learning (CIL), on the basis of a pre-trained model (PTM) has garnered substantial research interest in recent years. However, how to effectively learn both discriminative and comprehensive feature representations while maintaining stability and plasticity over very long task sequences remains an open problem. We propose CaRE, a scalable {C}ontinual Le{a}rner with efficient Bi-Level {R}outing Mixture-of-{E}xperts (BR-MoE). The core idea of BR-MoE is a bi-level routing mechanism: a router selection stage that dynamically activates relevant task-specific routers, followed by an expert routing phase that dynamically activates and aggregates experts, aiming to inject discriminative and comprehensive representations into every intermediate network layer. On the other hand, we introduce a challenging dataset, OmniBenchmark-1K, for CIL performance evaluation on very long task sequences with hundreds of tasks. Extensive experiments show that CaRE demonstrates leading performance across a variety of datasets and task settings, including commonly used CIL datasets with classical CIL settings (e.g., 5-20 tasks). To the best of our knowledge, CaRE is the first continual learner that scales to very long task sequences (ranging from 100 to over 300 non-overlapping tasks), while outperforming all baselines by a large margin on such task sequences. We hope that this work will inspire further research into continual learning over extremely long task sequences. Code and dataset are publicly released at https://github.com/LMMMEng/CaRE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于双层路由专家混合模型的持续学习扩展至300+任务
近年来，基于预训练模型（PTM）的持续学习，特别是类别增量学习（CIL），已成为研究热点。然而，如何在极长任务序列中同时有效学习判别性与综合性特征表示，并保持稳定性和可塑性，仍是一个开放性问题。我们提出CaRE，一种高效的双层路由专家混合（BR-MoE）持续学习器。BR-MoE的核心思想是双层路由机制：首先通过路由器选择阶段动态激活相关任务特定路由器，随后通过专家路由阶段动态激活并聚合专家，旨在将判别性与综合性表示注入每个中间网络层。同时，我们引入了一个具有挑战性的数据集OmniBenchmark-1K，用于评估在数百任务的极长任务序列上的CIL性能。大量实验表明，CaRE在多种数据集和任务设置下均表现领先，包括常用CIL数据集及其经典设置（如5-20任务）。据我们所知，CaRE是首个可扩展至极长任务序列（100至300+非重叠任务）的持续学习器，并在这些任务序列上显著优于所有基线方法。我们希望本工作能激发对极长任务序列持续学习的进一步研究。代码与数据集已公开于https://github.com/LMMMEng/CaRE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of scaling continual learning to very long task sequences while preserving stability and plasticity, the authors propose CaRE, a bi-level routing Mixture-of-Experts (BR-MoE) architecture that dynamically routes task-specific routers and experts to inject discriminative and comprehensive features into every network layer. Evaluated on the newly introduced OmniBenchmark-1K dataset with up to 300+ non-overlapping tasks, CaRE consistently outperforms all baselines across diverse CIL settings, including standard datasets with 5–20 tasks. Its scalability and performance gains demonstrate its effectiveness in handling extreme task sequences, setting a new benchmark for continual learning.
为解决在极长任务序列上实现持续学习时保持稳定性和可塑性的挑战，作者提出CaRE框架，采用双层路由机制动态激活任务专属路由器与专家模块，将判别性与综合性特征注入网络中间层。在新构建的OmniBenchmark-1K数据集（支持100至300+非重叠任务）上，CaRE在多个持续学习基准上均显著优于现有方法，尤其在超长任务序列中表现突出，展现出卓越的可扩展性与性能稳定性。
CA-SQL: Complexity-Aware Inference Time Reasoning for Text-to-SQL via Exploration and Compute Budget Allocation
Authors: James Petullo, Nianwen Xue
First: 2026-05-08T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While recent advancements in inference-time learning have improved LLM reasoning on Text-to-SQL tasks, current solutions still struggle to perform well on the most challenging tasks in the Bird-Bench (BIRD) benchmark. This is due to inadequate solution space exploration, which is necessary to uncover promising candidate queries that can be further refined to produce the correct output. To address this challenge, we introduce CA-SQL, a novel Text-to-SQL pipeline that utilizes the estimated difficulty of a task to dynamically scale the breadth of the exploration for generating solution candidates. In addition, we use a custom prompt seeding method, based on principles of evolutionary search, to further elicit exploratory behavior from the base LLM and a novel voting method to select the best candidate solution at the end of the search. Experiments demonstrate that our solution achieves a state-of-the-art score of 51.72% on the "challenging" tier of BIRD development set problems, using only GPT-4o-mini, out-performing other in-context learning approaches, even those that leverage larger models. Overall, our method attains a competitive 61.06% execution accuracy and 68.77% Soft F1 score on the BIRD development dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CA-SQL：通过探索与计算预算分配实现面向文本到SQL的复杂度感知推理时间推理
尽管近期推理时学习的进展提升了大语言模型在文本到SQL任务中的推理能力，但当前方案在Bird-Bench（BIRD）基准中最难的任务上仍表现不佳，这归因于探索空间不足，难以发现可进一步优化以生成正确输出的候选查询。为解决此问题，我们提出CA-SQL，一种新颖的文本到SQL流程，利用任务预估难度动态调整生成候选解的探索广度。此外，我们采用基于进化搜索原理的自定义提示播种方法，进一步激发基础大语言模型的探索行为，并引入一种新颖投票机制，在搜索结束时选择最优候选解。实验表明，仅使用GPT-4o-mini，我们的方案在BIRD开发集“困难”层级问题上达到51.72%的最先进得分，超越其他上下文学习方法，包括那些使用更大模型的方法。总体而言，我们的方法在BIRD开发数据集上实现61.06%的执行准确率和68.77%的Soft F1分数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of current inference-time learning methods on the hardest Text-to-SQL tasks in the Bird-Bench benchmark, CA-SQL introduces a complexity-aware exploration strategy that dynamically adjusts the breadth of solution space search based on task difficulty. It employs a custom prompt seeding technique inspired by evolutionary search to encourage diverse candidate query generation and a novel voting mechanism to select the optimal solution. Experiments show that CA-SQL achieves a 51.72% score on the challenging tier of BIRD’s development set using only GPT-4o-mini, surpassing other in-context learning methods, while also achieving 61.06% execution accuracy and 68.77% Soft F1 score on the full BIRD dev set.
为解决当前推理时学习方法在Bird-Bench基准中最难Text-to-SQL任务上的表现不佳问题，作者提出CA-SQL方法，根据任务难度动态调整探索广度以发现更优候选查询。该方法采用受进化搜索启发的提示引导策略引导基础大模型生成多样解，并结合新颖投票机制选择最终答案。实验表明，仅使用GPT-4o-mini模型，CA-SQL在BIRD开发集“挑战”级别上达到51.72%准确率，超越其他上下文学习方法，同时在完整开发集上实现61.06%执行准确率和68.77% Soft F1分数。
Towards Highly-Constrained Human Motion Generation with Retrieval-Guided Diffusion Noise Optimization
Authors: Hanchao Liu, Fang-Lue Zhang, Shining Zhang, Tai-Jiang Mu, Shi-Min Hu
First: 2026-05-08T17:43:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:43:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating human motion that satisfies customized zero-shot goal functions, enabling applications such as controllable character animation and behavior synthesis for virtual agents, is a critical capability. While current approaches handle many unseen constraints, they fail on tasks with very challenging spatiotemporal restrictions, such as severe spatial obstacles or specified numbers of walking steps. To equip motion generators for these highly constrained tasks, we present a retrieval-guided method built on the training-free diffusion noise optimization framework. The key idea is to search within large motion datasets for guidance that can potentially satisfy difficult constraints. We introduce relational task parsing to group target constraints and identify the difficult ones to be handled by retrieved reference. A better initialization for diffusion noise is then obtained via a reward-guided mask that combines random noise with retrieved noise. By optimizing diffusion noise from this improved initialization, we successfully solve highly constrained generation tasks. By leveraging LLM for relational task parsing, the whole framework is further enabled to automatically reason for what to retrieve, improving the intelligence of moving agents under a training-free optimization scheme.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于检索引导扩散噪声优化的高约束人体运动生成
生成满足自定义零样本目标函数的人体运动，以实现可控角色动画和虚拟代理行为合成等应用，是一项关键能力。当前方法虽能处理许多未见过的约束，但在空间障碍严重或指定步数等具有极高时空约束的任务上仍失败。为使运动生成器胜任此类高约束任务，我们提出一种基于无训练扩散噪声优化框架的检索引导方法。核心思想是在大型运动数据集中搜索可能满足困难约束的引导信息。我们引入关系任务解析，将目标约束分组并识别出需由检索参考处理的困难约束。通过结合随机噪声与检索噪声的奖励引导掩码，获得更优的扩散噪声初始化。由此优化扩散噪声，成功解决高约束生成任务。进一步利用大语言模型进行关系任务解析，使整个框架能自动推理应检索内容，提升无训练优化方案下移动代理的智能性。
Summary / 总结
To enable human motion generation under highly constrained, zero-shot conditions such as spatial obstacles or step-count requirements, this work introduces a retrieval-guided diffusion noise optimization method. It leverages large motion datasets to retrieve reference motions that align with difficult constraints, using relational task parsing to identify and prioritize such constraints. A reward-guided mask combines random and retrieved noise to initialize diffusion optimization, leading to improved generation quality. Experiments show the method successfully handles previously unsolvable highly constrained tasks, with LLM-enhanced parsing enabling automatic retrieval reasoning without training.
为实现空间障碍或步数限制等高约束条件下的人体动作生成，该研究提出一种基于检索引导的扩散噪声优化方法。通过从大规模动作数据集中检索参考动作，结合关系任务解析识别困难约束并优先处理，利用奖励引导掩码融合随机与检索噪声以优化扩散初始化，从而成功生成满足复杂零样本目标的动作。框架进一步引入大语言模型自动推理检索内容，提升系统在无训练优化方案下处理高难度任务的能力。
Reinforcement Learning for Exponential Utility: Algorithms and Convergence in Discounted MDPs
Authors: Gugan Thoppe, L. A. Prashanth, Ankur Naskar, Sanjay Bhat
First: 2026-05-08T17:41:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:41:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) for exponential-utility optimization in discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) lacks principled value-based algorithms. We address this gap in the fixed risk-aversion setting. Building on the Bellman-type equation for exponential utility studied in \cite{porteus1975optimality}, we derive two Q-value-style extensions and show that the associated operators are contractions in the $L_\infty$ and sup-log/Thompson metrics, respectively. We characterize their fixed points and prove that the induced greedy stationary policy is optimal for the exponential-utility objective among stationary policies. These structural results lead to two model-free algorithms: a two-timescale Q-learning--style algorithm, for which we establish almost-sure convergence and provide finite-time convergence rates via timescale separation, and a one-timescale algorithm governed by a sublinear power-law operator. Since the latter does not admit a global contraction in standard metrics, we prove its convergence using delicate arguments based on local Lipschitzness, monotonicity, homogeneity, and Dini derivatives, and provide a scalar finite-time analysis that highlights the challenges in obtaining convergence rates in the vector case. Our work provides a foundation for value-based RL under exponential-utility objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：指数效用强化学习：折扣MDP中的算法与收敛性
在折扣马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中，针对指数效用优化的强化学习（RL）缺乏基于价值的原理性算法。本文在固定风险规避设定下填补这一空白。基于Porteus（1975）研究的指数效用Bellman型方程，我们推导出两种Q值风格的扩展，并证明其对应的算子在L∞和sup-log/Thompson度量下分别为压缩映射。我们刻画了其不动点，并证明由此诱导的贪婪平稳策略在所有平稳策略中对指数效用目标是最优的。这些结构结果催生了两种无模型算法：一种是双时间尺度Q学习风格算法，我们建立了其几乎必然收敛性，并通过时间尺度分离给出了有限时间收敛速率；另一种是一时间尺度算法，由次线性幂律算子驱动。由于后者在标准度量下不满足全局压缩性，我们通过局部Lipschitz性、单调性、齐次性和Dini导数的精细论证证明其收敛性，并提供标量有限时间分析，凸显向量情形下获得收敛速率的挑战。本文为指数效用目标下的基于价值的强化学习奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of principled value-based reinforcement learning algorithms for exponential-utility optimization in discounted MDPs, this work derives two Q-value-style operators based on Bellman equations for exponential utility, proving their contraction properties in specific metrics. The fixed points of these operators correspond to optimal stationary policies under exponential utility. Two model-free algorithms are proposed: a two-timescale Q-learning variant with almost-sure convergence and finite-time rates via timescale separation, and a one-timescale algorithm using a sublinear power-law operator, whose convergence is established through local Lipschitzness, monotonicity, homogeneity, and Dini derivatives, with a scalar finite-time analysis revealing challenges in vector-case convergence rates.
为解决折扣马尔可夫决策过程中指数效用优化缺乏基于价值的强化学习算法的问题，本文基于指数效用的贝尔曼方程推导了两种Q值风格算子，并证明其在特定度量下具有压缩性。这些算子的不动点对应于指数效用下的最优平稳策略。提出两种无模型算法：一种是双时间尺度的Q学习变体，具有几乎必然收敛性及通过时间尺度分离获得的有限时间收敛速率；另一种是一时间尺度算法，采用次线性幂律算子，其收敛性通过局部利普希茨性、单调性、齐次性和Dini导数等精细论证得出，同时提供标量有限时间分析，揭示向量情形下获得收敛速率的困难。
Goal-Conditioned Decision Transformer for Multi-Goal Offline Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Paweł Gajewski, Dominik Żurek, Marcin Pietroń, Kamil Faber
First: 2024-10-08T20:35:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:41:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) in robotics faces significant hurdles regarding sample efficiency and generalization across varying goals. While Offline RL mitigates the need for costly online interactions, its integration with goal-conditioned policies and transformer-based architectures remains underexplored. We introduce a Goal-Conditioned Decision Transformer adapted for offline multi-goal robotics. By explicitly incorporating goal states into the sequence modeling framework, our approach efficiently solves varying tasks using only pre-collected data. We validate this method on a newly released offline dataset for the Franka Emika Panda platform. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art online baselines in complex tasks and maintains robustness in sparse-reward settings, even with limited expert demonstrations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多目标离线强化学习的目标条件决策变换器
机器人领域的强化学习面临样本效率和跨不同目标泛化能力的重大挑战。虽然离线强化学习减少了昂贵的在线交互需求，但其与目标条件策略及基于变换器的架构的结合仍鲜有探索。我们提出了一种面向离线多目标机器人学习的目标条件决策变换器。通过将目标状态显式纳入序列建模框架，我们的方法仅使用预收集的数据即可高效解决不同任务。我们在Franka Emika Panda平台新发布的离线数据集上验证了该方法。实验结果表明，我们的方法在复杂任务中优于当前最先进的在线基线，并在稀疏奖励设置下保持鲁棒性，即使专家演示有限。

Motivated by the need for sample-efficient and generalizable reinforcement learning in robotics, this paper proposes a Goal-Conditioned Decision Transformer tailored for offline multi-goal learning. The method integrates goal states directly into the transformer’s sequence modeling to enable task-agnostic policy learning from pre-collected data. Evaluated on a new offline dataset for the Franka Emika Panda robot, the approach achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art online baselines in complex tasks and remains robust under sparse reward conditions, even with limited expert demonstrations.
为解决机器人领域强化学习中样本效率低和跨目标泛化能力差的问题，本文提出一种面向离线多目标学习的条件目标决策变换器。该方法将目标状态显式融入序列建模框架，使模型能仅通过预收集数据高效学习不同任务策略。在Franka Emika Panda机器人新发布的离线数据集上实验表明，该方法在复杂任务中优于当前最优在线基线，并在稀疏奖励环境下仍保持良好鲁棒性，即使专家演示样本有限。
MoCoTalk: Multi-Conditional Diffusion with Adaptive Router for Controllable Talking Head Generation
Authors: Xinyan Ye, Jiankang Deng, Abbas Edalat
First: 2026-05-08T17:40:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:40:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Talking-head generation requires joint modeling of identity, head pose, facial expression, and mouth dynamics. Existing methods typically address only a subset of these factors, and rely on fixed-weight or heuristic fusion when multiple conditions are involved. We present MoCoTalk, a multi-conditional video diffusion framework that unifies four complementary control signals: a reference image, facial keypoints, 3DMM-rendered shading meshes, and the corresponding speech audio. To resolve destructive interference among heterogeneous conditions, we introduce an Adaptive Multi-Condition Router that computes channel-wise, timestep-aware gating over the four condition streams, allowing the fusion strategy to vary with both feature subspace and noise level. To better capture speech-related facial dynamics, we design a Mouth-Augmented Shading Mesh, a 3DMM-based representation that decouples head motion, mouth motion, expression, and lighting. This design provides a temporally consistent geometric prior and allows flexible recombination of these attributes at inference. We further introduce a lip consistency loss to tighten audio-visual alignment. Extensive experiments show that MoCoTalk achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of structural, motion, and perceptual metrics, while offering attribute-level controllability that single-condition methods do not provide.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoCoTalk：用于可控说话头生成的多条件扩散与自适应路由
说话头生成需要联合建模身份、头部姿态、面部表情和嘴部动态。现有方法通常仅处理这些因素中的部分，并在涉及多个条件时依赖固定权重或启发式融合。我们提出MoCoTalk，一种多条件视频扩散框架，统一了四种互补控制信号：参考图像、面部关键点、3DMM渲染的着色网格和对应的语音音频。为解决异质条件间的破坏性干扰，我们引入自适应多条件路由器，对四个条件流进行通道级、时间步感知的门控，使融合策略随特征子空间和噪声水平动态变化。为更好地捕捉语音相关的面部动态，我们设计了口部增强着色网格，一种基于3DMM的表示方法，将头部运动、嘴部运动、表情和光照解耦。该设计提供时序一致的几何先验，并允许在推理时灵活重组这些属性。我们进一步引入唇部一致性损失以加强视听对齐。大量实验表明，MoCoTalk在结构、运动和感知指标上均达到最先进水平，同时提供单条件方法无法实现的属性级可控性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to jointly model identity, pose, expression, and mouth dynamics in talking-head generation, MoCoTalk introduces a multi-conditional diffusion framework that integrates four control signals: reference image, facial keypoints, 3DMM shading meshes, and speech audio. It employs an Adaptive Multi-Condition Router to dynamically gate and fuse these heterogeneous inputs based on feature subspace and noise level, mitigating destructive interference. Additionally, it uses a Mouth-Augmented Shading Mesh to decouple facial attributes for temporally consistent geometric representation and introduces a lip consistency loss to align audio and visual signals. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across structural, motion, and perceptual metrics, with superior attribute-level controllability compared to single-condition methods.
为实现身份、姿态、表情与口型动态的联合建模，MoCoTalk提出一种多条件扩散框架，整合参考图像、面部关键点、3DMM着色网格与语音音频四类控制信号。通过自适应多条件路由器，按特征子空间与噪声水平动态门控融合异构输入，缓解干扰。同时设计口型增强着色网格，解耦面部属性以实现时间一致的几何先验与灵活重组，并引入唇部一致性损失强化音视频对齐。实验表明其在结构、运动与感知指标上达到最先进水平，且具备单条件方法所不具备的属性级可控性。
Accurate and Efficient Statistical Testing for Word Semantic Breadth
Authors: Yo Ehara
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-08T17:38:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:38:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Measuring the breadth of a word's meaning, or its spread across contexts, has become feasible with contextualized token embeddings. A word type can be represented as a cloud of token vectors, with dispersion-based statistics serving as proxies for contextual diversity (Nagata and Tanaka-Ishii, ACL2025). These measurements are useful for deciding appropriate sense distinctions when constructing thesauri and domain-specific dictionaries. However, when comparing the breadth of two word types, naive hypothesis testing on dispersion can be misleading: differences in semantic direction can masquerade as dispersion differences, inflating Type-I error and yielding "statistically significant" outcomes even when there is no true breadth difference. This is problematic because significance testing should distinguish genuine effects from incidental fluctuations in small-difference regimes. We propose a Householder-aligned permutation test to isolate dispersion differences from directional differences. Our method applies a single Householder reflection to align the mean directions of the two word types and then performs a permutation test on the aligned token clouds, yielding calibrated, non-parametric p-values. For practicality, we introduce a GPU-oriented implementation that batches permutations and linear algebra operations. Empirically, our alignment reduced Type-I error by 32.5% while preserving sensitivity to genuine breadth differences, and achieved a 23x speedup over the CPU baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于词义广度的准确高效统计检验
借助上下文化词元嵌入，测量词义的广度（即其在不同语境中的分布）已成为可能。一个词型可表示为词元向量的云，基于离散度的统计量可作为上下文多样性的代理（Nagata 和 Tanaka-Ishii，ACL2025）。这些度量在构建同义词词典和领域词典时，有助于确定合适的词义区分。然而，当比较两个词型的广度时，对离散度进行朴素假设检验可能产生误导：语义方向的差异可能伪装成离散度差异，导致I类错误率上升，即使实际不存在广度差异时也会得出“统计显著”的结果。这在小差异情形下尤为成问题，因为显著性检验应区分真实效应与偶然波动。我们提出一种Householder对齐的置换检验方法，以分离离散度差异与方向差异。该方法通过单次Householder反射对齐两个词型的平均方向，然后在对齐后的词元云上进行置换检验，生成校准后的非参数p值。为提高实用性，我们引入面向GPU的实现，批量处理置换与线性代数运算。实证表明，该对齐方法将I类错误率降低了32.5%，同时保持对真实广度差异的敏感性，并比CPU基线快23倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately compare semantic breadth between word types using contextualized embeddings, the paper introduces a Householder-aligned permutation test to distinguish true dispersion differences from misleading directional effects that inflate Type-I error. The method aligns the mean directions of two word type token clouds via a single Householder reflection before performing a permutation test on the aligned vectors, yielding calibrated p-values. Experimentally, this alignment reduced false positives by 32.5% while maintaining sensitivity to real breadth differences, and the GPU-optimized implementation delivered a 23x speedup over CPU-based baselines.
为准确比较词义广度，该文提出一种Householder对齐置换检验方法，通过单次Householder反射对齐两个词云的均值方向，从而分离分散度与方向性差异，避免因方向偏差导致的一类错误膨胀。实验表明，该对齐方法将一类错误降低32.5%，同时保持对真实广度差异的敏感性，且GPU优化实现比CPU基线快23倍。
Synergistic Benefits of Joint Molecule Generation and Property Prediction
Authors: Adam Izdebski, Jan Olszewski, Pankhil Gawade, Krzysztof Koras, Serra Korkmaz, Valentin Rauscher, Jakub M. Tomczak, Ewa Szczurek
Venue: Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026
First: 2025-04-23T09:36:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:37:53+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling the joint distribution of data samples and their properties allows to construct a single model for both data generation and property prediction, with synergistic benefits reaching beyond purely generative or predictive models. However, training joint models presents daunting architectural and optimization challenges. Here, we propose Hyformer, a transformer-based joint model that successfully blends the generative and predictive functionalities, using an alternating attention mechanism and a joint pre-training scheme. We show that Hyformer is simultaneously optimized for molecule generation and property prediction, while exhibiting synergistic benefits in conditional sampling, out-of-distribution property prediction and representation learning. Finally, we demonstrate the benefits of joint learning in a drug design use case of discovering novel antimicrobial~peptides.
