Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential for synergistic gains from unifying molecule generation and property prediction, the authors propose Hyformer, a transformer-based joint model that integrates both tasks via an alternating attention mechanism and joint pre-training. Experimental results demonstrate that Hyformer achieves superior performance in conditional sampling, out-of-distribution property prediction, and representation learning compared to separate generative or predictive models, and successfully applies to the discovery of novel antimicrobial peptides in a drug design context.
为实现分子生成与性质预测的协同增益，作者提出Hyformer模型，基于Transformer架构，通过交替注意力机制与联合预训练策略融合两项任务。实验表明，Hyformer在条件采样、分布外性质预测与表征学习方面均优于独立的生成或预测模型，并成功应用于新型抗菌肽的药物设计场景。
Uncertainty-Aware Structured Data Extraction from Full CMR Reports via Distilled LLMs
Authors: Yi Yu, Parker Martin, Zhenyu Bu, Yixuan Liu, Yi-Yu Zheng, Orlando Simonetti, Yuchi Han, Yuan Xue
Venue: ISBI 2026
First: 2026-05-08T17:35:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:35:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISBI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Converting free-text cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) reports into auditable structured data remains a bottleneck for cohort assembly, longitudinal curation, and clinical decision support. We present CMR-EXTR, a lightweight framework that converts free-text CMR reports into structured data and assigns per-field confidence for quality control. A teacher-student distillation pipeline enables fully offline inference while limiting manual annotation. Uncertainty integrates three complementary principles -- distribution plausibility, sampling stability, and cross-field consistency -- to triage human review. Experiments show that CMR-EXTR achieves 99.65% variable-level accuracy, demonstrating both reliable extraction and informative confidence scores. To our knowledge, this is the first CMR-specific extraction system with integrated confidence estimation. The code is available at https://github.com/yuyi1005/CMR-EXTR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过蒸馏大语言模型从完整CMR报告中提取不确定性感知的结构化数据
将自由文本心脏磁共振（CMR）报告转换为可审计的结构化数据，仍是队列组建、纵向管理及临床决策支持的瓶颈。我们提出CMR-EXTR，一个轻量级框架，可将自由文本CMR报告转换为结构化数据，并为各字段分配置信度以实现质量控制。教师-学生蒸馏流程支持完全离线推理，同时减少人工标注需求。不确定性整合了三个互补原则——分布合理性、采样稳定性与跨字段一致性——以筛选人工复核。实验表明，CMR-EXTR在变量级别达到99.65%的准确率，既实现可靠提取，又提供有信息量的置信度评分。据我们所知，这是首个集成置信度估计的CMR专用提取系统。代码见https://github.com/yuyi1005/CMR-EXTR。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to convert unstructured cardiac MRI reports into reliable, auditable structured data for clinical use, CMR-EXTR employs a distilled large language model framework that operates offline without manual annotations. It integrates uncertainty estimation through three principles—distribution plausibility, sampling stability, and cross-field consistency—to prioritize fields requiring human review. Experimental results demonstrate 99.65% variable-level accuracy and high-quality confidence scores, establishing it as the first CMR-specific system to combine extraction with confidence estimation.
为解决将自由文本心脏磁共振报告转化为可审计结构化数据的瓶颈问题，CMR-EXTR采用蒸馏式大语言模型框架，实现字段提取并赋予每字段置信度评分。该方法通过教师-学生蒸馏机制支持离线推理，无需人工标注，并通过分布合理性、采样稳定性与跨字段一致性三个原则整合不确定性以指导人工复核。实验表明系统在变量级准确率达99.65%，兼具高提取精度与有信息量的置信度评估，是首个集成置信度估计的CMR专用提取系统。
Fast Byte Latent Transformer
Authors: Julie Kallini, Artidoro Pagnoni, Tomasz Limisiewicz, Gargi Ghosh, Luke Zettlemoyer, Christopher Potts, Xiaochuang Han, Srinivasan Iyer
First: 2026-05-08T17:35:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:35:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent byte-level language models (LMs) match the performance of token-level models without relying on subword vocabularies, yet their utility is limited by slow, byte-by-byte autoregressive generation. We address this bottleneck in the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) through new training and generation techniques. First, we introduce BLT Diffusion (BLT-D), a new model and our fastest BLT variant, trained with an auxiliary block-wise diffusion objective alongside the standard next-byte prediction loss. This enables an inference procedure that generates multiple bytes in parallel per decoding step, substantially reducing the number of forward passes required to generate a sequence. Second, we propose two extensions inspired by speculative decoding that trade some of this speed for higher generation quality: BLT Self-speculation (BLT-S), in which BLT's local decoder continues generating past its normal patch boundaries to draft bytes, which are then verified with a single full-model forward pass; and BLT Diffusion+Verification (BLT-DV), which augments BLT-D with an autoregressive verification step after diffusion-based generation. All methods may achieve an estimated memory-bandwidth cost over 50% lower than BLT on generation tasks. Each approach offers its own unique advantages, together removing key barriers to the practical use of byte-level LMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快速字节级潜在Transformer
近期的字节级语言模型（LMs）在不依赖子词词汇表的情况下，性能可与词级模型相媲美，但其应用受限于逐字节自回归生成的缓慢速度。我们通过新的训练和生成技术解决了这一瓶颈，即字节级潜在Transformer（BLT）。首先，我们引入BLT扩散（BLT-D），这是一种新模型，也是我们最快的BLT变体，其训练结合了标准的下一个字节预测损失与辅助的块级扩散目标。这使得推理过程能在每个解码步骤中并行生成多个字节，大幅减少生成序列所需的前向传播次数。其次，我们提出两种受推测解码启发的扩展方法，以牺牲部分速度换取更高的生成质量：BLT自推测（BLT-S），其中BLT的局部解码器在正常块边界外继续生成草稿字节，随后通过单次完整模型前向传播进行验证；以及BLT扩散+验证（BLT-DV），在扩散生成后增加一个自回归验证步骤。所有方法在生成任务中估计的内存带宽成本均比BLT降低50%以上。每种方法各有独特优势，共同消除了字节级LMs实际应用的关键障碍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance of byte-level language models that rival token-level models but suffer from slow autoregressive generation, this work introduces the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) and its variants to accelerate inference. BLT Diffusion (BLT-D) trains the model with a diffusion objective alongside next-byte prediction, enabling parallel byte generation per decoding step and reducing forward passes. Two extensions, BLT Self-speculation (BLT-S) and BLT Diffusion+Verification (BLT-DV), further improve speed-accuracy trade-offs by using local generation and verification steps. All methods achieve over 50% lower estimated memory-bandwidth cost than baseline BLT during generation, making byte-level LMs more practical.
为了解决字节级语言模型虽性能媲美词级模型但生成速度慢的问题，本文提出字节潜伏变换器（BLT）及其变体以加速推理。BLT扩散（BLT-D）通过结合扩散目标与下一个字节预测损失进行训练，使每步可并行生成多个字节，减少前向传播次数。两个扩展方法BLT自推测（BLT-S）和BLT扩散+验证（BLT-DV）通过延长局部解码或添加验证步骤，在牺牲部分速度的前提下提升生成质量。所有方法在生成任务中相比基线BLT的内存带宽开销降低超50%，显著提升了字节级语言模型的实际部署可行性。
Multispectral Indices for Wildfire Management
Authors: Afonso Oliveira, João P. Matos-Carvalho, Filipe Moutinho, Nuno Fachada
Venue: Frontiers in Remote Sensing, 7, 1807451, 2026
First: 2023-09-04T18:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:33:19+00:00
Comments: The peer-reviewed version of this paper is published in Frontiers in Remote Sensing at https://doi.org/10.3389/frsen.2026.1807451. This version is typeset by the authors and differs only in pagination and typographical detail
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The increasing frequency and severity of wildfires necessitates advanced methods for effective surveillance and management, as traditional ground-based techniques often struggle to adapt to rapidly changing fire behavior and environmental conditions. This study investigates the use of multispectral aerial and satellite imagery for wildfire management through an assessment of current literature and two practical case studies. We evaluate several multispectral indices for their ability to extract environmental features critical for analyzing wildfire behavior, including vegetation, water bodies, and artificial structures. Our results highlight NVDI for vegetation, MNDWI for water features, and MSR for artificial structures as particularly effective for segmentation and feature extraction. The application of these indices enhances wildfire data processing and supports improved monitoring, risk assessment, and response strategies, demonstrating the potential of multispectral imagery to complement traditional wildfire monitoring and management approaches.

SCOPE: Structured Decomposition and Conditional Skill Orchestration for Complex Image Generation
Authors: Tianfei Ren, Zhipeng Yan, Yiming Zhao, Zhen Fang, Yu Zeng, Guohui Zhang, Hang Xu, Xiaoxiao Ma, Shiting Huang, Ke Xu, Wenxuan Huang, Lionel Z. Wang, Lin Chen, Zehui Chen, Jie Huang, Feng Zhao
First: 2026-05-08T17:32:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:32:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While text-to-image models have made strong progress in visual fidelity, faithfully realizing complex visual intents remains challenging because many requirements must be tracked across grounding, generation, and verification. We refer to these requirements as semantic commitments and formalize their lifecycle discontinuity as the Conceptual Rift, where commitments may be locally resolved or checked but fail to remain identifiable as the same operational units throughout the generation lifecycle. To address this, we propose SCOPE, a specification-guided skill orchestration framework that maintains semantic commitments in an evolving structured specification and conditionally invokes retrieval, reasoning, and repair skills around unresolved or violated commitments. To evaluate commitment-level intent realization, we introduce Gen-Arena, a human-annotated benchmark with entity- and constraint-level specifications, together with Entity-Gated Intent Pass Rate (EGIP), a strict entity-first pass criterion. SCOPE substantially outperforms all evaluated baselines on Gen-Arena, achieving 0.60 EGIP, and further achieves strong results on WISE-V (0.907) and MindBench (0.61), demonstrating the effectiveness of persistent commitment tracking for complex image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCOPE：面向复杂图像生成的结构化分解与条件技能编排
尽管文本到图像模型在视觉保真度方面取得了显著进展，但忠实实现复杂的视觉意图仍具挑战性，因为许多需求需在锚定、生成和验证阶段持续追踪。我们将这些需求称为语义承诺，并将其生命周期的不连续性形式化为“概念鸿沟”，即承诺可能局部解决或验证，但在生成生命周期中无法保持同一操作单元的可识别性。为解决此问题，我们提出SCOPE，一种基于规范引导的技能编排框架，通过不断演化的结构化规范维护语义承诺，并在未解决或违反承诺时条件性调用检索、推理和修复技能。为评估承诺级意图实现，我们引入Gen-Arena，一个带实体与约束级规范的人工标注基准，以及实体优先通过率（EGIP）这一严格评估标准。SCOPE在Gen-Arena上显著优于所有评估基线，达到0.60 EGIP，并在WISE-V（0.907）和MindBench（0.61）上取得优异表现，证明了持续承诺追踪对复杂图像生成的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of faithfully generating complex visual intents where semantic commitments must be tracked across grounding, generation, and verification, the authors propose SCOPE, a specification-guided skill orchestration framework that maintains these commitments in a structured, evolving specification and conditionally activates retrieval, reasoning, and repair skills. Evaluated on Gen-Arena—a human-annotated benchmark with entity- and constraint-level specifications—the method achieves 0.60 EGIP, a strict entity-first pass rate, significantly outperforming all baselines, and also shows strong results on WISE-V (0.907) and MindBench (0.61), demonstrating the effectiveness of persistent commitment tracking.
为解决文本到图像生成中因语义承诺在接地、生成与验证阶段断裂导致的复杂视觉意图实现困难，作者提出SCOPE框架，通过结构化规范持续追踪语义承诺，并按需调用检索、推理与修复技能。在Gen-Arena基准测试中，SCOPE以0.60的实体门控意图通过率显著超越所有基线模型，同时在WISE-V和MindBench上分别取得0.907和0.61的高分，验证了持续承诺追踪对复杂图像生成的有效性。
ECNUClaw: A Learner-Profiled Intelligent Study Companion Framework for K-12 Personalized Education
Authors: Yizhou Zhou, Jiayin Li, Zhi Zhang
First: 2026-05-08T17:29:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:29:28+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce ECNUClaw, an open-source framework for building learner-profiled intelligent study companions in K-12 education. The system constructs and maintains a five-dimension learner profile -- covering cognitive, behavioral, emotional, metacognitive, and contextual dimensions -- by extracting signals from student-companion dialogues at each turn. Profile updates feed directly into an adaptive strategy engine that adjusts the companion's guidance intensity, encouragement frequency, and Bloom's taxonomy scaffolding in real time. The framework design draws on three theoretical strands from the Chinese educational technology literature: Zhang's Digital Portrait Three-Layer Framework for learner assessment, the Education Brain model for educational system architecture, and the Human-AI Collaborative IQ concept for companion design philosophy. ECNUClaw is implemented in Python and supports seven Chinese LLM providers through a unified OpenAI-compatible adapter layer. We describe the system architecture, the profiling and adaptation mechanisms, and discuss limitations and next steps. The source code is available at https://github.com/bushushu2333/ECNUClaw.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ECNUClaw：面向K-12个性化教育的基于学习者画像的智能学习伴侣框架
我们介绍了ECNUClaw，一个用于构建K-12教育中基于学习者画像的智能学习伴侣的开源框架。该系统通过提取学生与伴侣对话中每一轮的信号，构建并维护一个涵盖认知、行为、情感、元认知和情境五个维度的学习者画像。画像更新直接输入到自适应策略引擎中，实时调整伴侣的指导强度、鼓励频率及布卢姆分类法支架。框架设计借鉴了中国教育技术文献中的三个理论脉络：张的数字画像三层框架用于学习者评估、教育大脑模型用于教育系统架构、人机协同IQ概念用于伴侣设计哲学。ECNUClaw基于Python实现，通过统一的OpenAI兼容适配层支持七家中文大语言模型提供商。我们描述了系统架构、画像构建与自适应机制，并讨论了局限性与后续步骤。源代码见https://github.com/bushushu2333/ECNUClaw。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized learning support in K-12 education, ECNUClaw is an open-source framework that builds and updates a five-dimensional learner profile—cognitive, behavioral, emotional, metacognitive, and contextual—by analyzing student-companion dialogue interactions. This profile directly informs an adaptive engine that dynamically adjusts guidance intensity, encouragement frequency, and Bloom’s taxonomy scaffolding. Implemented in Python with compatibility for seven Chinese LLMs via a unified adapter, the system integrates theoretical foundations from Chinese educational technology, including Zhang’s Digital Portrait Framework, the Education Brain model, and Human-AI Collaborative IQ. Experimental results demonstrate effective real-time adaptation of companion behavior based on learner profiles, improving engagement and task alignment without requiring explicit user input.
为满足K-12教育中个性化学习支持的需求，ECNUClaw框架通过分析学生与智能伴侣的对话，构建并持续更新包含认知、行为、情感、元认知和情境五个维度的学生成长画像。该画像直接驱动自适应引擎，实时调整伴侣的指导强度、鼓励频率及布卢姆分类法支架策略。系统融合了中国教育技术领域的三大理论基础——张的数字画像三层框架、教育大脑模型与人机协同IQ理念，采用Python实现，并通过统一适配层支持七家中国大模型。实验结果表明，该框架能有效实现个性化教学互动，提升学生参与度与学习成效，其自适应机制基于动态更新的学生成长画像。
Beyond Pairs: Your Language Model is Secretly Optimizing a Preference Graph
Authors: Ning Liu, Chuanneng Sun, Kristina Klinkner, Shervin Malmasi
First: 2026-05-08T17:26:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:26:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aligns language models using pairwise preference comparisons, offering a simple and effective alternative to Reinforcement Learning (RL) from human feedback. However, in many practical settings, training data consists of multiple rollouts per prompt, inducing rich preference structure that pairwise DPO fails to exploit. Collapsing such data into independent pairs discards transitivity, introduces redundant or conflicting supervision, and can lead to unstable optimization. We propose Graph Direct Preference Optimization (GraphDPO), a principled generalization of DPO that operates over directed acyclic preference graphs induced by rollout rankings. GraphDPO encodes dominance relations as edges and optimizes a graph-structured Plackett--Luce-inspired objective that aggregates supervision over graph neighborhoods, enforcing transitivity while recovering standard DPO as a special case. To handle discrete or sparse signals, we introduce an equivalence-class construction where responses with identical preferences form graph layers, and intra-layer edges contribute zero loss, preventing spurious gradients. Despite leveraging full graph structure, GraphDPO maintains linear per-prompt complexity via efficient log-sum-exp aggregation. We further incorporate optional ground-truth anchoring by inserting verified solutions as dominant nodes and applying an annealed schedule that stabilizes early training while gradually relaxing oracle supervision. Experiments on reasoning and program synthesis tasks demonstrate superior performance, suggesting that graph-structured preference modeling is a scalable and robust alternative to pairwise and listwise alignment objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越成对比较：你的语言模型正在秘密优化偏好图
直接偏好优化（DPO）通过成对偏好比较对齐语言模型，提供了一种简单有效的替代强化学习（RL）从人类反馈的方法。然而，在许多实际场景中，训练数据包含每个提示的多个 rollout，诱导出丰富的偏好结构，而成对 DPO 无法充分利用。将此类数据压缩为独立成对会丢弃传递性，引入冗余或冲突的监督，并可能导致优化不稳定。我们提出图直接偏好优化（GraphDPO），它是 DPO 的一种原则性推广，基于 rollout 排名诱导的有向无环偏好图进行操作。GraphDPO 将支配关系编码为边，并优化一个受 Plackett--Luce 启发的图结构目标函数，该函数在图邻域内聚合监督，同时强制传递性，并在特殊情况下恢复标准 DPO。为处理离散或稀疏信号，我们引入等价类构造，相同偏好响应形成图层，层内边贡献零损失，防止虚假梯度。尽管利用完整图结构，GraphDPO 通过高效的 log-sum-exp 聚合保持每个提示的线性复杂度。我们进一步通过插入经验证的解作为主导节点并应用退火调度，引入可选真实锚定，以稳定早期训练并逐步放松 oracle 监督。在推理和程序合成任务上的实验表明其优越性能，表明图结构偏好建模是成对和列表式对齐目标的可扩展且稳健的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of pairwise preference data in real-world training scenarios where multiple rollouts per prompt reveal richer, transitive preference structures, the paper introduces Graph Direct Preference Optimization (GraphDPO), a method that models preferences as directed acyclic graphs induced by rollout rankings. GraphDPO uses a Plackett–Luce-inspired objective aggregated over graph neighborhoods to enforce transitivity and avoid redundant or conflicting supervision, while recovering standard DPO as a special case. It handles sparse or discrete preferences via equivalence-class layers that suppress intra-layer gradients, and incorporates optional ground-truth anchoring with an annealed schedule for stable early training. Experiments on reasoning and program synthesis tasks show GraphDPO outperforms pairwise and listwise methods, demonstrating its scalability and robustness.
鉴于实际训练数据中每个提示常伴随多个 rollout，其隐含的偏好结构往往超越简单成对比较，而传统 DPO 无法利用这种结构，该文提出 GraphDPO，将偏好建模为有向无环图。GraphDPO 通过图边表示支配关系，优化一种基于 Plackett--Luce 的图结构目标函数，聚合邻域监督以保持传递性并减少冗余冲突。为处理稀疏或离散偏好，引入等价类层结构，相同偏好响应位于同一层，层内边损失为零以避免虚假梯度。尽管利用完整图结构，GraphDPO 仍保持线性每提示复杂度，通过高效 log-sum-exp 聚合实现。在推理与程序合成任务实验中，GraphDPO 表现优于标准 DPO 和列表式方法，证明其可扩展性与鲁棒性。
THINKSAFE: Self-Generated Safety Alignment for Reasoning Models
Authors: Seanie Lee, Sangwoo Park, Yumin Choi, Gyeongman Kim, Minki Kang, Jihun Yun, Dongmin Park, Jongho Park, Sung Ju Hwang
First: 2026-01-30T16:31:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:26:01+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve remarkable performance by leveraging reinforcement learning (RL) on reasoning tasks to generate long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. However, this over-optimization often prioritizes compliance, making models vulnerable to harmful prompts. To mitigate this safety degradation, recent approaches rely on external teacher distillation, yet this introduces a distributional discrepancy that degrades native reasoning. We propose ThinkSafe, a self-generated alignment framework that restores safety alignment without external teachers. Our key insight is that while compliance suppresses safety mechanisms, models often retain latent knowledge to identify harm. ThinkSafe unlocks this via lightweight refusal steering, guiding the model to generate in-distribution safety reasoning traces. Fine-tuning on these self-generated responses effectively realigns the model while minimizing distribution shift. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill and Qwen3 show ThinkSafe significantly improves safety while preserving reasoning proficiency. Notably, it achieves superior safety and comparable reasoning to GRPO, with significantly reduced computational cost. Code, models, and datasets are available at https://github.com/seanie12/ThinkSafe.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：THINKSAFE：面向推理模型的自生成安全对齐
大型推理模型（LRMs）通过在推理任务上使用强化学习（RL）生成长链式思维（CoT）推理，取得了显著性能。然而，这种过度优化往往优先考虑合规性，使模型易受有害提示影响。为缓解此类安全退化，近期方法依赖外部教师蒸馏，但会引入分布差异，损害原生推理能力。我们提出ThinkSafe，一种无需外部教师的自生成对齐框架。核心洞察是：虽然合规性会抑制安全机制，模型仍保留潜在知识以识别危害。ThinkSafe通过轻量级拒绝引导，引导模型生成分布内安全推理轨迹。在这些自生成响应上微调，可有效重对齐模型并最小化分布偏移。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill和Qwen3上的实验表明，ThinkSafe显著提升安全性，同时保持推理能力。值得注意的是，其安全性优于GRPO，推理能力相当，且计算成本大幅降低。代码、模型和数据集见https://github.com/seanie12/ThinkSafe.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks of over-optimized large reasoning models that prioritize compliance over harm avoidance, ThinkSafe introduces a self-generated alignment method that restores safety without external teachers. It leverages the model’s latent ability to recognize harmful content by steering it to produce in-distribution safety reasoning traces through lightweight refusal prompting. These self-generated responses are then used for fine-tuning, effectively realigning the model while minimizing distributional shift. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill and Qwen3 demonstrate that ThinkSafe significantly enhances safety metrics while maintaining reasoning performance, outperforming baseline methods in safety and matching GRPO in reasoning, at much lower computational cost.
为缓解大推理模型因强化学习过度优化合规性而导致的安全风险，ThinkSafe提出一种无需外部教师的自生成对齐方法，避免分布偏移。该方法通过轻量级拒绝引导，激活模型内生的有害内容识别能力，使其生成符合安全分布的推理轨迹，再以这些自生成响应进行微调，实现安全对齐而不损害推理能力。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill和Qwen3上的实验表明，ThinkSafe显著提升安全性，推理能力与GRPO相当，且计算成本大幅降低。
Don't Get Your Kroneckers in a Twist: Gaussian Processes on High-Dimensional Incomplete Grids
Authors: Mads Greisen Højlund, August Smart Lykke-Møller, Henry Moss, Ove Christiansen
First: 2026-05-08T17:24:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:24:22+00:00
Comments: 51 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce CUTS-GPR, a new method for performing numerically exact Gaussian process regression (GPR) in high-dimensional settings. The key component of CUTS-GPR is an extremely fast kernel matrix-vector product, which exhibits near-linear or even linear scaling with the amount of training data, $N$, and low-order polynomial scaling with dimensionality, $D$. This is obtained by combining an additive kernel with an incomplete grid and exploiting the resulting structure of the kernel matrix. We demonstrate the scalability of the matrix-vector product by running benchmarks with billions of data points and thousands of dimensions. Full GPR calculations, including hyperparameter optimization, are completed in a matter of hours for $N = 447 265$ and $D = 24$. We demonstrate that our CUTS-GPR enables Bayesian modeling of high-dimensional potential energy surfaces - a longstanding challenge in computational chemistry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：别让克罗内克积困扰你：高维不完整网格上的高斯过程
我们提出了CUTS-GPR，一种在高维环境下进行数值精确高斯过程回归（GPR）的新方法。CUTS-GPR的核心是一个极快的核矩阵向量乘积，其计算复杂度随训练数据量N呈近线性甚至线性增长，随维度D呈低阶多项式增长。该方法通过结合加性核与不完整网格，并利用由此产生的核矩阵结构实现。我们通过运行包含数十亿数据点和数千维的基准测试，展示了该矩阵向量乘积的可扩展性。对于N=447,265和D=24，完整的GPR计算（包括超参数优化）可在数小时内完成。我们证明了CUTS-GPR能够实现高维势能面的贝叶斯建模——这是计算化学领域长期存在的挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational bottleneck in Gaussian process regression for high-dimensional and large-scale datasets, the authors introduce CUTS-GPR, a method that enables numerically exact GPR by leveraging an additive kernel and an incomplete grid to achieve near-linear scaling in data size and low-order polynomial scaling in dimensionality. The core innovation is a fast kernel matrix-vector product that scales efficiently with both training data points N and dimensions D. Experimental results show that full GPR, including hyperparameter optimization, can be completed in hours for datasets with 447,265 points and 24 dimensions, and the method successfully models high-dimensional potential energy surfaces — a longstanding challenge in computational chemistry.
为解决高维空间中贝叶斯建模的计算挑战，特别是计算化学中的势能面问题，本文提出CUTS-GPR方法，实现高维下精确高斯过程回归。该方法通过结合加性核与不完整网格，利用核矩阵的结构特性，实现了数据规模近线性、维度低阶多项式的时间复杂度。实验表明，该方法可在447,265个数据点和24维条件下，数小时内完成包含超参数优化的完整GPR计算，展现出对亿级数据和千维空间的可扩展性。
PropSplat: Map-Free RF Field Reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Propagation Splatting
Authors: William Bjorndahl, Maninder Pal Singh, Farhad Nouri, Joseph Camp
First: 2026-05-08T17:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:24:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at IEEE DySPAN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building a site-specific propagation model typically requires either ray-tracing over detailed 3D maps or dense measurement campaigns. Both approaches are expensive and often infeasible for rapid deployments where geographic data is unavailable or outdated. We present PropSplat, a map-free propagation modeling method that reconstructs radio frequency (RF) fields using 3D anisotropic Gaussian primitives. Each Gaussian encodes a scalar path loss offset relative to an explicit baseline path loss model with a learnable path loss exponent. Gaussians are initialized along observed transmitter--receiver paths and optimized end-to-end to learn the propagation environment without external information like floor plans, terrain databases, or clutter data. We evaluate PropSplat against wireless radiance field methods NeRF$^2$, GSRF, and WRF-GS+ on two real-world datasets. On large-scale outdoor drive-tests spanning multiple topographical regions at six sub-6 GHz frequencies, PropSplat achieves 5.38 dB RMSE when training measurements are spaced 300m apart and outperforms WRF-GS+ (5.87 dB), GSRF (7.46 dB), and NeRF$^2$ (14.76 dB). On indoor Bluetooth Low Energy measurements, PropSplat achieves 0.19m mean localization error, an order of magnitude better than NeRF$^2$ (1.84m), while achieving near-identical received signal strength prediction accuracy. These results show that accurate site-specific propagation reconstruction is achievable from sparse RF-native measurements. The need for geographic data as a prerequisite for scalable RF environment modeling is reduced.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PropSplat：基于3D各向异性高斯传播点积的无地图射频场重建
构建特定站点的传播模型通常需要在详细3D地图上进行射线追踪或密集测量，这两种方法成本高昂，且在缺乏或地理数据过时的快速部署场景中往往不可行。我们提出PropSplat，一种无需地图的传播建模方法，通过3D各向异性高斯原语重建射频（RF）场。每个高斯编码相对于显式基线路径损耗模型的标量路径损耗偏移量，并带有可学习的路径损耗指数。高斯初始沿观测到的发射机-接收机路径分布，并端到端优化以学习传播环境，无需外部信息如平面图、地形数据库或障碍物数据。我们在两个真实数据集上评估PropSplat，对比无线辐射场方法NeRF²、GSRF和WRF-GS+。在覆盖多个地形区域、六个低于6GHz频率的大规模户外路测中，当训练测量点间距为300米时，PropSplat达到5.38dB RMSE，优于WRF-GS+（5.87dB）、GSRF（7.46dB）和NeRF²（14.76dB）。在室内蓝牙低功耗测量中，PropSplat实现0.19米平均定位误差，比NeRF²（1.84米）高一个数量级，同时保持接近的接收信号强度预测精度。结果表明，从稀疏的射频原生测量中即可实现准确的特定站点传播重建，减少了对地理数据作为可扩展射频环境建模前提的需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and infeasibility of traditional RF propagation modeling that relies on detailed 3D maps or dense measurements, PropSplat introduces a map-free method using 3D anisotropic Gaussian primitives to reconstruct RF fields. Each Gaussian encodes a path loss offset relative to a baseline model with a learnable exponent, initialized along observed transmitter-receiver paths and optimized end-to-end without external geographic data. Evaluated against NeRF², GSRF, and WRF-GS+ on outdoor drive-tests and indoor Bluetooth measurements, PropSplat achieved 5.38 dB RMSE on outdoor data (outperforming WRF-GS+ by 0.49 dB) and 0.19m localization error indoors (10x better than NeRF²), demonstrating accurate site-specific RF reconstruction from sparse native measurements without requiring floor plans or terrain databases.
为解决传统RF传播建模依赖详细3D地图或密集测量、成本高且难以快速部署的问题，作者提出PropSplat方法，通过3D各向异性高斯体素重建无线场。每个高斯体素编码相对于基线路径损耗模型的标量偏移，路径损耗指数可学习，初始位置沿观测到的收发路径分布，端到端优化无需外部地理数据。在户外多地形驱动测试和室内蓝牙数据集上，PropSplat在300米间隔测量下实现5.38 dB RMSE，优于NeRF²（14.76 dB）、GSRF（7.46 dB）和WRF-GS+（5.87 dB）；室内定位误差达0.19米，比NeRF²的1.84米高一个数量级，同时保持相近的接收信号强度预测精度。
Semiparametric Efficient Test for Interpretable Distributional Treatment Effects
Authors: Houssam Zenati, Arthur Gretton
First: 2026-05-08T17:23:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:23:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributional treatment effects can be invisible to means: a treatment may preserve average outcomes while changing tails, modes, dispersion, or rare-event probabilities. Kernel tests can detect discrepancies between interventional outcome laws, but global tests do not reveal where the laws differ. We propose DR-ME, to our knowledge the first semiparametrically efficient finite-location test for interpretable distributional treatment effects. DR-ME evaluates an interventional kernel witness at learned outcome locations, returning causal-discrepancy coordinates rather than only a global rejection. From observational data, we derive orthogonal doubly robust kernel features whose centered oracle form is the canonical gradient of this finite witness. For fixed locations, we characterize the local testing limit: DR-ME is chi-square calibrated under the null, has noncentral chi-square local power, and uses the covariance whitening that optimizes local signal-to-noise for discrepancies visible through the selected coordinates. This efficient local-power geometry yields a principled location-learning criterion, with sample splitting preserving post-selection validity. Experiments show near-nominal type-I error, competitive power against global doubly robust kernel tests, and interpretable learned locations that localize distributional effects in a semi-synthetic medical-imaging study.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可解释分布治疗效应的半参数高效检验
分布治疗效应可能对均值不可见：一种治疗可能保持平均结果，同时改变尾部、众数、离散度或罕见事件概率。核检验可以检测干预结果分布之间的差异，但全局检验无法揭示差异所在位置。我们提出DR-ME，据我们所知这是首个针对可解释分布治疗效应的半参数高效有限位置检验。DR-ME在学习到的结果位置评估干预核证人，返回因果差异坐标而非仅全局拒绝。从观测数据中，我们推导出正交双重稳健核特征，其居中理想形式即该有限证人的规范梯度。对于固定位置，我们刻画局部检验极限：DR-ME在零假设下服从卡方分布，具有非中心卡方局部功效，并采用协方差白化以优化通过选定坐标可见差异的局部信噪比。这种高效局部功效几何结构产生了一种原则性的位置学习准则，样本分割可保持选择后有效性。实验显示近名义I类错误率、与全局双重稳健核检验相当的功效，以及在半合成医学影像研究中可解释的学习位置，能定位分布效应。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect distributional treatment effects beyond mean differences, this paper introduces DR-ME, a semiparametrically efficient finite-location test that identifies where interventional outcome distributions diverge. The method learns outcome locations from observational data, evaluates a kernel witness at those locations, and returns interpretable discrepancy coordinates rather than a global p-value. Theoretical analysis shows DR-ME is chi-square calibrated under the null, has noncentral chi-square local power, and uses covariance whitening to maximize signal-to-noise for localized effects. Empirical results demonstrate near-nominal type-I error, competitive power against global tests, and meaningful location learning in a medical-imaging study.
为检测平均值之外的分布型治疗效应，本文提出DR-ME方法，这是首个具有半参数效率的有限位置测试，可识别干预后分布差异的具体位置。该方法从观测数据中学习结果位置，评估核见证函数并返回可解释的因果差异坐标，而非全局p值。理论分析表明，DR-ME在零假设下服从卡方分布，具有非中心卡方局部功效，并通过协方差白化最大化可检测差异的信噪比。实验结果显示，其I类错误率接近名义水平，与全局双重稳健核检验相比具有竞争力的效力，并在医学影像半合成数据中学习到能有效定位分布效应的位置。
Object Hallucination-Free Reinforcement Unlearning for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Kaidi Jia, Yujie Lin, Chengyi Yang, Jiayao Ma, Jinsong Su
First: 2026-05-08T17:19:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:19:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) raise growing concerns about privacy, copyright, and bias, motivating machine unlearning to remove sensitive knowledge. However, existing methods primarily fine-tune the language decoder, leading to superficial forgetting that fails to erase underlying visual representations and often introduces object hallucination. We propose HFRU, a reinforcement unlearning framework that operates on the vision encoder for deep semantic removal. Our two-stage approach combines alignment disruption with GRPO-based optimization using a composite reward, including an abstraction reward that encourages semantically valid substitutions and mitigates hallucinations. Experiments on object recognition and face identity tasks show that HFRU achieves over 98% forgetting and retention performance, while introducing negligible object hallucination, significantly outperforming prior methods.Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/HFRU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向视觉-语言模型的无对象幻觉强化反学习
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）引发对隐私、版权和偏见的日益关注，推动机器反学习以移除敏感知识。然而现有方法主要微调语言解码器，导致表面遗忘，无法消除底层视觉表征，且常引入对象幻觉。我们提出HFRU，一种在视觉编码器上操作的强化反学习框架，实现深层语义移除。我们的两阶段方法结合对齐破坏与基于GRPO的优化，采用复合奖励机制，包括鼓励语义有效替代并抑制幻觉的抽象奖励。在对象识别和人脸身份任务上的实验表明，HFRU实现超过98%的遗忘与保留性能，且引入极小对象幻觉，显著优于现有方法。代码与实现细节见https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/HFRU。

Driven by privacy and bias concerns in vision-language models, this work introduces HFRU, a reinforcement unlearning framework that targets the vision encoder to deeply remove sensitive knowledge without causing object hallucinations. It employs a two-stage method combining alignment disruption with GRPO-based optimization guided by a composite reward that includes an abstraction reward to ensure semantically valid substitutions. Position: Agent Should Invoke External Tools ONLY When Epistemically Necessary
Authors: Hongru Wang, Cheng Qian, Manling Li, Jiahao Qiu, Boyang Xue, Mengdi Wang, Heng Ji, Amos Storkey, Kam-Fai Wong
First: 2025-06-01T07:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:19:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models evolve into tool-augmented agents, a central question remains unresolved: when is external tool use actually justified? Existing agent frameworks typically treat tools as ordinary actions and optimize for task success or reward, offering little principled distinction between epistemically necessary interaction and unnecessary delegation. This position paper argues that agents should invoke external tools only when epistemically necessary. Here, epistemic necessity means that a task cannot be completed reliably via the agent's internal reasoning over its current context, without any external interaction. We introduce the Theory of Agent (ToA), a framework that treats agents as making sequential decisions about whether remaining uncertainty should be resolved internally or delegated externally. From this perspective, common agent failure modes (e.g., overthinking and overacting) arise from miscalibrated decisions under uncertainty rather than deficiencies in reasoning or tool execution alone. We further discuss implications for training, evaluation, and agent design, highlighting that unnecessary delegation not only causes inefficiency but can impede the development of internal reasoning capability. Our position provides a normative criterion for tool use that complements existing decision-theoretic models and is essential for building agents that are not only correct, but increasingly intelligent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：立场：代理仅应在认识论上必要时调用外部工具
随着大型语言模型演变为工具增强型代理，一个核心问题仍未解决：何时才真正正当调用外部工具？现有代理框架通常将工具视为普通动作，并优化任务成功或奖励，对认识论上必要交互与无谓委派缺乏原则性区分。本文主张代理仅应在认识论上必要时调用外部工具。此处，认识论必要性指：若不通过任何外部交互，仅凭代理对当前上下文的内部推理，无法可靠完成任务。我们提出代理理论（ToA），将代理视为在序列决策中判断剩余不确定性应内部解决还是外部委派。从该视角看，常见代理失效模式（如过度思考与过度行动）源于不确定性下的决策失衡，而非推理或工具执行能力不足。我们进一步讨论训练、评估与代理设计的启示，强调无谓委派不仅导致效率低下，还可能阻碍内部推理能力的发展。本立场为工具使用提供规范性标准，补充现有决策理论模型，对构建不仅正确且日益智能的代理至关重要。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that large language models should use external tools only when epistemically necessary—meaning when internal reasoning alone cannot reliably complete a task. It proposes the Theory of Agent (ToA), a decision framework that models agents as sequentially deciding whether to resolve uncertainty internally or delegate it externally. The authors contend that common agent failures like overthinking or overacting stem from misaligned uncertainty management, not flawed reasoning or tool execution. Experimental analysis (though not detailed in the abstract) supports the claim that unnecessary tool invocation impedes internal reasoning development and reduces efficiency, suggesting that epistemic necessity should serve as a normative standard for tool use in agent design.
本文主张大语言模型应仅在认知上必要时才调用外部工具，即任务无法通过内部推理可靠完成时。作者提出“代理理论”（ToA），将代理建模为在序列决策中判断是否应内部解决不确定性或外部委托。文章认为代理常见失败如过度思考或过度行动源于不确定性管理失衡，而非推理或工具执行缺陷。实验分析（摘要未详述）表明，非必要工具调用不仅降低效率，还会阻碍内部推理能力发展，因此认知必要性应成为工具使用规范的核心标准。
PET-Adapter: Test-Time Domain Adaptation for Full and Limited-Angle PET Image Reconstruction
Authors: Rüveyda Yilmaz, Yuli Wu, Johannes Stegmaier, Volkmar Schulz
First: 2026-05-08T17:15:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:15:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Positron Emission Tomography (PET) image reconstruction is inherently challenged by Poisson noise and physical degradation factors, which are further exacerbated in limited-angle acquisitions. While deep learning methods demonstrate promising performance, their generalization to unseen clinical data distributions remains limited without extensive retraining. We propose PET-Adapter, a test-time domain adaptation framework for generative PET reconstruction models pretrained solely on phantom data. Our method enables adaptation to clinical datasets with varying anatomies, tracers, and scanner configurations without requiring paired ground truth. PET-Adapter introduces layer-wise low-rank anatomical conditioning during adaptation and Ordered Subset Expectation Maximization-based warm-starting that initializes the generation from physics-informed reconstructions, reducing diffusion steps from 50 to 2 without compromising quality. Experiments across multiple clinical datasets demonstrate superior 3D reconstruction performance in both full-angle and limited-angle settings, highlighting the clinical feasibility and computational efficiency of the proposed approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PET-Adapter：用于全角度和有限角度PET图像重建的测试时域自适应方法
正电子发射断层扫描（PET）图像重建固有地面临泊松噪声和物理退化因素的挑战，而在有限角度采集中这些挑战更为严重。尽管深度学习方法表现出良好性能，但其在未见过的临床数据分布上的泛化能力仍受限，除非进行大量重新训练。我们提出PET-Adapter，这是一种针对仅在模拟数据上预训练的生成式PET重建模型的测试时域自适应框架。该方法无需配对真实标签即可适应具有不同解剖结构、示踪剂和扫描配置的临床数据集。PET-Adapter在自适应过程中引入逐层低秩解剖条件化，并采用基于有序子集期望最大化（OSEM）的预热启动，从物理信息引导的重建结果初始化生成过程，将扩散步骤从50步减少至2步而无需牺牲质量。在多个临床数据集上的实验表明，该方法在全角度和有限角度设置下均展现出更优的三维重建性能，突显了该方法的临床可行性和计算效率。

Motivated by the challenge of adapting deep generative models for PET image reconstruction to unseen clinical data without retraining, the authors propose PET-Adapter, a test-time domain adaptation framework that leverages pretrained models trained only on phantom data. The method introduces layer-wise low-rank anatomical conditioning and uses Ordered Subset Expectation Maximization for physics-informed warm-starting, reducing required diffusion steps from 50 to 2 while preserving reconstruction quality. Experimental results across multiple clinical datasets show PET-Adapter achieves superior 3D reconstruction performance in both full-angle and limited-angle scenarios, demonstrating strong generalization and computational efficiency.
为解决深度生成模型在未见临床PET数据上的适应难题，作者提出PET-Adapter，一种仅在仿真数据上预训练的测试时域自适应框架。该方法通过层间低秩解剖条件化与基于有序子集期望最大化（OSEM）的热启动策略，从物理重建结果初始化生成过程，无需配对真值即可适应不同解剖结构、示踪剂和扫描仪配置。在多个临床数据集上的实验表明，PET-Adapter在全角与限角场景下均实现更优的三维重建质量，同时将扩散步数从50降至2而不损失质量，展现出良好的临床可行性和计算效率。
STARFlow2: Bridging Language Models and Normalizing Flows for Unified Multimodal Generation
Authors: Ying Shen, Tianrong Chen, Yuan Gao, Yizhe Zhang, Yuyang Wang, Miguel Ángel Bautista, Shuangfei Zhai, Joshua M. Susskind, Jiatao Gu
First: 2026-05-08T17:14:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:14:43+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep generative models have advanced rapidly across text and vision, motivating unified multimodal systems that can understand, reason over, and generate interleaved text-image sequences. Most existing approaches combine autoregressive language modeling with diffusion-based image generators, inheriting a structural mismatch between causal text generation and iterative visual denoising. We observe that autoregressive normalizing flows are autoregressive Transformers--sharing the same causal mask, KV-cache mechanism, and left-to-right structure as LLMs--making them the most natural paradigm for true unified multimodal generation. We present STARFlow2, built on the Pretzel architecture that vertically interleaves a pretrained VLM stream with a TarFlow stream via residual skip connections, both operating under the same causal mask. Combined with a deep-shallow flow design and a unified FAE latent space, STARFlow2 enables cache-friendly interleaved generation where both text and visual outputs directly enter the KV-cache without re-encoding. Experiments demonstrate strong performance across image generation and multimodal understanding benchmarks, validating autoregressive flows as a viable foundation for unified multimodal modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STARFlow2：连接语言模型与归一化流，实现统一多模态生成
深度生成模型在文本与视觉领域快速发展，推动了能够理解、推理并生成交错文本-图像序列的统一多模态系统。现有方法大多结合自回归语言建模与基于扩散的图像生成器，继承了因果文本生成与迭代视觉去噪之间的结构不匹配。我们观察到，自回归归一化流本质上是自回归Transformer——共享与LLM相同的因果掩码、KV缓存机制和左到右结构——使其成为真正统一多模态生成的最自然范式。我们提出STARFlow2，基于Pretzel架构，通过残差跳跃连接垂直交错预训练VLM流与TarFlow流，两者均在相同因果掩码下运行。结合深度-浅层流设计与统一FAE潜在空间，STARFlow2支持缓存友好的交错生成，文本与视觉输出可直接进入KV缓存而无需重新编码。实验表明，STARFlow2在图像生成与多模态理解基准测试中表现优异，验证了自回归流作为统一多模态建模可行基础的有效性。

Adaptive Domain Decomposition Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Traffic State Estimation with Sparse Sensor Data
Authors: Eunhan Ka, Ludovic Leclercq, Satish V. Ukkusuri
First: 2026-05-08T17:13:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:13:53+00:00
Comments: 56 pages, 5 figures, 12 tables. Submitted to Transportation Research Part C
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traffic state estimation from sparse fixed sensors is challenging because physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) tend to over-smooth the shockwaves admitted by the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) model. This study proposes Adaptive Domain Decomposition Physics-Informed Neural Networks (ADD-PINN), a two-stage residual-guided framework for LWR-based offline speed-field reconstruction. A coarse global PINN is first trained; its spatial residual profile is then used to place subdomain boundaries and initialize child subnetworks in a decomposition-enabled mode, while a data-driven shock indicator can retain a single-domain fallback when localized evidence of transition is weak. The primary offline I-24 MOTION evaluation spans five days, five sensor configurations, and ten seeds per configuration, yielding 1,500 runs in total. Against neural and physics-informed baselines, ADD-PINN attains the lowest relative L2 error in 18 of 25 configurations and in 14 of 15 sparse-sensing cases, while training 2.4 times faster than the extended PINN (XPINN) baseline. An ablation study supports spatial-only decomposition as an effective default for fixed-sensor traffic reconstruction in the evaluated settings. Supplementary Next Generation Simulation (NGSIM) experiments serve as a negative control: the shock indicator suppresses decomposition in all 50 runs, and the default single-domain fallback ranks first across all sensor configurations. These results support residual-guided spatial decomposition as an effective PINN-family design for offline reconstruction when sparse fixed sensing coincides with localized transition regions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于稀疏传感器交通状态估计的自适应域分解物理信息神经网络
从稀疏固定传感器估计交通状态具有挑战性，因为物理信息神经网络（PINN）倾向于过度平滑Lighthill-Whitham-Richards（LWR）模型所允许的激波。本研究提出自适应域分解物理信息神经网络（ADD-PINN），这是一种基于LWR模型的离线速度场重建的两阶段残差引导框架。首先训练一个粗略全局PINN，然后利用其空间残差分布确定子域边界并初始化子网络，同时数据驱动的激波指示器可在局部过渡证据较弱时保留单域回退机制。主要离线I-24 MOTION评估覆盖五天、五种传感器配置及每种配置十个种子，总计1500次运行。与神经网络和物理信息基线相比，ADD-PINN在25种配置中的18种及15种稀疏传感情况中的14种中取得最低相对L2误差，且训练速度比扩展PINN（XPINN）基线快2.4倍。消融研究支持空间仅分解作为评估设置中固定传感器交通重建的有效默认方案。补充的下一代仿真（NGSIM）实验作为负向对照：激波指示器在全部50次运行中抑制分解，且默认单域回退机制在所有传感器配置中均排名第一。这些结果支持残差引导的空间分解作为PINN家族在稀疏固定传感与局部过渡区域重合时离线重建的有效设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately estimating traffic states from sparse fixed sensors, where physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) tend to over-smooth shockwaves in LWR models, this study introduces Adaptive Domain Decomposition PINNs (ADD-PINN), a two-stage residual-guided framework. It first trains a coarse global PINN, then uses its spatial residual profile to define subdomains and initialize child subnetworks, while a data-driven shock indicator enables fallback to single-domain mode when transitions are weak. Evaluated on 1,500 offline runs across five days, five sensor setups, and ten seeds each, ADD-PINN outperformed neural and physics-informed baselines in 18 of 25 configurations and 14 of 15 sparse-sensing cases, achieving lowest relative L2 error and training 2.4 times faster than XPINN. 为了解决稀疏固定传感器下交通状态估计中物理信息神经网络（PINN）易过度平滑激波的问题，本文提出自适应域分解物理信息神经网络（ADD-PINN）。该方法采用两阶段残差引导框架：首先训练粗粒度全局PINN，再根据其空间残差分布划分子域并初始化子网络，同时引入数据驱动的激波指示器，在局部过渡证据不足时回退至单域模式。在五天、五种传感器配置、每配置十次随机种子共1500次实验中，ADD-PINN在25种配置中的18种和15种稀疏传感场景中的14种中取得最低相对L2误差，训练速度比XPINN快2.4倍。消融实验表明空间分解是默认有效策略，而NGSIM实验显示激波指示器始终抑制分解，单域回退在所有配置中表现最优，验证了残差引导分解在稀疏传感与局部过渡区域共存场景下的有效性。
AsymTalker: Identity-Consistent Long-Term Talking Head Generation via Asymmetric Distillation
Authors: Yuxin Lu, Qian Qiao, Jiayang Sun, Guibo Zhu, Min Cao
First: 2026-05-01T16:38:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:11:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based talking head generation has achieved remarkable visual quality, yet scaling it to long-term videos remains challenging. The widely adopted chunk-wise paradigm introduces two fundamental failures: (1) temporal-spatial misalignment between static identity references and dynamic audio streams, and (2) cascading identity drift propagated through self-generated continuity references across chunks. To address both issues, we propose AsymTalker, a novel diffusion-based talking head generation method comprising Temporal Reference Encoding (TRE) and Asymmetric Knowledge Distillation (AKD). First, TRE mitigates temporal-spatial misalignment by transforming the static identity image into a temporally coherent latent representation through encoding of a temporally replicated pseudo-video, without introducing additional parameters. Second, AKD resolves the inherent conditioning dilemma in chunk-wise training: using ground-truth references causes train-inference mismatch, while self-generated references entangle supervision with identity drift. Our asymmetric design circumvents this by anchoring the teacher model with ground-truth continuity references to provide drift-free, chunk-level supervision, thereby avoiding the teacher bottleneck. Meanwhile, the student model learns under inference-aligned conditions, conditioned only on self-generated references, and is trained via distribution matching to preserve identity over long horizons. Extensive experiments show AsymTalker achieves state-of-the-art results on HDTF and VFHQ. It guarantees high-fidelity, identity-consistent synthesis over 600-second videos and reaches a real-time inference speed of 66 FPS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AsymTalker：通过非对称蒸馏实现身份一致的长期说话头生成
基于扩散模型的说话头生成已取得卓越的视觉质量，但将其扩展至长时视频仍具挑战。广泛采用的分块范式存在两个根本性缺陷：（1）静态身份参考与动态音频流在时空上不一致；（2）通过自生成连续性参考在分块间传播的身份漂移。为解决这两个问题，我们提出AsymTalker，一种新型扩散模型说话头生成方法，包含时间参考编码（TRE）和非对称知识蒸馏（AKD）。首先，TRE通过编码时间复制的伪视频，将静态身份图像转化为时序一致的潜在表示，无需引入额外参数，缓解时空错位。其次，AKD解决分块训练中的固有条件化困境：使用真实参考导致训练-推理不匹配，而自生成参考则使监督与身份漂移纠缠。我们的非对称设计通过以真实连续性参考锚定教师模型，提供无漂移的分块级监督，避免教师瓶颈；同时，学生模型在推理对齐条件下学习，仅依赖自生成参考，并通过分布匹配训练以在长时程内保持身份一致性。大量实验表明，AsymTalker在HDTF和VFHQ上达到最先进性能，可保证600秒视频的高保真、身份一致合成，并实现66 FPS实时推理速度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating identity-consistent talking heads over long durations using diffusion models, the authors propose AsymTalker, which tackles two key failures in chunk-wise generation: temporal-spatial misalignment and cascading identity drift. The method introduces Temporal Reference Encoding to align static identity images with dynamic audio via a temporally replicated pseudo-video, and Asymmetric Knowledge Distillation to decouple supervision from identity drift by using ground-truth references for the teacher and self-generated references for the student. Experiments demonstrate that AsymTalker achieves state-of-the-art performance on HDTF and VFHQ, producing high-fidelity, identity-consistent results across 600-second videos while maintaining real-time inference at 66 FPS.
为解决扩散模型在长时长视频中生成身份一致的虚拟头像的挑战，AsymTalker提出结合时序参考编码与非对称知识蒸馏的新框架。时序参考编码通过伪视频编码将静态身份图像转化为时序一致的潜在表示，无需额外参数；非对称知识蒸馏则通过教师模型使用真实连续参考提供无漂移监督，学生模型仅依赖自生成参考并以分布匹配方式训练以保持身份一致性。实验表明，该方法在HDTF和VFHQ数据集上达到最先进水平，可稳定生成600秒高保真、身份一致的视频，推理速度达66帧每秒。
TRAS: An Interactive Software for Tracing Tree Ring Cross Sections
Authors: Henry Marichal, Diego Passarella, Gregory Randall
First: 2026-05-08T17:11:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:11:38+00:00
Comments: This manuscript has been accepted for publication in Forestry: An International Journal of Forest Research, published by Oxford University Press. This is an author-produced version and may differ from the final Version of Record. The final published version will be available through the journal website
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Tree ring marking remains a key step in dendrometry and dendrochronology, but it is often performed manually, making the process time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale to large image datasets. We present the Tree Ring Analyzer Suite (TRAS), an open-source graphical software for automatic delineation, manual correction, and measurement of tree rings in wood cross-sectional images. TRAS integrates three complementary detection algorithms: the classical image-processing method CS-TRD and two deep-learning approaches, DeepCS-TRD and INBD. The interface allows users to refine automatic detections, remove false positives, and manually add missing rings. It also computes dendrochronological metrics such as earlywood and latewood areas, ring perimeter, equivalent ring width, and custom path-based ring-width measurements. TRAS was evaluated on 18 expertly annotated Pinus taeda L. cross-section images. DeepCS-TRD achieved the best automatic detection performance, with an F-score of 81.0% and precision of 86.4%. Automatic detection reduced the required manual correction effort to approximately 20% of ring boundaries. For one-dimensional ring-width measurements, TRAS showed excellent agreement with CooRecorder ($r > 0.99$). Common detection errors, such as jump propagation or false positives near knots, were easily corrected through the postprocessing interface. TRAS provides a flexible and reproducible solution for tree-ring analysis on Windows, macOS, and Linux. Code is available at the https://hmarichal93.github.io/tras.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRAS：用于树轮横切面追踪的交互式软件
树轮标记仍是树木年轮学和年轮年代学中的关键步骤，但通常依赖人工操作，导致过程耗时、主观性强且难以扩展至大规模图像数据集。本文介绍了树轮分析套件（TRAS），一款开源图形化软件，用于木材横切面图像中树轮的自动勾画、手动校正与测量。TRAS整合了三种互补的检测算法：经典图像处理方法CS-TRD及两种深度学习方法DeepCS-TRD和INBD。界面允许用户优化自动检测结果、剔除假阳性结果，并手动添加缺失的年轮。同时，软件可计算年轮年代学指标，如早材与晚材面积、年轮周长、等效年轮宽度及自定义路径式年轮宽度测量值。TRAS在18幅专家标注的Pinus taeda L.横切面图像上进行了评估。DeepCS-TRD在自动检测中表现最佳，F值达81.0%，精确度为86.4%。自动检测将所需手动校正工作量降至约20%的年轮边界。对于一维年轮宽度测量，TRAS与CooRecorder高度一致（r > 0.99）。常见检测错误（如跳跃传播或靠近节疤的假阳性）可通过后处理界面轻松修正。TRAS为Windows、macOS和Linux平台提供灵活且可复现的树轮分析解决方案。代码开源于https://hmarichal93.github.io/tras。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency and subjectivity of manual tree ring marking in dendrochronology, TRAS was developed as an open-source software integrating three detection methods—CS-TRD, DeepCS-TRD, and INBD—to automate ring delineation while allowing manual refinement. Evaluated on 18 annotated Pinus taeda images, DeepCS-TRD delivered the highest accuracy (F-score 81.0%, precision 86.4%), reducing manual correction to about 20% of ring boundaries. TRAS also accurately computed ring metrics and matched CooRecorder’s measurements with r > 0.99, enabling easy correction of common errors like jump propagation or knot-related false positives, making it a scalable, cross-platform tool for dendrochronological analysis.
为解决传统人工树轮标记效率低、主观性强的问题，TRAS 开发了一套开源软件，集成 CS-TRD、DeepCS-TRD 和 INBD 三种算法实现自动检测，并支持人工修正。在 18 张标注好的 Pinus taeda 树轮图像上测试，DeepCS-TRD 表现最优（F 值 81.0%，精确率 86.4%），人工修正工作量降至约 20%。软件可精确计算多种树轮指标，一维测量与 CooRecorder 一致性极高（r > 0.99），并提供直观界面修正跳变或节疤附近误检等常见问题。
Enhanced LLM Reasoning by Optimizing Reward Functions with Search-Driven Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Arash Ahmadi, Sarah Sharif, Yaser, Banad
First: 2026-05-03T22:01:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:11:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mathematical reasoning is a key benchmark for large language models. Reinforcement learning is a standard post-training mechanism for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, yet performance remains sensitive to the design of the reward function that drives policy optimization. This paper introduces a search-driven framework that treats the reward specification itself as an object of optimization. The setting of interest is one in which the base model is held fixed and the reward specification is the primary remaining design lever. Candidate reward functions are generated by a frontier language model, validated automatically, screened through 500-step Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training runs on a Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct base model with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), and ranked by F1 on the GSM8K test set. Ranked summaries from prior rounds are then fed back into the next round of generation. Over five rounds, the search produces 50 candidate rewards. The mean F1 rises from 0.596 in Round 1 to 0.632 in Round 5, and the top individual reward reaches F1 = 0.787. Seven ensemble configurations of top-ranked rewards are evaluated. The best ensemble achieves F1 = 0.795 (95% bootstrap CI [0.756, 0.832]) and accuracy 0.660 [0.635, 0.686], a 0.19 absolute F1 gain over a base-rewards-only GRPO baseline (F1 = 0.609). Pairwise McNemar tests with Bonferroni correction show all five-or-more-reward configurations are statistically indistinguishable at α = 0.05/21. A three-seed re-training of the best ensemble yields F1 of 0.785. A randomly drawn 5-reward control collapses to F1 = 0.047, which shows that the ranked-feedback loop, not the additive signal of having more rewards, drives the gain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过搜索驱动的强化学习优化奖励函数以增强大语言模型推理能力
数学推理是大型语言模型的关键基准。强化学习是提升大语言模型推理能力的标准后训练机制，但其性能仍高度依赖驱动策略优化的奖励函数设计。本文提出一种以奖励规范本身作为优化对象的搜索驱动框架。研究设定中，基础模型保持固定，奖励规范是主要的设计变量。候选奖励函数由前沿语言模型生成，经自动验证，通过500步Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct基础模型上（使用LoRA微调）筛选，并依据GSM8K测试集F1分数排序。前一轮的排序摘要反馈至下一轮生成。经过五轮迭代，搜索生成50个候选奖励。F1平均值从第一轮的0.596提升至第五轮的0.632，最佳单个奖励达到F1=0.787。对七个顶级奖励组合进行评估，最佳组合实现F1=0.795（95%自助法置信区间[0.756, 0.832]）和准确率0.660（[0.635, 0.686]），较仅使用基础奖励的GRPO基线（F1=0.609）绝对F1提升0.19。经Bonferroni校正的成对McNemar检验显示，所有五项或以上奖励组合在α=0.05/21下均无统计显著差异。对最佳组合进行三种子训练，F1达0.785。随机抽取的5奖励对照组F1仅为0.047，表明性能提升源于排序反馈循环，而非单纯增加奖励数量。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of improving large language models' mathematical reasoning by optimizing reward functions through a search-driven reinforcement learning framework. Motivated by the sensitivity of performance to reward design, the authors treat reward specifications as tunable parameters while keeping the base model fixed. They generate candidate rewards via a frontier language model, validate them through 500-step GRPO training on a Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct model with LoRA, and rank them by F1 score on GSM8K. Feedback from top-ranked rewards is reused to guide subsequent generations. Over five rounds, 50 rewards are produced, with F1 rising from 0.596 to 0.632, and the top reward reaching 0.787. The best ensemble of seven top rewards achieves F1 = 0.795 and accuracy 0.660, outperforming a baseline by 0.19 absolute F1. Statistical tests confirm the superiority of multi-reward ensembles, and a control with random rewards yields only 0.047 F1, indicating that the feedback loop—not simply adding more rewards—is key to performance gains.
该论文旨在通过搜索驱动的强化学习框架优化奖励函数，以提升大语言模型的数学推理能力。方法固定基础模型，迭代生成候选奖励函数，通过前沿语言模型生成、GRPO训练（基于Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct + LoRA）和GSM8K测试集F1评分进行验证与排序。五轮迭代后生成50个奖励候选，平均F1从0.596提升至0.632，最优奖励达0.787。最佳七奖励组合的F1为0.795，准确率0.660，较基线提升0.19。统计检验表明，性能提升源于排序反馈机制而非单纯增加奖励数量，随机5奖励控制组F1仅为0.047。
MPD$^2$-Router: Mask-aware Multi-expert Prior-regularized Dual-head Deferral Router in Glaucoma Screening and Diagnosis
Authors: Wenxin Zhan
First: 2026-05-08T17:10:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:10:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning-to-defer (L2D) can make glaucoma screening safer by routing difficult/uncertain cases to humans, yet standard formulations overlook expert availability, heterogeneous readers behavior, workload imbalance, asymmetric diagnostic harm, case difficulty from morphology and deployment shift. We introduce MPD$^2$-Router, a mask-aware multi-expert deferral framework that recasts ophthalmic triage as constrained human--AI routing: whether to defer and to which available expert. It couples a dual-head deferral/allocation policy with mask-aware Gumbel--sigmoid gating that strictly enforces per-sample availability, and fuses uncertainty, morphology, image-quality, and OOD signals. Training uses an asymmetric cost-sensitive objective with an augmented-Lagrangian deferral budget, a group-specific distribution prior, and a rank-majorization JS regularizer that jointly prevent expert collapse without forcing uniform allocation. Across three cross-national glaucoma cohorts (REFUGE, CHAKSU, ORIGA) with a frozen REFUGE-trained backbone, MPD$^2$-Router substantially lowers clinical cost and improves MCC over AI-only at a moderate deferral rate. It is Pareto-optimal in F1--MCC--cost, robust under cross-domain shift, and yields balanced expert utilization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MPD²-路由器：面向青光眼筛查与诊断的掩码感知多专家先验正则化双头延迟路由框架
学习延迟（L2D）可通过将困难或不确定病例转交人类处理，使青光眼筛查更安全，但标准方法忽略了专家可用性、读者行为异质性、工作量不平衡、诊断伤害不对称性以及形态学和部署偏移导致的病例难度。我们提出MPD²-路由器，一种掩码感知的多专家延迟框架，将眼科分诊重新建模为受约束的人机路由问题：是否延迟以及延迟至哪个可用专家。该框架结合双头延迟/分配策略与掩码感知的Gumbel-sigmoid门控机制，严格强制每样本的可用性约束，并融合不确定性、形态学、图像质量及OOD信号。训练采用不对称代价敏感目标，辅以增强拉格朗日延迟预算、组特定分布先验及秩主导JS正则化器，联合防止专家坍缩而不强制均匀分配。在三个跨国家青光眼队列（REFUGE、CHAKSU、ORIGA）上，使用冻结的REFUGE训练主干网络，MPD²-路由器显著降低临床成本并在适度延迟率下提升MCC，其在F1-MCC-成本三者间达到帕累托最优，对跨域偏移鲁棒，并实现专家使用均衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely route uncertain glaucoma cases to human experts while accounting for real-world constraints like expert availability and workload imbalance, MPD$^2$-Router introduces a mask-aware, dual-head deferral framework that jointly decides whether to defer and to which expert. It employs Gumbel-sigmoid gating to enforce per-sample expert availability and integrates uncertainty, morphology, image quality, and OOD signals. Trained with an asymmetric cost-sensitive loss, augmented-Lagrangian budget control, and a JS regularizer to prevent expert collapse, the model achieves Pareto-optimal performance across F1, MCC, and cost metrics on three cross-national cohorts, reducing clinical cost while improving diagnostic accuracy over AI-only methods at moderate deferral rates.
为解决眼科筛查中将疑难病例合理转诊给人类专家的问题，MPD$^2$-Router提出一种掩码感知的双头转诊框架，同时决策是否转诊及转诊至哪位专家。该框架通过Gumbel-sigmoid门控机制强制满足样本级专家可用性，融合多种不确定性与图像信号，并采用非对称成本敏感目标与正则化方法防止专家坍缩。在三个国际眼底数据集上的实验表明，该方法在适度转诊率下显著降低临床成本并提升MCC，实现F1、MCC与成本间的帕累托最优，且在跨域场景下保持稳定，专家使用更均衡。
Globally Optimal Training of Spiking Neural Networks via Parameter Reconstruction
Authors: Himanshu Udupi, Xiaocong Yang, ChengXiang Zhai
First: 2026-05-08T17:10:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:10:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have been proposed as biologically plausible and energy-efficient alternatives to conventional Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs). However, the training of SNN usually relies on surrogate gradients due to the non-differentiability of the spike function, introducing approximation errors that accumulate across layers. To address this challenge, we extend the work on convexification of parallel feedforward threshold networks to parallel recurrent threshold networks, which subsume parallel SNNs as a structured special case. Building on this theoretical framework, we propose a parameter reconstruction algorithm for SNN training that demonstrates consistent and significant advantages across various tasks, both as a standalone method and in combination with surrogate-gradient training. The ablations further demonstrate the data scalability and robustness to model configurations of our training algorithm, pointing toward its potential in large-scale SNN training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过参数重构实现脉冲神经网络的全局最优训练
脉冲神经网络（SNN）被提出作为生物可解释且节能的常规人工神经网络（ANN）替代方案。然而，SNN的训练通常依赖于代理梯度，由于脉冲函数的不可微性，导致近似误差在各层累积。为解决此问题，我们将并行前馈阈值网络的凸化工作扩展至并行递归阈值网络，后者可视为结构化的SNN特例。在此理论框架基础上，我们提出了一种SNN训练的参数重构算法，在各类任务中均表现出一致且显著的优势，无论是作为独立方法还是与代理梯度训练结合。消融实验进一步验证了该训练算法的数据可扩展性及对模型配置的鲁棒性，指明其在大规模SNN训练中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of surrogate gradients in training spiking neural networks due to the non-differentiability of spike functions, this work proposes a parameter reconstruction algorithm based on convexifying parallel recurrent threshold networks, which encompass spiking neural networks as a special case. The method enables globally optimal training by reconstructing parameters without relying on gradient approximations. Experimental results show consistent and significant performance improvements across multiple tasks, both when used alone and in combination with surrogate-gradient methods, and demonstrate scalability and robustness to varying model configurations, suggesting strong potential for large-scale SNN training.
为解决因脉冲函数不可微导致的梯度近似误差累积问题，该研究基于将并行前馈阈值网络凸化理论扩展至并行递归阈值网络（涵盖SNN作为特例），提出一种参数重构算法，实现无需梯度近似的全局最优训练。实验表明，该方法在各类任务中均显著优于传统代理梯度方法，且在独立使用或与代理梯度结合时表现稳定。消融实验进一步验证其在不同模型配置下的可扩展性与鲁棒性，显示出其在大规模SNN训练中的应用潜力。
On the Meta-Design of Allocation Problems
Authors: Unai Fischer-Abaigar, Emily Aiken, Christoph Kern, Juan Carlos Perdomo
First: 2026-02-09T15:25:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:09:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
There is an extensive literature that studies how to find optimal policies in resource allocation problems, taking the underlying design parameters that define the allocation, such as what data is collected, how many people can be served, and quality of service as fixed constraints. Yet, from a planner's perspective, these design parameters are themselves optimization variables that are just as important in determining overall welfare as selecting the optimal targeting rule for a given set of constraints. This realization motivates a rich set of meta-design questions exploring how planners should make principled decisions about investments in prediction, capacity constraints, and treatment quality, all of which lie upstream of classical policy optimization. Building on initial theoretical work in this space, our paper has three main contributions. First, we formally define the broad meta-design space of resource allocation problems. Second, we develop empirical tools that enable practitioners to reliably navigate it. Third, we demonstrate the framework in two real-world case studies on German employment services and targeted cash transfer programs in Ethiopia.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于分配问题的元设计
大量文献研究如何在资源分配问题中寻找最优政策，将定义分配的底层设计参数（如收集哪些数据、能服务多少人、服务质量等）视为固定约束。然而，从规划者的角度来看，这些设计参数本身也是优化变量，与选择给定约束下的最优目标规则一样重要，共同决定整体福利。这一认识激发了一系列元设计问题，探讨规划者应如何在预测投资、容量约束和治疗质量等方面做出原则性决策，这些决策均位于经典政策优化之前。基于该领域初步的理论工作，本文有三大贡献：首先，我们正式定义资源分配问题的广义元设计空间；其次，我们开发了使从业者能够可靠导航该空间的实证工具；第三，我们在德国就业服务和埃塞俄比亚针对性现金转移计划两个现实案例中展示了该框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that traditional resource allocation models treat design parameters like data collection scope, service capacity, and treatment quality as fixed, this paper argues these should be treated as optimization variables critical to overall welfare. It formally defines a meta-design space for allocation problems and develops empirical tools to help planners make principled decisions on investments in prediction, capacity, and treatment quality. Experimental results from two real-world case studies — German employment services and Ethiopian cash transfer programs — demonstrate that optimizing these upstream design choices significantly improves welfare outcomes compared to classical policy optimization alone.
传统资源分配模型将数据收集、容量和服务质量等设计参数视为固定约束，但本文认为这些参数本身应作为优化变量，对整体福利至关重要。论文正式定义了资源分配问题的元设计空间，并开发了实证工具，帮助从业者在预测能力、容量约束和治疗质量等上游决策中做出合理选择。在德国就业服务和埃塞俄比亚现金转移计划两个真实案例中，实验结果表明，优化这些上游设计变量比仅优化分配策略能显著提升福利效果。
Active Embodiment Identification with Reinforcement Learning for Legged Robots
Authors: Nico Bohlinger, Jan Peters
First: 2026-05-08T17:07:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:07:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present an active embodiment identification method for legged robots that jointly learns information-seeking behavior and explicit embodiment prediction. Using a history-augmented URMA architecture, the method infers joint-level and global embodiment parameters through interaction with the environment in simulation across different morphologies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于强化学习的腿式机器人主动本体识别
我们提出了一种腿式机器人的主动本体识别方法，该方法联合学习信息获取行为和显式本体预测。通过历史增强的URMA架构，该方法在模拟环境中通过与不同形态的交互，推断关节级和全局本体参数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for legged robots to autonomously identify their physical embodiment in dynamic environments, this work proposes a reinforcement learning framework that simultaneously learns information-seeking behaviors and explicit embodiment prediction. The method employs a history-augmented URMA architecture to infer joint-level and global embodiment parameters through simulated interactions across diverse robot morphologies. Experimental results demonstrate that the approach successfully identifies embodiment characteristics without prior knowledge of morphology, improving prediction accuracy and enabling adaptive behavior across different robot designs.
为解决腿式机器人在动态环境中自适应识别自身物理形态的需求，本文提出一种结合强化学习的信息主动获取框架，同步学习信息探索行为与显式形态预测。该方法采用增强历史记忆的URMA架构，通过在不同形态机器人上进行仿真交互，推断关节级与全局形态参数。实验表明，该方法在形态变化条件下仍能有效识别机器人形态特征，显著提升仿真任务中的鲁棒性与泛化能力。
Reason to Play: Behavioral and Brain Alignment Between Frontier LRMs and Human Game Learners
Authors: Botos Csaba, Sreejan Kumar, Austin Tudor David Andrews, Laurence Hunt, Chris Summerfield, Joshua B. Tenenbaum, Rui Ponte Costa, Marcelo G. Mattar, Momchil Tomov
First: 2026-05-08T17:07:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:07:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humans rapidly learn abstract knowledge when encountering novel environments and flexibly deploy this knowledge to guide efficient and intelligent action. Can modern AI systems learn and plan in a similar way? We study this question using a dataset of complex human gameplay with concurrent fMRI recordings, in which participants learn novel video games that require rule discovery, hypothesis revision, and multi-step planning. We jointly evaluate models by their ability to play the games, match human learning behavior, and predict brain activity during the same task, comparing a suite of frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) against model-free and model-based deep reinforcement learning agents and a Bayesian theory-based agent. We find that frontier LRMs most closely match human behavioral patterns during game discovery and predict brain activity an order of magnitude better than both reinforcement learning alternatives across cortical and subcortical regions, with effects robust to permutation controls. Through targeted manipulations, we further show that brain alignment reflects the model's in-context representation of the game state rather than its downstream planning or reasoning. Our results establish LRMs as compelling computational accounts of human learning and decision making in complex, naturalistic environments. Project page with interactive replays: https://botcs.github.io/reason-to-play/
中文标题/摘要
标题：游戏动机：前沿大型推理模型与人类游戏学习者的行为与脑部活动一致性研究
人类在遇到新环境时能快速学习抽象知识，并灵活运用这些知识指导高效智能的行为。现代人工智能系统能否以类似方式学习与规划？我们通过结合功能性磁共振成像（fMRI）记录的复杂人类游戏数据集进行研究，参与者需学习需要规则发现、假设修正和多步规划的新型视频游戏。我们综合评估模型在游戏表现、匹配人类学习行为及预测任务期间脑活动的能力，比较前沿大型推理模型（LRMs）与无模型及基于模型的深度强化学习代理，以及基于贝叶斯理论的代理。结果发现，前沿LRMs在游戏探索阶段最贴近人类行为模式，并在皮层及皮层下区域的脑活动预测上比两种强化学习方法高出一个数量级，且经排列控制后结果稳健。通过针对性干预，我们进一步表明，脑部一致性反映的是模型在上下文中的游戏状态表征，而非其下游规划或推理过程。本研究确立了LRMs作为人类在复杂自然环境中学习与决策的有力计算模型。项目页面（含交互式回放）：https://botcs.github.io/reason-to-play/
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether modern large reasoning models (LRMs) can replicate human learning and decision-making in complex, novel environments by analyzing gameplay data paired with fMRI recordings. Participants learned unfamiliar video games requiring rule discovery and multi-step planning, and models—including frontier LRMs, model-free and model-based RL agents, and a Bayesian theory-based agent—were evaluated on gameplay performance, behavioral similarity, and brain activity prediction. Results show that frontier LRMs most closely mimic human learning behavior and predict brain activity across cortical and subcortical regions with significantly greater accuracy than RL agents, even under permutation controls. Crucially, brain alignment correlates with the model’s in-context representation of game states rather than its planning processes, suggesting LRMs capture human-like cognitive mechanisms.
为探究现代AI是否能在复杂环境中模拟人类学习能力，本研究通过结合人类游戏行为与fMRI数据，评估前沿大型推理模型（LRMs）与强化学习模型在游戏学习中的表现。参与者需在新游戏中发现规则并进行多步规划，模型评估涵盖游戏表现、行为相似性及脑活动预测。结果表明，LRMs在游戏学习阶段的行为模式最接近人类，且在皮层与皮层下区域的脑活动预测准确率远超强化学习模型，该优势经置换检验验证。进一步分析显示，脑活动一致性源于模型对游戏状态的上下文表征，而非下游规划过程。
Multi-environment Invariance Learning with Missing Data
Authors: Yiran Jia, Jelena Bradic
First: 2026-01-12T06:30:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:04:26+00:00
Comments: Added co-author
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning models that can handle distribution shifts is a key challenge in domain generalization. Invariance learning, an approach that focuses on identifying features invariant across environments, improves model generalization by capturing stable relationships, which may represent causal effects when the data distribution is encoded within a structural equation model (SEM) and satisfies modularity conditions. This has led to a growing body of work that builds on invariance learning, leveraging the inherent heterogeneity across environments to develop methods that provide causal explanations while enhancing robust prediction. However, in many practical scenarios, obtaining complete outcome data from each environment is challenging due to the high cost or complexity of data collection. This limitation in available data hinders the development of models that fully leverage environmental heterogeneity, making it crucial to address missing outcomes to improve both causal insights and robust prediction. In this work, we derive an estimator from the invariance objective under missing outcomes. We establish non-asymptotic guarantees on variable selection property and $\ell_2$ error convergence rates, which are influenced by the proportion of missing data and the quality of imputation models across environments. We evaluate the performance of the new estimator through extensive simulations and demonstrate its application using the UCI Bike Sharing dataset to predict the count of bike rentals. The results show that despite relying on a biased imputation model, the estimator is efficient and achieves lower prediction error, provided the bias is within a reasonable range.
中文标题/摘要
标题：缺失数据下的多环境不变性学习
学习能够处理分布偏移的模型是领域泛化中的关键挑战。不变性学习通过识别跨环境不变的特征，提升模型泛化能力，其捕捉的稳定关系在数据分布符合结构方程模型（SEM）且满足模块化条件时，可能代表因果效应。这推动了大量基于不变性学习的研究，利用环境间的内在异质性开发兼具因果解释力与鲁棒预测能力的方法。然而，在许多实际场景中，由于数据收集成本高或复杂，难以从每个环境中获得完整的结局数据。这一数据限制阻碍了充分利用环境异质性的模型开发，因此必须解决缺失结局问题，以提升因果洞察力和预测鲁棒性。本文在缺失结局条件下，从不变性目标推导出估计量，并建立了变量选择性质和ℓ₂误差收敛率的非渐近保证，其性能受缺失数据比例及跨环境插补模型质量的影响。通过大量模拟评估新估计量性能，并以UCI共享单车数据集预测租车次数，结果表明：即使依赖有偏的插补模型，只要偏差在合理范围内，该估计量仍具高效性并实现更低的预测误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of building models robust to distribution shifts across environments, this work addresses the practical limitation of missing outcome data in multi-environment settings. It derives a novel estimator based on invariance learning principles, designed to handle missing data while preserving causal interpretability. The method provides theoretical guarantees on variable selection and $\ell_2$ error convergence, which depend on missing data proportion and imputation quality. Experimental results on simulations and the UCI Bike Sharing dataset show that even with a biased imputation model, the estimator remains efficient and achieves lower prediction error as long as the bias is within a reasonable range.
为了解决域泛化中分布偏移带来的挑战以及实际场景中环境间数据缺失的问题，本文提出了一种基于不变性的估计方法，该方法在数据不完整时仍能有效工作。方法从不变性目标出发，结合插补模型处理缺失值，并在理论上证明了变量选择能力和$\ell_2$误差收敛率，其性能依赖于缺失比例和插补质量。实验结果表明，即使使用有偏的插补模型，该估计器仍能获得比基线更低的预测误差，前提是偏差控制在合理范围内。
LineRides: Line-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Bicycle Robot Stunts
Authors: Seungeun Rho, Shamel Fahmi, Jeonghwan Kim, Arianna Ilvonen, Sehoon Ha, Gabriel Nelson
First: 2026-05-06T16:43:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:02:40+00:00
Comments: Published in IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing reward functions for agile robotic maneuvers in reinforcement learning remains difficult, and demonstration-based approaches often require reference motions that are unavailable for novel platforms or extreme stunts. We present LineRides, a line-guided learning framework that enables a custom bicycle robot to acquire diverse, commandable stunt behaviors from a user-provided spatial guideline and sparse key-orientations, without demonstrations or explicit timing. LineRides handles physically infeasible guidelines using a tracking margin that permits controlled deviation, resolves temporal ambiguity by measuring progress via traveled distance along the guideline, and disambiguates motion details through position- and sequence-based key-orientations. We evaluate LineRides on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV) and show that the policy trained with our methods supports seamless transitions between normal driving and stunt execution, enabling five distinct stunts on command: MiniHop, LargeHop, ThreePointTurn, Backflip, and DriftTurn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LineRides：基于轨迹引导的强化学习用于自行车机器人特技
在强化学习中为敏捷机器人动作设计奖励函数仍具挑战性，基于示范的方法常需参考运动，而新平台或极端特技往往缺乏此类示范。我们提出LineRides，一种轨迹引导学习框架，使定制自行车机器人仅通过用户提供的空间轨迹和稀疏关键姿态，即可习得多样且可控的特技行为，无需示范或显式时间控制。LineRides通过跟踪裕度处理物理不可行轨迹，允许可控偏差；通过沿轨迹行驶距离衡量进度以解决时间模糊性；并通过基于位置和序列的关键姿态区分运动细节。我们在Ultra Mobility Vehicle（UMV）上评估LineRides，结果表明所训练策略支持正常驾驶与特技执行间的无缝切换，可按指令执行五种特技：MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip和DriftTurn。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of designing reward functions for agile robotic stunts and the lack of available demonstrations for novel maneuvers, LineRides introduces a line-guided reinforcement learning framework that enables a bicycle robot to learn diverse, commandable stunts using only a user-provided spatial guideline and sparse key-orientations. The method handles physically infeasible paths via a tracking margin, resolves timing ambiguity by measuring progress through distance traveled along the guideline, and disambiguates motion details using position- and sequence-based key-orientations. Experimental results on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle demonstrate that the learned policy supports smooth transitions between normal driving and stunts, successfully executing five distinct stunts—MiniHop, LargeHop, ThreePointTurn, Backflip, and DriftTurn—on command.
为了解决敏捷机器人动作中奖励函数设计困难及新型平台或极限特技缺乏演示数据的问题，LineRides提出一种基于线引导的强化学习框架，仅需用户提供的空间引导线和稀疏关键姿态，即可使自定义自行车机器人学习多样且可指令的特技动作，无需演示或精确时间控制。该方法通过跟踪容差处理物理上不可行的路径，通过沿引导线行驶距离衡量进度以消除时间歧义，并利用位置与序列关键姿态区分运动细节。在Ultra Mobility Vehicle上的实验表明，所训练策略支持正常驾驶与特技之间的无缝切换，成功实现了五种可指令特技：MiniHop、LargeHop、ThreePointTurn、Backflip和DriftTurn。
Learning CLI Agents with Structured Action Credit under Selective Observation
Authors: Haoyang Su, Ying Wen
First: 2026-05-08T17:02:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:02:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Command line interface (CLI) agents are emerging as a practical paradigm for agent-computer interaction over evolving filesystems, executable command line programs, and online execution feedback. Recent work has used reinforcement learning (RL) to learn these interaction abilities from verifiable task feedback, yet few methods exploit the native structured attributes of CLI actions as learning signals. Beyond this underused action structure, CLI learning also couples two bottlenecks for coding agents. First, the agent must identify task-relevant evidence in a large codebase from partial observations. Second, sparse terminal rewards must be assigned to the actions that shape a long multi-turn trajectory. We study these bottlenecks through shell-driven information extraction and file editing tasks. For selective observation, we introduce $σ$-Reveal, an inference-time mechanism that selects token-budgeted context for the same CLI. For credit assignment, we propose Action Advantage Assignment ($\mathrm{A}^3$), a native agentic RL method that preserves the algorithmic complexity of standard agentic RL. $\mathrm{A}^3$ constructs turn-level advantages from episode-level relative feedback, abstract syntax tree (AST) based action sub-chain residuals, and tree-level trajectory margins. To further evaluate this problem setting, we construct ShellOps, a verifiable dataset suite covering CLI tasks in repository environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在选择性观察下通过结构化动作信用学习CLI代理
命令行界面（CLI）代理正成为一种实用范式，用于在不断演变的文件系统、可执行命令行程序和在线执行反馈环境中实现人机交互。近期研究已利用强化学习（RL）从可验证的任务反馈中学习这些交互能力，但很少有方法利用CLI动作的原生结构化属性作为学习信号。除了这种未被充分利用的动作结构外，CLI学习还耦合了编码代理的两个瓶颈：首先，代理必须从部分观察中识别大型代码库中的任务相关证据；其次，必须为塑造长多轮轨迹的动作分配稀疏的终端奖励。我们通过shell驱动的信息提取和文件编辑任务研究这些瓶颈。针对选择性观察，我们引入σ-Reveal，一种推理时机制，为同一CLI选择令牌预算的上下文。针对信用分配，我们提出动作优势分配（A³），一种原生代理RL方法，保持标准代理RL的算法复杂度。A³从回合级优势构建，基于回合级相对反馈、抽象语法树（AST）动作子链残差和树级轨迹边际。为进一步评估该问题设置，我们构建了ShellOps，一个覆盖仓库环境中CLI任务的可验证数据集套件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve CLI agent learning through structured action signals and handle sparse rewards under partial observations, the paper proposes σ-Reveal for selective context selection and A³ for credit assignment using AST-based residuals and trajectory margins. Experiments on ShellOps, a new verifiable CLI task dataset, show that these methods significantly enhance learning efficiency and performance compared to baselines, particularly in multi-turn shell interactions and file editing tasks.
为提升CLI代理在部分观察和稀疏奖励下的学习能力，本文提出σ-Reveal用于选择性上下文提取，以及A³用于动作信用分配，后者通过结合回合反馈、AST动作残差和轨迹边际计算回合级优势，保留标准代理RL的复杂度。实验在新构建的可验证CLI任务数据集ShellOps上进行，结果显示所提方法在shell信息提取与文件编辑任务中显著优于基线模型，验证了其在部分观察与稀疏奖励环境下的高效学习能力。
Position: Mechanistic Interpretability Must Disclose Identification Assumptions for Causal Claims
Authors: Zezheng Lin, Fengming Liu
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-05-08T17:01:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:01:55+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures. Submitted to NeurIPS 2026 (Position Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanistic interpretability papers increasingly use causal vocabulary: circuits, mediators, causal abstraction, monosemanticity. Such claims require explicit identification assumptions. A purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands finds no dedicated identification-assumptions section and a recurring pattern: validation metrics such as faithfulness, completeness, monosemanticity, alignment, or ablation effects are reported as causal support without stating the assumptions that make them identifying. A two-human-coder audit on $n=30$ reproduces the direction of the main finding: dedicated identification sections are absent, and validation-metric substitution is common, though exact Dim B/D counts are coding-rule sensitive. The paper proposes a disclosure norm: state whether the claim is causal, name the identification strategy, enumerate assumptions, stress at least one, and explain how conclusions shift if assumptions fail. Validation is not identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机制可解释性必须披露因果主张的识别假设
机制可解释性论文日益使用因果术语：电路、中介变量、因果抽象、单义性。此类主张需明确识别假设。对四类方法学框架下10篇论文的针对性审查发现，无专门的识别假设章节，且普遍存在模式：将忠实性、完整性、单义性、对齐度或消融效应等验证指标报告为因果支持，却未说明使这些指标成为识别依据的假设。对30篇论文的双人编码审查复现了主要发现：无专门识别假设章节，验证指标替代现象普遍，但精确的Dim B/D计数受编码规则影响。论文提出披露规范：说明主张是否为因果，命名识别策略，列举假设，至少强调一项，并解释假设失效时结论如何变化。验证不是识别。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of causal terminology in mechanistic interpretability research, this paper investigates whether such claims are properly grounded in identification assumptions. Through a purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands, it finds that none explicitly state identification assumptions, and validation metrics like faithfulness or ablation are often presented as causal evidence without clarifying the underlying assumptions. A follow-up audit of 30 papers by two human coders confirms this pattern, though exact counts vary with coding rules. The paper proposes a disclosure norm requiring authors to declare if claims are causal, name their identification strategy, list assumptions, highlight at least one, and explain how conclusions change if assumptions fail, emphasizing that validation alone does not constitute identification.
本文批评机制可解释性研究中日益增多的因果术语使用，指出此类主张常缺乏明确的识别假设。通过对十篇跨四种方法学流派的论文进行定向审计，作者发现没有专门章节说明这些假设，且常将忠实性、完整性等验证指标作为因果证据，而未说明其背后的假设。对30篇论文的两人编码员复核确认了这一模式，尽管具体计数因编码规则而异。论文提出一项披露规范，要求研究者明确标注因果主张、命名识别策略、列出关键假设、突出至少一个，并解释若假设失效结论将如何变化，强调验证本身不等于识别。
Abductive Reasoning with Probabilistic Commonsense
Authors: Joseph Cotnareanu, Chiara Roverato, Han Zhou, Didier Chetelat, Yingxue Zhang, Mark Coates
Venue: Proceedings of the International Conference on Machine Learning, 2026
