First: 2026-05-08T17:01:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T17:01:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent efforts to improve the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have focused on integrating formal logic solvers within neurosymbolic frameworks. A key challenge is that formal solvers lack commonsense world knowledge, preventing them from making reasoning steps that humans find obvious. Prior methods address this by using LLMs to supply missing commonsense assumptions, but these approaches implicitly assume universal agreement on such commonsense facts. In reality, commonsense beliefs vary across individuals. We propose a probabilistic framework for abductive commonsense reasoning that explicitly models this variation, aiming to determine whether most people would judge a statement as true or false. We introduce Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense (PACS), a novel algorithm that uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample proofs as observations of individuals' distinct commonsense beliefs, and aggregates conclusions across these samples. Empirically, PACS outperforms chain-of-thought reasoning, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率常识的溯因推理
近期提升大型语言模型（LLM）推理能力的努力集中于在神经符号框架中整合形式逻辑求解器。关键挑战在于，形式求解器缺乏常识世界知识，无法完成人类认为显而易见的推理步骤。此前方法通过使用LLM提供缺失的常识假设来解决此问题，但这些方法隐式假设常识事实具有普遍共识。实际上，常识信念因人而异。我们提出一种概率框架，显式建模这种差异，旨在判断多数人是否认为某陈述为真或假。我们引入了概率溯因常识（PACS）算法，该算法利用LLM和形式求解器采样证明作为个体不同常识信念的观察，并对这些样本的结论进行聚合。实证结果表明，PACS在多个基准测试中优于链式思维推理、先前的神经符号方法及基于搜索的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between formal logic solvers' lack of commonsense knowledge and human reasoning, this work proposes PACS, a probabilistic framework that models individual variation in commonsense beliefs. PACS uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample diverse proof paths representing different people’s assumptions, then aggregates results to infer what most people would judge as true or false. Experiments show PACS consistently outperforms chain-of-thought, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.
为解决形式化求解器缺乏常识知识与人类推理能力之间的差距，本文提出PACS框架，通过结合大语言模型与形式化求解器采样不同个体的推理路径，显式建模常识信念的个体差异，并聚合结果以判断多数人对某陈述的判断。实验表明，PACS在多个基准测试中显著优于链式思维、先前神经符号方法及搜索类方法。
Hot Wire 5D+: Evaluating Cognitive and Motor Trade-offs of Visual Feedback for 5D Augmented Reality Trajectories
Authors: Christian Masuhr, Julian Koch, Arne Wendt, Thorsten Schüppstuhl
First: 2026-05-08T16:59:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:59:41+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures. Preprint. Includes supplementary appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Augmented Reality (AR) is increasingly utilized to guide users through complex spatial tasks in domains such as manufacturing, non-destructive testing, and surgery. These applications often require strict compliance with 5D+ trajectories using rotation-symmetric tools (3D position, 2D orientation, and movement speed). However, the sensori-motor baselines of untrained users during these multidimensional tracing tasks, along with the cognitive-motor trade-offs induced by varying visual feedback paradigms, remain underexplored. We present a controlled within-subjects user study (N=30) evaluating three distinct AR UI concepts for trajectory guidance, both with and without explicit orientation constraints. We analyzed spatial, orientational, and speed compliance based on the internal AR tracking, which was validated against a high-precision external optical tracking system to rule out hardware drift. By segmenting the execution into transient and steady-state phases and applying Aligned Rank Transform (ART) ANOVA, we isolated the interaction effects between visual design and task complexity. Alongside subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, SUS), our results establish conservative performance baselines for novice users performing freehand 5D trajectory following. We reveal orientation-induced cognitive-motor trade-offs and identify mitigating UI synergies. Ultimately, we provide empirical baselines and actionable design guidelines for developing effective AR guidance systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：热丝5D+：评估5D增强现实轨迹中视觉反馈的认知与运动权衡
增强现实（AR）正越来越多地用于指导用户在制造、无损检测和手术等领域的复杂空间任务。这些应用通常需要使用旋转对称工具严格遵循5D+轨迹（3D位置、2D方向和运动速度）。然而，未经训练的用户在这些多维轨迹任务中的感觉运动基线，以及不同视觉反馈范式引发的认知-运动权衡，仍缺乏深入研究。我们开展了一项受控的被试内用户研究（N=30），评估三种不同的AR用户界面概念对轨迹引导的效果，包括有无明确方向约束的情况。我们基于内部AR跟踪数据（经高精度外部光学跟踪系统验证，排除硬件漂移）分析了空间、方向和速度的合规性。通过将执行过程划分为瞬态和稳态阶段，并应用对齐秩变换（ART）ANOVA，我们分离了视觉设计与任务复杂度之间的交互效应。结合主观指标（NASA-TLX、SUS），我们的结果为新手用户进行自由手5D轨迹跟随建立了保守性能基线，揭示了方向引发的认知-运动权衡，并识别了缓解该问题的UI协同策略。最终，我们提供了实证基线和可操作的设计指南，以开发有效的AR引导系统。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how visual feedback in augmented reality affects cognitive and motor performance during 5D trajectory tracing tasks, which involve 3D position, 2D orientation, and speed control. Motivated by the lack of empirical baselines for untrained users in complex spatial domains like surgery or manufacturing, the researchers conducted a within-subjects experiment with 30 participants evaluating three AR UI designs under varying orientation constraints. Using high-precision external tracking to validate internal AR data, they segmented task execution into transient and steady-state phases and applied Aligned Rank Transform ANOVA to isolate visual design by task complexity interactions. Results showed significant cognitive-motor trade-offs induced by orientation constraints, with subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, SUS) corroborating performance degradation. The study establishes conservative performance baselines for novice users and identifies UI design synergies that reduce cognitive load and improve motor compliance.
该研究探讨了增强现实中的视觉反馈如何影响用户在5D轨迹追踪任务中的认知与运动表现，该任务涉及3D位置、2D方向和速度控制。鉴于缺乏对未训练用户在复杂AR应用中的基准数据，研究人员对30名参与者进行了组内实验，评估三种AR界面设计在不同方向约束下的表现。通过高精度外部跟踪系统校准内部AR数据，将任务划分为瞬态与稳态阶段，并采用对齐秩变换ANOVA分析视觉设计与任务复杂度的交互效应。结果表明，方向约束引发显著的认知-运动权衡，并识别出可缓解性能损耗的界面协同设计。结合主观评价与客观合规数据，研究确立了新手用户的性能基准，并为AR引导系统的设计提供了实证依据与可操作指南。
Interpreting Reinforcement Learning Agents with Susceptibilities
Authors: Chris Elliott, Einar Urdshals, David Quarel, Daniel Murfet
First: 2026-05-08T16:59:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:59:38+00:00
Comments: 55 pages, comments welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Susceptibilities are a technique for neural network interpretability that studies the response of posterior expectation values of observables to perturbations of the loss. We generalize this construction to the setting of the regret in deep reinforcement learning and investigate the utility of susceptibilities in a simple gridworld model that nevertheless exhibits non-trivial stagewise development. We argue that susceptibilities reveal internal features of the development of the model in parameter space that one cannot detect purely by studying the development of the learned policy. We validate these results with activation-steering, and discuss the framework's extension to RLHF post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用易感性解释强化学习代理
易感性是一种用于神经网络可解释性的技术，研究可观测量的后验期望值对损失扰动的响应。我们将这一构造推广到深度强化学习中的遗憾度场景，并在具有非平凡阶段式发展的简单网格世界模型中研究易感性的实用性。我们认为，易感性揭示了模型在参数空间中发展过程中的内部特征，而这些特征仅通过研究学习到的策略发展是无法检测到的。我们通过激活引导验证了这些结果，并讨论了该框架在RLHF后训练中的扩展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how deep reinforcement learning agents evolve during training, this paper extends the concept of susceptibilities—originally used for neural network interpretability—to analyze how model parameters respond to perturbations in regret. Applied to a simple gridworld environment, the method reveals internal dynamics in parameter space that are invisible when only observing policy changes. Experimental results confirm that susceptibilities capture meaningful developmental stages of the agent, and the framework is shown to be extendable to RLHF post-training settings.
为超越仅观察策略行为的局限，本文将用于神经网络可解释性的‘易感性’概念扩展至强化学习中的后悔值分析，通过研究可观测量后验期望对损失扰动的响应，揭示参数空间中模型发展的内部特征。在简单网格世界实验中，易感性揭示了非平凡的阶段性动态，经激活引导验证，表明该框架可拓展用于RLHF后训练阶段的理解。
Penalty-Based First-Order Methods for Bilevel Optimization with Minimax and Constrained Lower-Level Problems
Authors: Yiyang Shen, Yutian He, Weiran Wang, Qihang Lin
First: 2026-05-08T16:59:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:59:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a class of bilevel optimization problems in which both the upper- and lower-level problems have minimax structures. This setting captures a broad range of emerging applications. Despite the extensive literature on bilevel optimization and minimax optimization separately, existing methods mainly focus on bilevel optimization with lower-level minimization problems, often under strong convexity assumptions, and are not directly applicable to the minimax lower-level setting considered here. To address this gap, we develop penalty-based first-order methods for bilevel minimax optimization without requiring strong convexity of the lower-level problem. In the deterministic setting, we establish that the proposed method finds an $ε$-KKT point with $\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity. We further show that bilevel problems with convex constrained lower-level minimization can be reformulated as special cases of our framework via Lagrangian duality, leading to an $\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ complexity bound that improves upon the existing $\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})$ result. Finally, we extend our approach to the stochastic setting, where only stochastic gradient oracles are available, and prove that the proposed stochastic method finds a nearly $ε$-KKT point with $\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})$ oracle complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于惩罚的用于具有极小极大和约束下层问题的双层优化的一阶方法
我们研究一类双层优化问题，其中上下层问题均具有极小极大结构。该设定涵盖了广泛新兴应用。尽管双层优化和极小极大优化各自已有大量文献，现有方法主要关注下层为最小化问题的双层优化，常假设强凸性，且不直接适用于本文考虑的极小极大下层设定。为弥补这一不足，我们开发了无需下层问题强凸性假设的基于惩罚的一阶方法。在确定性设定下，所提方法以\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})的算子复杂度找到ε-KKT点。我们进一步证明，具有凸约束下层最小化问题的双层问题可通过拉格朗日对偶转化为本框架的特例，从而获得优于现有\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})结果的\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})复杂度界。最后，我们将方法扩展至随机设定，仅使用随机梯度算子，证明所提随机方法以\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})算子复杂度找到近似ε-KKT点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to solve bilevel optimization problems where both levels involve minimax structures—common in emerging applications—the paper proposes penalty-based first-order methods that do not require strong convexity of the lower level. In the deterministic setting, the method achieves an ε-KKT point with \tilde{O}(ε^{-4}) oracle complexity, and for convex-constrained lower-level problems, it leverages Lagrangian duality to achieve the same complexity, improving upon the existing \tilde{O}(ε^{-7}) bound. In the stochastic setting, the method finds a nearly ε-KKT point with \tilde{O}(ε^{-9}) oracle complexity using only stochastic gradients.
为解决上下层均含极小极大结构的双层优化问题（常见于新兴应用），本文提出无需下层强凸性假设的惩罚型一阶方法。在确定性情形下，该方法可达到ε-KKT点，复杂度为\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})；对于凸约束下层问题，通过拉格朗日对偶可将其纳入框架，同样达到\tilde{O}(ε^{-4})复杂度，优于现有\tilde{O}(ε^{-7})结果。在随机情形下，仅依赖随机梯度，方法可找到近似ε-KKT点，复杂度为\tilde{O}(ε^{-9})。
STEPS: A Temporal Smooth Error Propagation Solver on the Manifolds for Test-Time Adaptation in Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Jiaqi Liu, Yifan Ouyang, Zhifei Song, Sim Kuan Goh, Ashwaq Qasem
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-05-08T16:58:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:58:10+00:00
Comments: 9 pages main text, appendix included. 7 figures. Submitted to NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) aims to improve time series forecasting under distribution shifts by using limited observations revealed during inference. However, forecasting TTA must operate in a source-free online setting, where the adaptation signal is short, temporally correlated, and potentially noisy. Existing methods can therefore suffer from weak identifiability, error accumulation, and unstable long-horizon corrections when the revealed prefix is sparse or contaminated. To address these issues, we propose STEPS, a Smooth Temporal Error Propagation Solver for TTA in time-series forecasting. STEPS reformulates forecasting TTA as a Dirichlet Boundary Value Problem on a temporal manifold, where the revealed prefix error serves as the boundary condition for the unknown future error field. Then, STEPS solves a smooth and bounded correction field in prediction space: a Local Solver propagates prefix errors under temporal smoothness, a Global Solver retrieves stable cross-window error memory and Spatiotemporal Manifold Fusion (SMF) integrates both solutions into the final correction. Across six standard benchmarks and four frozen backbones, STEPS achieves an average relative MSE reduction of 26.82% over the zero-shot backbone, exceeding the strongest compared TTA baseline by 12.77%. Additional sparse prefix and contamination tests confirm the robustness of STEPS under limited and noisy prefixes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STEPS：面向时间序列预测测试时自适应的时间平滑误差传播求解器
测试时自适应（TTA）旨在通过推理期间揭示的有限观测值，在分布偏移下提升时间序列预测性能。然而，TTA预测必须在无源在线环境中运行，其中适应信号短暂、具有时间相关性且可能含噪。现有方法因此在揭示前缀稀疏或受污染时易出现弱可识别性、误差累积和长时程修正不稳定等问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出STEPS（时间平滑误差传播求解器），将TTA预测重新建模为时间流形上的狄利克雷边界值问题，其中揭示的前缀误差作为未知未来误差场的边界条件。随后，STEPS在预测空间中求解一个平滑且有界的修正场：局部求解器在时间平滑约束下传播前缀误差，全局求解器提取稳定跨窗口误差记忆，时空流形融合（SMF）将二者整合为最终修正。在六个标准基准和四个冻结骨干网络上，STEPS相较零样本骨干网络平均相对MSE降低26.82%，超越最强对比TTA基线12.77%。额外的稀疏前缀与污染测试进一步验证了STEPS在有限且含噪前缀下的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of adapting time series forecasts under distribution shifts with limited, noisy, and temporally correlated test-time observations, STEPS reformulates TTA as a Dirichlet boundary value problem on a temporal manifold, where the revealed prefix error acts as a boundary condition. It then solves for a smooth, bounded correction field using a Local Solver that propagates errors under temporal smoothness, a Global Solver that captures stable cross-window error memory, and Spatiotemporal Manifold Fusion (SMF) that integrates both. Experiments across six benchmarks and four frozen backbones show STEPS reduces average relative MSE by 26.82% over zero-shot baselines, outperforming the strongest baseline by 12.77%, with robustness confirmed under sparse and contaminated prefix conditions.
为解决在分布偏移下利用有限、噪声且时序相关的测试时观测进行时间序列预测自适应的挑战，STEPS将TTA建模为时间流形上的狄利克雷边界值问题，其中已知前缀误差作为未知未来误差场的边界条件。通过局部求解器在时序平滑约束下传播误差、全局求解器稳定捕获跨窗口误差记忆，以及时空流形融合（SMF）整合二者，生成平滑有界的修正场。在六个基准和四个冻结模型上，STEPS相较零样本基线平均相对MSE降低26.82%，超越最强基线12.77%，并在稀疏与污染前缀条件下验证了其鲁棒性。
SphereVAD: Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection via Geodesic Inference on the Unit Hypersphere
Authors: Chao Huang, Penfei Wei, Wei Wang, Jie Wen, Zhihua Wang, Li Shen, Wenqi Ren, Xiaochun Cao
First: 2026-05-08T16:57:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:57:38+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 25 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to automatically identify events that deviate from normal patterns in untrimmed surveillance videos. Existing methods universally depend on large-scale annotations or task-specific training procedures, severely limiting their rapid deployment to novel scenes. We observe that intermediate-layer features of pre-trained multimodal large language models (MLLMs) already encode rich anomaly semantics, yet existing approaches rely on the language output pathway and fail to exploit the geometric discriminability latent in these representations. Based on this finding, we propose SphereVAD, a fully training-free, zero-shot VAD framework that recasts anomaly discrimination as von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-ratio geodesic inference on the unit hypersphere, unleashing latent discriminability through principled geometric reasoning rather than learning new representations. Specifically, SphereVAD first applies Frechet mean centering to unfold feature distributions and eliminate domain biases, then employs Holistic Scene Attention (HSA) to reinforce feature consistency using cross-video priors, and finally performs vMF-guided Spherical Geodesic Pulling (SGP) to align ambiguous segments with directional prototypes on the spherical manifold. This training-free pipeline requires only minimal synthetic images for calibration. SphereVAD establishes new state-of-the-art results among training-free approaches on three major benchmarks and remains competitive with fully supervised baselines. Code will be available upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SphereVAD：通过单位超球面上的测地推断实现无训练视频异常检测
视频异常检测（VAD）旨在自动识别未剪辑监控视频中偏离正常模式的事件。现有方法普遍依赖大规模标注或特定任务训练流程，严重限制其在新场景中的快速部署。我们观察到，预训练多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的中间层特征已编码丰富异常语义，但现有方法依赖语言输出路径，未能利用这些表示中蕴含的几何判别性。基于此发现，我们提出SphereVAD，一个完全无训练、零样本的VAD框架，将异常判别重构为单位超球面上的von Mises-Fisher（vMF）似然比测地推断，通过原理性的几何推理释放潜在判别性，而非学习新表示。具体而言，SphereVAD首先应用Fréchet均值中心化展开特征分布并消除领域偏差，然后采用全局场景注意力（HSA）利用跨视频先验强化特征一致性，最后执行vMF引导的球面测地拉伸（SGP），在球面流形上将模糊片段与方向原型对齐。该无训练流程仅需少量合成图像进行校准。SphereVAD在三大主流基准上建立了新的无训练方法最先进结果，并保持与全监督基线的竞争力。代码将在录用后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for rapid deployment of video anomaly detection without training or annotations, SphereVAD leverages pre-trained multimodal large language models to extract geometrically discriminative features from intermediate layers, bypassing reliance on language outputs. It formulates anomaly detection as a geodesic inference problem on the unit hypersphere using von Mises-Fisher likelihood ratios, enabling zero-shot detection through principled geometric reasoning. The method first centers feature distributions via Fréchet mean, then enhances consistency with cross-video priors via Holistic Scene Attention, and finally aligns ambiguous segments with directional prototypes using Spherical Geodesic Pulling. Experiments show SphereVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance among training-free methods on three benchmarks while remaining competitive with supervised baselines, requiring only minimal synthetic calibration data.
为实现无需标注数据和训练的视频异常检测快速部署，SphereVAD 利用预训练多模态大语言模型中间层特征中固有的异常语义，但现有方法未能有效挖掘其几何判别性。该框架将异常判别重构为单位超球面上的 von Mises-Fisher 概率比的测地线推理问题，通过几何原理而非学习新表示释放判别能力。具体流程包括：首先通过 Fréchet 均值中心化消除域偏置，再利用全局场景注意力增强跨视频特征一致性，最后通过球面测地线拉伸将模糊片段对齐方向原型。实验表明，SphereVAD 在三个主流基准上达到训练免模型最优性能，且与监督方法相当，仅需少量合成图像进行校准。
How Do Language Models Compose Functions?
Authors: Apoorv Khandelwal, Ellie Pavlick
First: 2025-10-02T05:21:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:56:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While large language models (LLMs) appear to be increasingly capable of solving compositional tasks, it is an open question whether they do so using compositional mechanisms. In this work, we investigate how feedforward LLMs solve two-hop factual recall tasks, which can be expressed compositionally as $g(f(x))$. We first confirm that modern LLMs continue to suffer from the "compositionality gap", i.e. their ability to compute both $z = f(x)$ and $y = g(z)$ does not entail their ability to compute the composition $y = g(f(x))$. We then decode residual stream representations and identify two processing mechanisms: one which solves tasks $\textit{compositionally}$, computing $f(x)$ along the way to $g(f(x))$, and one which solves them $\textit{directly}$, without any detectable signature of the intermediate variable $f(x)$. Finally, we find that embedding space geometry is strongly related to which mechanism is employed, where the idiomatic mechanism is dominant when tasks are represented by translations from $x$ to $g(f(x))$ in the embedding spaces. We fully release our data and code at: https://github.com/apoorvkh/composing-functions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型如何组合函数？
尽管大型语言模型（LLM）在解决组合任务方面似乎越来越有能力，但它们是否使用组合机制来实现这一点仍是一个开放性问题。在本研究中，我们探讨了前馈LLM如何解决两跳事实回忆任务，该任务可组合表达为$g(f(x))$。我们首先确认，现代LLM仍存在“组合性缺口”，即它们能够分别计算$z = f(x)$和$y = g(z)$的能力，并不意味着它们能计算组合结果$y = g(f(x))$。接着，我们解码残差流表示，识别出两种处理机制：一种是组合性地解决问题，在计算$g(f(x))$的过程中同时计算$f(x)$；另一种是直接解决问题，无任何可检测到的中间变量$f(x)$的痕迹。最后，我们发现嵌入空间几何结构与所采用机制密切相关，当任务在嵌入空间中以从$x$到$g(f(x))$的翻译形式表示时，惯用机制占主导地位。我们的数据和代码已完全开源：https://github.com/apoorvkh/composing-functions。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) solve compositional tasks like g(f(x)) using true compositional mechanisms or instead rely on non-compositional strategies. The authors analyze feedforward LLMs on two-hop factual recall tasks and confirm a 'compositionality gap'—the inability to compute g(f(x)) even when f(x) and g(z) can be computed separately. By decoding residual stream representations, they identify two distinct mechanisms: one compositional, where f(x) is computed as an intermediate step, and one direct, where no trace of the intermediate variable is detectable. Their findings show that embedding space geometry determines which mechanism dominates, with idiomatic representations favoring the compositional approach.
本研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）是否能通过真正的组合机制解决如 g(f(x)) 的组合任务，发现它们常无法跨越‘组合性缺口’——即即使能分别计算 f(x) 和 g(z)，也无法组合计算 y = g(f(x))。研究人员分析了前馈 LLM 的残差流表示，识别出两种解题机制：一种是组合式机制，会先计算中间变量 f(x) 再应用 g；另一种是直接式机制，不显式保留中间变量。研究发现嵌入空间的几何结构显著影响所采用的机制，当任务在嵌入空间中表现为从输入到输出的直接映射时，组合式机制占主导地位。
Rethinking Dense Optical Flow without Test-Time Scaling
Authors: Praroop Chanda, Suryansh Kumar
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-08T16:56:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:56:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at CVPR 2026; ViSCALE Workshop. Draft info: 10 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent progress in dense optical flow has been driven by increasingly complex architectures and multi-step refinement for test-time scaling. While these approaches achieve strong benchmark performance, they also require substantial computation during inference. This raises a fundamental question: Is scaling test-time computation the only way to improve dense optical flow accuracy? We argue that it is not. Instead, powerful visual semantic and geometric priors encoded in modern foundation models can reduce, if not overcome, the need for computationally expensive iterative refinement at test-time. In this paper, we present a framework that estimates dense optical flow in a single forward pass, leveraging pretrained foundation representations, while avoiding iterative refinement and additional inference-time computation, thus offering an alternative to test-time scaling. Our method extracts visual semantic features from a frozen DINO-v2 backbone and combines them with geometric cues from a monocular depth foundation model. We fuse these complementary priors into a unified representation and apply a global matching formulation to estimate dense correspondences without recurrent updates or test-time optimization. Despite avoiding iterative refinement, our approach achieves strong cross-dataset generalization across challenging benchmarks. On Sintel Final, we obtain 2.81 EPE without refinement, significantly improving over state-of-the-art (SOTA) SEA-RAFT under comparable training conditions and outperforming RAFT, GMFlow (without refinement), and recent FlowSeek in the same setting. These results suggest that strong foundation priors can substitute for test-time scaling, offering a computationally efficient alternative to refinement-heavy pipelines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需测试时缩放的稠密光流重新思考
近期稠密光流的进展主要依赖日益复杂的架构和多步细化以实现测试时缩放。虽然这些方法在基准测试中表现强劲，但推理时计算量巨大。这引发了一个根本性问题：提高稠密光流精度是否必须依赖测试时计算量的扩展？我们认为并非如此。相反，现代基础模型中编码的强大视觉语义和几何先验，可以减少甚至替代测试时昂贵的迭代细化。本文提出一种框架，通过预训练基础表示，在单次前向传播中估计稠密光流，避免迭代细化和额外推理时计算，从而提供测试时缩放的替代方案。我们的方法从冻结的DINO-v2主干中提取视觉语义特征，并结合单目深度基础模型的几何线索，将这些互补先验融合为统一表示，采用全局匹配形式估计稠密对应关系，无需递归更新或测试时优化。尽管避免迭代细化，我们的方法在多个挑战性基准上实现了强大的跨数据集泛化能力。在Sintel Final数据集上，我们无需细化即获得2.81的EPE，显著优于在相似训练条件下最先进的SEA-RAFT，且超越RAFT、GMFlow（无细化）和近期FlowSeek的表现。这些结果表明，强大的基础先验可替代测试时缩放，提供一种计算效率更高的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of test-time scaling in dense optical flow methods, this work investigates whether visual and geometric priors from foundation models can replace iterative refinement. The method uses a single forward pass, combining semantic features from a frozen DINO-v2 backbone with depth cues from a monocular depth model, then fuses them into a unified representation for global matching. Experimental results show that without any refinement, the approach achieves 2.81 EPE on Sintel Final, outperforming state-of-the-art methods like SEA-RAFT, RAFT, GMFlow (without refinement), and FlowSeek under comparable training conditions, demonstrating that strong foundation priors can effectively substitute for computationally expensive test-time scaling.
为解决密集光流方法中测试时缩放带来的高计算开销问题，本文提出一种无需迭代优化的单次前向传播框架，利用预训练视觉与几何先验（来自冻结的DINO-v2特征和单目深度模型）直接估计光流。通过融合互补先验信息并采用全局匹配形式，该方法在不增加推理计算的前提下实现高精度。实验表明，在Sintel Final数据集上其EPE为2.81，显著优于SEA-RAFT、RAFT及FlowSeek等当前主流方法，证明强基础模型先验可替代测试时缩放，提供更高效的光流估计方案。
Graph-Structured Hyperdimensional Computing for Data-Efficient and Explainable Process-Structure-Property Prediction
Authors: Jingzhan Ge, Ajeeth Vellore, Ajinkya Palwe, Ahsan Khan, David Gorsich, Matthew P. Castanier, SeungYeon Kang, Farhad Imani
First: 2026-05-08T16:55:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:55:44+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 18 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multiphoton photoreduction enables high-fidelity fabrication of complex 3D microstructures, yet reliable process-structure-property (PSP) prediction remains difficult because the available data are sparse, heterogeneous, and interaction-dominated. In this regime, conventional feature-vector models are statistically underdetermined, making them prone to spurious correlations, poor regime transfer, and unstable post hoc explanations, whereas mechanistic pipelines depend on calibrated submodels that are rarely available during early process development. We present PSP-HDC, a graph-structured hyperdimensional computing framework that encodes a directed PSP graph as an internal prior for representation, inference, and explanation. A trainable scalar-to-hypervector encoder learns parameter-specific embeddings on a fixed hyperdimensional basis to accommodate heterogeneous scales and noise. Sample representations are then composed through graph-aligned binding and bundling along directed PSP dependencies, and prediction is performed by associative-memory retrieval against class prototypes. Because the same prototype memories support both decision making and attribution, PSP-HDC provides intrinsic explanations at the parameter, group, and within-group levels, while memory alignment and separation quantify prototype formation during training. On sheet-resistance regime prediction for the 3D platform, PSP-HDC achieves an accuracy of 0.910 +/- 0.077 over 1000 random splits and 0.896 under process-fold generalization, outperforming strong baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图结构高维计算的数据高效且可解释的工艺-结构-性能预测
多光子光还原可实现复杂三维微结构的高保真制造，但可靠的工艺-结构-性能（PSP）预测仍困难，因为现有数据稀疏、异构且以相互作用为主导。在此情境下，传统特征向量模型统计上欠定，易产生虚假相关性、领域迁移能力差及事后解释不稳定；而机理流程依赖校准的子模型，但在工艺早期开发阶段往往难以获得。本文提出PSP-HDC，一种图结构高维计算框架，将定向PSP图编码为表示、推理与解释的内部先验。可训练的标量到超向量编码器在固定高维基上学习参数特异性嵌入，以适应异构尺度与噪声。样本表示通过图对齐绑定与沿定向PSP依赖的捆绑组合，预测则通过与类别原型的关联记忆检索完成。由于同一原型记忆同时支持决策与归因，PSP-HDC在参数、组及组内层级提供内生解释，而记忆对齐与分离量化训练期间原型形成过程。在三维平台的片阻域预测中，PSP-HDC在1000次随机划分下准确率达0.910±0.077，在工艺折叠泛化下达0.896，优于强基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting process-structure-property relationships in multiphoton photoreduction where data are sparse and interaction-dominated, the authors propose PSP-HDC, a graph-structured hyperdimensional computing framework. It encodes a directed PSP graph as an internal prior, using a trainable scalar-to-hypervector encoder to generate parameter-specific embeddings on a fixed hyperdimensional basis. Representations are composed via graph-aligned binding and bundling, and predictions are made through associative memory retrieval against class prototypes. The framework enables intrinsic explanations at multiple levels and quantifies prototype formation. Experiments on sheet-resistance prediction show PSP-HDC achieves 0.910 ± 0.077 accuracy across 1000 random splits and 0.896 under process-fold generalization, outperforming strong baselines.
为了解决多光子光还原中因数据稀疏、交互主导而导致的工艺-结构-性能预测难题，作者提出了PSP-HDC框架，该框架基于图结构超维计算。它将定向的PSP图作为内部先验，通过可训练的标量到超向量编码器在固定超维基上生成参数特定嵌入。这些嵌入通过图对齐绑定与捆绑组合，再通过与类别原型的关联记忆检索进行预测。该框架支持参数、组内及组间层级的内生解释，并量化训练过程中原型的形成。在薄膜电阻预测实验中，PSP-HDC在1000次随机分割上达到0.910±0.077的准确率，在工艺折叠泛化测试中达到0.896，优于多个强基线方法。
Valence-Arousal Subspace in LLMs: Circular Emotion Geometry and Multi-Behavioral Control
Authors: Lihao Sun, Lewen Yan, Xiaoya Lu, Andrew Lee, Jie Zhang, Jing Shao
First: 2026-04-03T16:08:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:54:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show that emotion vectors in LLMs are organized by a two-dimensional valence-arousal (VA) subspace exhibiting circular geometry. Through principal component decomposition and ridge regression, we recover meaningful VA axes underlying emotion steering vectors whose projections correlate with human affect ratings across 44,728 words. Steering along these axes produces monotonic control over the affective properties of generated text, and further affords bidirectional control over multiple downstream behaviors (refusal and sycophancy) from a single subspace. These effects replicate across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B. We propose lexical mediation to explain why these effects and prior emotionally framed controls work: refusal and compliance tokens occupy distinct VA regions, and VA steering directly modulates their emission probabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的效价-唤醒子空间：圆形情绪几何与多行为控制
我们发现大语言模型中的情绪向量由一个二维效价-唤醒（VA）子空间组织，该子空间呈现圆形几何结构。通过主成分分解和岭回归，我们恢复了支撑情绪引导向量的有意义VA轴，其投影与44,728个词的人类情感评分相关。沿这些轴引导可实现生成文本情感属性的单调控制，并进一步从单一子空间实现对多个下游行为（拒绝与奉承）的双向控制。这些效果在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B模型中均得到验证。我们提出词汇中介机制以解释这些效应及先前情绪框架控制为何有效：拒绝与顺从词元占据不同的VA区域，VA引导可直接调节其发射概率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and control emotional expression in large language models, this study identifies a two-dimensional valence-arousal subspace within LLMs that exhibits circular geometry. Using principal component decomposition and ridge regression, the authors recover emotion axes that correlate with human affect ratings across 44,728 words. Steering along these axes enables monotonic control over generated text’s affective properties and bidirectional control over behaviors like refusal and sycophancy from a single subspace, consistently observed across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B. The authors attribute this behavior to lexical mediation, where refusal and compliance tokens reside in distinct VA regions, and VA steering directly modulates their emission probabilities.
为理解并控制大语言模型中的情绪表达，本研究发现LLM内部存在一个呈圆形几何结构的二维情绪子空间（效价-唤醒度）。通过主成分分解与岭回归，作者恢复了与44,728个词人类情绪评分相关的效价-唤醒轴。沿这些轴引导可实现生成文本情感属性的单调控制，并从同一子空间双向调控拒绝与奉承等下游行为，效果在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-14B上均得到验证。作者提出“词汇中介”解释：拒绝与顺从词汇位于不同VA区域，VA引导直接调节其生成概率。
Persistent Visual Memory: Sustaining Perception for Deep Generation in LVLMs
Authors: Siyuan Huang, Xiaoye Qu, Yafu Li, Tong Zhu, Zefeng He, Muxin Fu, Daizong Liu, Wei-Long Zheng, Yu Cheng
First: 2026-05-01T17:54:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:52:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While autoregressive Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate remarkable proficiency in multimodal tasks, they face a "Visual Signal Dilution" phenomenon, where the accumulation of textual history expands the attention partition function, causing visual attention to decay inversely with generated sequence length. To counteract this, we propose Persistent Visual Memory (PVM), a lightweight learnable module designed to strengthen sustained, on-demand access to visual evidence. Integrated as a parallel branch alongside the Feed-Forward Network (FFN) in LVLMs, PVM establishes a distance-agnostic retrieval pathway that directly provides visual embeddings for enhanced visual perception, thereby structurally mitigating the signal suppression inherent to deep generation. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL models demonstrate that PVM brings notable improvements with negligible parameter overhead, delivering consistent average accuracy gains across both 4B and 8B scales, particularly in complex reasoning tasks that demand persistent visual perception. Furthermore, in-depth analysis reveals that PVM shows improved robustness in longer generations and accelerates internal prediction convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：持久视觉记忆：在LVLMs中维持感知以实现深度生成
尽管自回归大视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在多模态任务中表现出卓越能力，但它们面临“视觉信号稀释”现象，即文本历史的累积扩大了注意力分配函数，导致视觉注意力随生成序列长度增加而衰减。为应对这一问题，我们提出持久视觉记忆（PVM），一种轻量级可学习模块，旨在增强对视觉证据的持续、按需访问。PVM作为并行分支集成于LVLMs的前馈网络（FFN）中，建立了一条与距离无关的检索路径，直接提供视觉嵌入以增强视觉感知，从而在结构上缓解深度生成固有的信号抑制问题。在Qwen3-VL模型上的大量实验表明，PVM带来显著性能提升且参数开销极小，在4B和8B规模下均实现稳定平均准确率提升，尤其在需要持续视觉感知的复杂推理任务中表现突出。此外，深入分析显示，PVM在长序列生成中表现出更强鲁棒性，并加速内部预测收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the visual signal dilution issue in autoregressive LVLMs, where visual attention weakens as sequence length increases, the paper introduces Persistent Visual Memory (PVM), a lightweight learnable module integrated in parallel with the FFN. PVM enables on-demand retrieval of visual embeddings without relying on distance metrics, directly enhancing visual perception during deep generation. Experiments on Qwen3-VL models show PVM delivers consistent accuracy gains across 4B and 8B scales, especially in complex reasoning tasks, with minimal parameter overhead and improved robustness in longer generations.
为解决自回归LVLM中因文本历史累积导致的视觉信号稀释问题，本文提出轻量级可学习模块Persistent Visual Memory（PVM），并将其与FFN并行集成。PVM通过无距离依赖的视觉嵌入检索机制，增强深度生成过程中的视觉感知，结构性地缓解注意力衰减。在Qwen3-VL模型上的实验表明，PVM在4B和8B规模下均带来稳定准确率提升，尤其在复杂推理任务中表现突出，且参数开销极小，同时在长生成中更具鲁棒性。
Upper Generalization Bounds for Neural Oscillators
Authors: Zifeng Huang, Konstantin M. Zuev, Yong Xia, Michael Beer
First: 2026-03-10T14:47:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:52:43+00:00
Comments: This manuscript contains 33 pages with 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural oscillators that originate from second-order ordinary differential equations (ODEs) have shown competitive performance in learning mappings between dynamic loads and responses of complex nonlinear structural systems. Despite this empirical success, theoretically quantifying the generalization capacities of their neural network architectures remains undeveloped. In this study, the neural oscillator consisting of a second-order ODE followed by a multilayer perceptron (MLP) is considered. Its upper probably approximately correct (PAC) generalization bound for approximating causal and uniformly continuous operators between continuous temporal function spaces and that for approximating the uniformly asymptotically incrementally stable second-order dynamical systems are derived by leveraging the Rademacher complexity framework. These bounds are further extended to the squared Wasserstein-1 distances between the probability measures of quantities of interest calculated from target causal operators and the corresponding learned neural oscillators. The theoretical results show that the estimation errors grow polynomially with respect to both MLP sizes and the time length, thereby avoiding the curse of parametric complexity. Furthermore, the derived error bounds demonstrate that constraining the Lipschitz constants of the MLPs via loss function regularization can improve the generalization ability of the neural oscillator. Numerical studies considering a Bouc-Wen nonlinear system under stochastic seismic excitation validates the theoretically predicted power laws of the estimation errors with respect to the sample size and time length, and confirms the effectiveness of constraining MLPs' matrix and vector norms in enhancing the performance of the neural oscillator under limited training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经振荡器的上泛化界
源自二阶常微分方程（ODE）的神经振荡器在学习复杂非线性结构系统动态载荷与响应之间的映射关系方面表现出竞争力。尽管有此经验成功，其神经网络架构的泛化能力的理论量化仍属未开发领域。本研究考虑由二阶ODE后接多层感知机（MLP）构成的神经振荡器，利用Rademacher复杂度框架推导了其近似因果且一致连续算子在连续时间函数空间之间的上概率近似正确（PAC）泛化界，以及近似均匀渐近增量稳定的二阶动力系统的上泛化界。这些界进一步扩展至目标因果算子与相应学习神经振荡器所计算的量纲概率测度之间的平方Wasserstein-1距离。理论结果表明，估计误差随MLP规模和时间长度呈多项式增长，从而避免了参数复杂度的诅咒。此外，所推导的误差界表明，通过损失函数正则化约束MLP的Lipschitz常数可提升神经振荡器的泛化能力。数值研究考虑在随机地震激励下的Bouc-Wen非线性系统，验证了估计误差相对于样本量和时间长度的理论幂律，并证实了在训练数据有限情况下，约束MLP矩阵和向量范数可提升神经振荡器性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of neural oscillators in modeling dynamic nonlinear systems, this study aims to theoretically quantify their generalization capacity. The method derives upper PAC bounds for neural oscillators composed of second-order ODEs followed by MLPs, using Rademacher complexity to analyze approximation errors for causal operators and stable dynamical systems. The bounds are further extended to Wasserstein-1 distances between probability measures of system responses. Experimental results on a Bouc-Wen system under stochastic excitation confirm polynomial error growth with respect to MLP size and time length, avoiding the curse of dimensionality, and demonstrate that regularizing MLP Lipschitz constants via loss functions improves generalization, especially under limited data.
鉴于神经振荡器在建模动态非线性系统中的实证成功，本研究旨在从理论上量化其泛化能力。方法基于Rademacher复杂度框架，推导出由二阶ODE与多层感知机（MLP）组成的神经振荡器在逼近因果算子和渐近稳定动力系统时的上界PAC泛化误差，并进一步扩展至目标算子与学习模型响应概率测度间的Wasserstein距离。结果表明，误差随MLP规模和时间长度呈多项式增长，避免了参数复杂度诅咒；同时，通过损失函数正则化约束MLP的Lipschitz常数可提升泛化能力。数值实验在随机地震激励下的Bouc-Wen系统上验证了误差的幂律缩放规律，并证实了在训练数据有限时，约束MLP矩阵与向量范数能有效提升性能。
Bayesian Sensitivity of Causal Inference Estimators under Evidence-Based Priors
Authors: Nikita Dhawan, Daniel Shen, Leonardo Cotta, Chris J. Maddison
First: 2026-05-08T16:50:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:50:05+00:00
Comments: TMLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal inference, especially in observational studies, relies on untestable assumptions about the true data-generating process. Sensitivity analysis helps us determine how robust our conclusions are when we alter these underlying assumptions. Existing frameworks for sensitivity analysis are concerned with worst-case changes in assumptions. In this work, we argue that using such pessimistic criteria can often become uninformative or lead to conclusions contradicting our prior knowledge about the world. To demonstrate this claim, we generalize the recent s-value framework (Gupta & Rothenhäusler, 2023) to estimate the sensitivity of three different common assumptions in causal inference. Empirically, we find that, indeed, worst-case conclusions about sensitivity can rely on unrealistic changes in the data-generating process. To overcome this, we extend the s-value framework with a new sensitivity analysis criterion: Bayesian Sensitivity Value (BSV), which computes the expected sensitivity of an estimate to assumption violations under priors constructed from real-world evidence. We use Monte Carlo approximations to estimate this quantity and illustrate its applicability in an observational study on the effect of diabetes treatments on weight loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于证据先验的因果推断估计量的贝叶斯敏感性分析
因果推断，尤其是在观察性研究中，依赖于关于真实数据生成过程的不可检验假设。敏感性分析有助于我们判断在改变这些基本假设时，我们的结论有多稳健。现有敏感性分析框架关注最坏情况下的假设变化。本文我们认为，使用这种悲观标准往往会导致无信息或与我们对世界的先验知识相矛盾的结论。为证明这一观点，我们将近期的s值框架（Gupta & Rothenhäusler, 2023）推广，以估计因果推断中三种常见假设的敏感性。实证发现，最坏情况下的敏感性结论确实依赖于对数据生成过程不现实的改变。为解决此问题，我们扩展s值框架，引入一种新的敏感性分析标准：贝叶斯敏感值（BSV），该值在基于现实世界证据构建的先验下，计算估计量对假设违反的预期敏感性。我们使用蒙特卡洛近似估计该量，并在一项关于糖尿病治疗对体重减轻影响的观察性研究中展示了其适用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of worst-case sensitivity analysis in causal inference, which can produce uninformative or counterintuitive results, this work introduces the Bayesian Sensitivity Value (BSV) as a more realistic alternative. BSV estimates the expected sensitivity of causal estimators to assumption violations under priors informed by real-world evidence, extending the s-value framework. Empirical evaluations on diabetes treatment data show that worst-case sensitivity measures often rely on implausible assumption shifts, while BSV yields more interpretable and evidence-aligned sensitivity assessments.
为解决现有最坏情况敏感性分析在因果推断中可能产生无意义或与现实矛盾的结论，本文提出一种基于贝叶斯的敏感性评估方法。该方法扩展了s值框架，引入“贝叶斯敏感值”（BSV），在由现实证据构建的先验下计算因果估计量对假设违反的期望敏感性。在糖尿病治疗的实证研究中发现，最坏情况敏感性分析依赖于不现实的假设扰动，而BSV能提供更贴近现实且可解释的敏感性评估结果。
A Theory of Saddle Escape in Deep Nonlinear Networks
Authors: Divit Rawal, Michael R. DeWeese
First: 2026-05-02T06:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:49:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In deep networks with small initialization, training exhibits long plateaus separated by sharp feature-acquisition transitions. Whereas shallow nonlinear networks and deep linear networks are well studied, extending these analyses to deep nonlinear networks remains challenging. We derive an exact identity for the imbalance of Frobenius norms of layer weight matrices that holds for any smooth activation and any differentiable loss and use this to classify activation functions into four universality classes. On the permutation-symmetric submanifold, the identity combines with an approximate balance law to reduce the full matrix flow to a scalar ODE, giving a critical-depth escape time law $τ_\star = Θ(\varepsilon^{-(r-2)})$ governed by the number $r$ of layers at the bottleneck scale rather than the total depth $L$. We find that this same $r-2$ exponent is recovered under He-normal initialization with $r$ bottleneck layers rescaled by $\varepsilon$, where the symmetry manifold is preserved by the flow but not attracting. We find close agreement between our theory and numerical simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度非线性网络中鞍点逃逸的理论
在小初始化的深度网络中，训练过程表现出由尖锐特征获取过渡分隔的长平台期。尽管浅层非线性网络和深层线性网络已被充分研究，将这些分析扩展到深层非线性网络仍具挑战性。我们推导出适用于任意平滑激活函数和任意可微损失函数的层权重矩阵Frobenius范数不平衡的精确恒等式，并据此将激活函数划分为四类普适性类别。在置换对称子流形上，该恒等式结合近似平衡律，将完整的矩阵流简化为标量常微分方程，得到临界深度逃逸时间定律τ⋆ = Θ(ε^{-(r-2)})，其由瓶颈尺度上的层数r决定，而非总深度L。我们发现，在He正则初始化下，当瓶颈层按ε缩放时，相同r-2指数亦可重现，此时对称流形在流中保持不变但非吸引子。我们的理论与数值模拟结果高度吻合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that deep nonlinear networks with small initialization exhibit long plateaus interrupted by sharp transitions during training, this work develops a theoretical framework to analyze saddle escape dynamics. It derives an exact identity for the imbalance of Frobenius norms of layer weight matrices, applicable to any smooth activation and differentiable loss, and classifies activations into four universality classes. On the permutation-symmetric submanifold, this identity, combined with an approximate balance law, reduces the full matrix dynamics to a scalar ordinary differential equation, yielding a critical-depth escape time law τ⋆ = Θ(ε^{-(r-2)}) where r is the number of bottleneck layers, not the total depth L. The theory is validated numerically under He-normal initialization, where the same r-2 exponent emerges even when the symmetry manifold is preserved but not attracting.
针对小初始化下深度非线性网络训练中出现的长平台与陡峭特征获取跃迁现象，本文构建理论框架分析鞍点逃逸动力学。推导出适用于任意光滑激活函数与可微损失函数的层权重矩阵Frobenius范数不平衡精确恒等式，并将激活函数划分为四类普适性类别。在置换对称子流形上，该恒等式结合近似平衡律，将完整矩阵流简化为标量常微分方程，得到临界深度逃逸时间 τ⋆ = Θ(ε^{-(r-2)})，其中 r 为瓶颈层数量而非总深度 L。在He正则初始化下，数值模拟结果与理论预测高度吻合，证实该 r-2 标度律，即使对称子流形非吸引子也成立。
Tool Calling is Linearly Readable and Steerable in Language Models
Authors: Zekun Wu, Ze Wang, Seonglae Cho, Yufei Yang, Adriano Koshiyama, Sahan Bulathwela, Maria Perez-Ortiz
First: 2026-05-08T16:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:47:08+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 6 figures, 7 tables. Manuscript under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When a tool-calling agent picks the wrong tool, the failure is invisible until execution: the email gets sent, the meeting gets missed. Probing 12 instruction-tuned models across Gemma 3, Qwen 3, Qwen 2.5, and Llama 3.1 (270M to 27B), we find the identity of the chosen tool is linearly readable and steerable inside the model. Adding the mean-difference between two tools' average internal activations switches which tool the model selects at 77-100% accuracy on name-only single-turn prompts (93-100% at 4B+), and the JSON arguments that follow autoregressively match the new tool's schema, so flipping the name is enough. The same per-tool means also flag likely errors before they happen: on Gemma 3 12B and 27B, queries where the gap between the top-1 and top-2 tool is smallest produce 14-21x more wrong calls than queries with the largest gap. The causal effect concentrates along one direction, the row of the output layer that produces the target tool's first token: a unit vector along it at matched magnitude already reaches 93-100%, while what is left over leaves the choice almost untouched. Activation patching localises this to a small set of mid- and late-layer attention heads, and a within-topic probe across 14 same-domain $τ$-bench airline tools reaches top-1 61-89% across five 4B-14B models, ruling out the reading that we are just moving the model along a topic axis. Even base models encode the right tool before they can emit it: cosine readout from the internal state recovers 69-82% on BFCL while base generation reaches only 2-10%, suggesting pretraining forms the representation and instruction tuning later wires it to the output. We measure tool identity selection and JSON schema correctness in single-turn fixed-menu settings; multi-turn agentic transfer is more fragile and is discussed in Limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中的工具调用具有线性可读性和可操控性
当工具调用代理选择错误工具时，失败在执行前是不可见的：邮件被发送，会议被错过。我们对Gemma 3、Qwen 3、Qwen 2.5和Llama 3.1（270M至27B）中的12个指令调优模型进行探测，发现所选工具的身份在模型内部具有线性可读性和可操控性。在仅含工具名称的单轮提示中，通过添加两个工具平均内部激活值的均值差，可使模型选择特定工具的准确率高达77-100%（4B+模型为93-100%），随后自回归生成的JSON参数符合新工具的结构，因此仅需切换工具名称即可。同一工具的均值差异还能在错误发生前预警：在Gemma 3 12B和27B模型中，工具选择前1和前2的激活值差距最小的查询，错误调用次数比差距最大的查询高出14-21倍。因果效应集中于输出层中生成目标工具首个token的行：沿该方向的单位向量已可达到93-100%准确率，其余部分对选择几乎无影响。激活修补将该效应定位到少量中层和深层注意力头，且在14个同领域τ基准航空工具上，跨五种4B-14B模型的同一主题探测达到前1准确率61-89%，排除了我们只是沿主题轴移动模型的解释。即使基础模型在输出前也已编码正确工具：在BFCL数据集上，从内部状态的余弦读取准确率69-82%，而基础生成仅达2-10%，表明预训练形成表征，指令调优后期将其连接至输出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and control tool selection in language models, this study probes 12 instruction-tuned models across multiple architectures to show that tool identity is linearly readable and steerable via internal activations. By manipulating the mean difference between tool-specific activation vectors, the model’s tool choice can be flipped with 77–100% accuracy on single-turn prompts, and the resulting JSON arguments automatically conform to the new tool’s schema. The effect is localized to specific attention heads and concentrated in the output layer’s direction for the target tool’s first token, with cosine readouts from internal states recovering tool identity even before generation. Base models also encode tool identity pre-training, with instruction tuning later aligning it to output. The method reveals tool selection is not merely topic-based, and errors are predictable based on activation gaps between top tools.
为理解并控制语言模型中的工具选择，该研究对12个指令微调模型进行探测，发现工具身份可通过内部激活线性读取与操控。通过调整不同工具激活向量的均值差，可将模型选择的工具切换，准确率达77-100%，且生成的JSON参数自动符合新工具的结构。研究还发现，工具选择错误与工具激活值差距小相关，因果效应集中在输出层某一方向，且可定位到特定注意力头。即使基础模型在生成前也已编码正确工具身份，表明预训练形成表征，指令微调才将其连接至输出。
SynthForensics: Benchmarking and Evaluating People-Centric Synthetic Video Deepfakes
Authors: Roberto Leotta, Salvatore Alfio Sambataro, Claudio Vittorio Ragaglia, Mirko Casu, Yuri Petralia, Francesco Guarnera, Luca Guarnera, Sebastiano Battiato
First: 2026-02-04T16:47:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:47:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern T2V/I2V generators synthesize people increasingly hard to distinguish from authentic footage, while current evaluation suites lag: legacy benchmarks target manipulation-based forgeries, and recent synthetic-video benchmarks prioritize scale over realistic human depiction. We introduce SynthForensics, a people-centric benchmark of $20{,}445$ videos from 8 T2V and 7 I2V open-source generators, paired-source from FF++/DFD reals, two-stage human-validated, in four compression versions with full metadata. In our paired-comparison human study, raters prefer SynthForensics in $71$--$77\%$ of head-to-head comparisons against each of nine existing synthetic-video benchmarks, while facial-quality metrics fall within the FF++/DFD baseline range. Across 15 detectors and three protocols, face-based methods drop $13$--$55$ AUC points (mean $27$) from FF++ to SynthForensics and a further $23$ under aggressive compression; fine-tuning closes the gap at a backward cost on legacy benchmarks; training from scratch shows synthetic and manipulation features largely disjoint for most detectors. We release dataset, pipeline, and code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SynthForensics：面向以人为中心的合成视频深度伪造的基准与评估
现代T2V/I2V生成器合成的人物视频越来越难以与真实视频区分，而当前评估套件却滞后：传统基准针对基于篡改的伪造，近期合成视频基准则优先考虑规模而非真实人物表现。我们引入SynthForensics，这是一个包含8个T2V和7个I2V开源生成器的20,445个视频的以人为中心的基准，与FF++/DFD真实视频配对，经两阶段人工验证，提供四种压缩版本及完整元数据。在我们的配对比较人工实验中，评测者在与九个现有合成视频基准的逐对比较中，71%–77%的场景更偏好SynthForensics，而面部质量指标处于FF++/DFD基线范围内。在15个检测器和三种协议下，基于面部的方法从FF++到SynthForensics的AUC下降13–55点（平均27），在高压缩下进一步下降23点；微调可缩小差距，但以牺牲传统基准性能为代价；从零训练显示，多数检测器中合成与篡改特征基本分离。我们发布数据集、流程及代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing realism of synthetic videos and the inadequacy of existing benchmarks for evaluating human-like deepfakes, the authors introduce SynthForensics, a new benchmark comprising 20,445 videos from 15 open-source T2V and I2V generators, paired with real footage from FF++/DFD and validated by human raters across four compression levels. In human preference tests, SynthForensics outperformed nine existing benchmarks in 71–77% of comparisons, while facial quality metrics remained comparable to real videos. Across 15 detectors and three evaluation protocols, face-based detectors saw a 13–55 point drop in AUC (mean 27) when moving from FF++ to SynthForensics, worsening further under compression; fine-tuning mitigated this gap but at the cost of reduced performance on legacy benchmarks, while training from scratch revealed synthetic and manipulation features are largely separable for most detectors.
鉴于当前合成视频生成器日益逼真，而现有评测基准难以有效评估人像类深度伪造，作者构建了SynthForensics数据集，包含20445个由15个开源T2V/I2V模型生成的视频，与FF++/DFD真实视频配对，并在四种压缩级别下进行双阶段人工验证。在人类偏好测试中，SynthForensics在71%至77%的对比中胜过九个现有基准；面部质量指标与真实视频相当。实验表明，15个检测器在SynthForensics上的AUC平均下降27点（范围13–55），压缩后进一步下降23点；微调可缓解该差距但牺牲了对旧基准的性能，而从零训练显示多数检测器对合成与篡改特征区分度较高。
Evaluation of an Actuated Spine in Agile Quadruped Locomotion
Authors: Nico Bohlinger, Piotr Kicki, Davide Tateo, Krzysztof Walas, Jan Peters
First: 2026-05-08T16:45:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:45:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The spine plays a crucial role in the dynamic locomotion of quadrupedal animals, improving the stability, speed, and efficiency of their gait, especially for fast-paced and highly agile movements. Therefore, the spine is also a promising and natural way to extend the capabilities of quadruped robots. This paper empirically investigates the benefits of an actuated spine for learning agile quadruped locomotion. We evaluate whether the use of the spine brings benefits in terms of high-speed running, climbing stairs, climbing high-angle slopes, hurdling, and crawling scenarios. We conducted an empirical study in MuJoCo simulation using the Silver Badger robot from MAB Robotics with an actuated 1-DOF spine in the sagittal plane. The obtained results show that the use of the spine provides the robot with increased agility and allows it to overcome higher stairs, steeper slopes, higher obstacles, and smaller passages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主动脊柱在敏捷四足运动中的评估
脊柱在四足动物的动态运动中起着关键作用，能提升其步态的稳定性、速度和效率，尤其适用于高速和高度敏捷的运动。因此，脊柱也是扩展四足机器人能力的一种有前景且自然的方式。本文实证研究了主动脊柱在学习敏捷四足运动中的优势，评估其在高速奔跑、爬楼梯、攀陡坡、跨障碍和爬行等场景中的性能提升。我们在MuJoCo仿真环境中使用MAB Robotics的Silver Badger机器人，配备单自由度矢状面主动脊柱进行实验。结果表明，主动脊柱使机器人具备更高的敏捷性，能够跨越更高的楼梯、更陡的斜坡、更高的障碍物和更窄的通道。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the biomechanical advantages of spinal motion in agile quadrupedal animals, this study investigates whether an actuated spine enhances the locomotion capabilities of a quadruped robot. Using a 1-DOF sagittal-plane spine on the Silver Badger robot within the MuJoCo simulation environment, the researchers evaluated performance across high-speed running, stair climbing, steep slope traversal, hurdling, and crawling. Experimental results demonstrate that the actuated spine significantly improves agility, enabling the robot to clear higher obstacles, climb steeper inclines, and navigate narrower passages compared to a spineless baseline.
鉴于脊柱在敏捷四足动物动态运动中的关键作用，本研究探讨了主动脊柱是否能提升四足机器人运动能力。在MuJoCo仿真环境中，研究人员在Silver Badger机器人上搭载单自由度矢状面主动脊柱，评估其在高速奔跑、爬楼梯、攀陡坡、跨障碍和爬行等场景下的表现。实验结果表明，主动脊柱显著增强了机器人的敏捷性，使其能够跨越更高障碍、攀爬更陡斜坡，并通过更狭窄的通道，相比无脊柱基线表现更优。
Uncertainty Quantification for Cardiac Shape Reconstruction with Deep Signed Distance Functions via MCMC methods
Authors: Jan Verhülsdonk, Thomas Grandits, Francisco Sahli Costabal, Thomas Beiert, Simone Pezzuto, Alexander Effland
First: 2026-05-08T16:45:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:45:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Atlas-based approaches allow high-quality, patient-specific shape reconstructions of cardiac anatomy from sparse and/or noisy data such as point clouds. However, these methods are mainly prior-driven, so the impact of uncertainty can be large, limiting their clinical reliability. We propose a probabilistic framework for uncertainty-aware cardiac shape reconstruction that combines Deep Signed Distance Functions (DeepSDFs) with Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. Cardiac geometries are modeled implicitly as zero-level sets of a neural network conditioned on learned latent codes, enabling multi-surface reconstruction of the left and right ventricles. By interpreting the reconstruction loss as a log-likelihood, we perform Bayesian inference in the latent space to obtain both maximum a posteriori (MAP) and posterior-sampled reconstructions. Experiments on a public cardiac dataset show that our approach produces accurate reconstructions and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度有符号距离函数与MCMC方法的心脏形状重建的不确定性量化
基于图谱的方法可从稀疏或噪声点云等数据中实现高质量、患者特异性的心脏解剖形状重建。然而，这些方法主要依赖先验信息，因此不确定性影响较大，限制了其临床可靠性。我们提出一种概率框架，结合深度有符号距离函数（DeepSDF）与马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样，实现不确定性感知的心脏形状重建。心脏几何结构隐式建模为神经网络零等值面，该网络受学习到的潜在编码条件约束，支持左、右心室的多表面重建。通过将重建损失解释为对数似然，我们在潜在空间中进行贝叶斯推断，获得最大后验（MAP）和后验采样重建结果。在公开心脏数据集上的实验表明，该方法可生成精确重建并提供良好校准的不确定性估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable cardiac shape reconstruction from sparse or noisy data, this work introduces a probabilistic method combining Deep Signed Distance Functions with MCMC sampling to quantify uncertainty. Cardiac geometries are modeled as zero-level sets of a neural network conditioned on latent codes, enabling multi-surface reconstruction of both ventricles. By treating the reconstruction loss as a log-likelihood, Bayesian inference is performed in the latent space to generate both MAP and posterior-sampled reconstructions. Experimental results on a public cardiac dataset demonstrate accurate anatomical reconstructions and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, improving clinical reliability over prior-driven atlas-based methods.
为提升稀疏或噪声数据下心脏形状重建的临床可靠性，该研究提出一种结合深度符号距离函数与MCMC采样的概率框架以量化不确定性。方法通过神经网络隐式建模心脏几何结构，基于学习到的潜在编码重建左、右心室。将重建损失视为对数似然，对潜在空间进行贝叶斯推断，生成MAP与后验采样重建结果。在公开心脏数据集上的实验表明，该方法能准确重建解剖结构并提供校准良好的不确定性估计，优于传统先验驱动的配准方法。
Towards Apples to Apples for AI Evaluations: From Real-World Use Cases to Evaluation Scenarios
Authors: Yee-Yin Choong, Kristen Greene, Alice Qian, Meryem Marasli, Ziqi Yang, Sophia Chen, Laura Dabbish, Anand Rao, Hong Shen
First: 2026-05-08T16:44:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:44:55+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI measurement science has a wide variety of methodologies and measurements for comparing AI systems, resulting in what often appear to be "apples-to-oranges" comparisons across AI evaluations. To move toward "apples-to-apples" comparisons in real-world AI evaluations, this work advocates for methodological transparency in evaluation scenarios, operational grounding, and human-centered design (HCD) principles. We propose a repeatable process for transforming high-level use cases to detailed scenarios by eliciting use cases from subject matter experts (SMEs) via a structured AI Use Case Worksheet with six key elements: use case, sector, user (direct and indirect), intended outcomes, expected impacts (positive and negative), and KPIs and metrics. We demonstrate utility of the worksheet and process in the U.S. financial services sector. This paper reports on example high-level AI use cases identified by financial services sector SMEs: cyber defense enablement, developer productivity, financial crime aggregation, suspicious activity report (SAR) filing, credit memo generation, and internal call center support. These AI use cases provided are illustrative of the process and not exhaustive. Central to our work is a three-stage expansion pipeline combining LLM prompting with human reviews to generate 107 scenarios from those use cases elicited from SMEs. This process integrates iterative human reviews at every juncture to ensure operational grounding: for scenario titles and descriptions; for core scenario elements like users, benefits and risks, and metrics; and for scenario narratives and evaluation objectives. Human checkpoints ensure scenarios remain reflective of real-world usage and human needs. We describe a validation rubric to assess scenario quality. By defining key scenario components, this work supports a more consistent and meaningful paradigm for human-centered AI evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向AI评估中的‘苹果对苹果’：从现实应用场景到评估场景
AI测量科学包含多种多样的方法和指标用于比较AI系统，导致不同AI评估之间常出现‘苹果对橙子’的比较。为实现真实世界AI评估中的‘苹果对苹果’比较，本文倡导评估场景中的方法透明性、操作基础性和以人为核心的设计（HCD）原则。我们提出一个可重复的过程，通过结构化的AI应用场景工作表（包含六个关键要素：应用场景、行业、用户（直接与间接）、预期成果、预期影响（正面与负面）及KPI与指标），从领域专家（SME）处获取应用场景，并将其转化为详细评估场景。我们在美国金融服务行业展示了该工作表和流程的实用性。本文报告了金融服务行业SME识别出的若干高阶AI应用场景示例：网络防御赋能、开发者生产力提升、金融犯罪聚合、可疑活动报告（SAR）提交、信用备忘录生成及内部呼叫中心支持。这些AI应用场景仅用于说明流程，并非穷尽所有。本研究的核心是一个三阶段扩展管道，结合大语言模型提示与人工审核，从SME提供的应用场景生成107个详细场景。该过程在每个环节均融入迭代人工审核，以确保操作基础性：包括场景标题与描述、核心要素（如用户、收益与风险、指标）及场景叙事与评估目标。人工检查点确保场景始终反映真实世界使用和人类需求。我们描述了一套评估场景质量的验证标准。通过定义关键场景组件，本研究支持更一致且有意义的人本导向AI评估范式。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of standardized, comparable AI evaluation methods by advocating for methodological transparency, operational grounding, and human-centered design. It introduces a structured AI Use Case Worksheet with six key elements to transform high-level use cases into detailed evaluation scenarios, validated through a three-stage pipeline combining LLM prompting and iterative human reviews. Applied in the U.S. financial services sector, the process generated 107 scenarios from six SME-identified use cases, such as cyber defense and credit memo generation, ensuring real-world relevance through human checkpoints at every stage. The resulting scenarios are evaluated using a validation rubric to ensure consistency and alignment with human needs, supporting more meaningful and comparable AI assessments.
为解决AI评估方法缺乏统一标准、导致比较结果难以对照的问题，本文提出一套以人为中心、可重复的流程，将高层AI应用场景转化为具体、可操作的评估场景。该方法通过结构化问卷从领域专家处收集包含六大要素的用例，再结合大语言模型提示与多轮人工审核，生成107个详细场景。人工审核贯穿整个流程，确保场景在标题、用户角色、影响、指标及叙事等方面贴合真实业务需求。该方法在金融服务业验证，覆盖如网络安全、信用报告等场景，证明结构化的人机协作能构建更一致、有意义的AI评估范式。
Dooly: Configuration-Agnostic, Redundancy-Aware Profiling for LLM Inference Simulation
Authors: Joon Ha Kim, Geon-Woo Kim, Anoop Rachakonda, Daehyeok Kim
First: 2026-05-08T16:44:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:44:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Selecting the optimal LLM inference configuration requires evaluation across hardware, serving engines, attention backends, and model architectures, since no single choice performs best across all workloads. Profile-based simulators are the standard tool, yet they hardcode their operation set to a specific configuration and re-profile every operation from scratch, making exploration prohibitively expensive. This cost stems from a missing structural understanding: every input dimension of each operation is fixed by the model configuration or determined by the incoming request. Many model-configuration values (e.g., head size, layer count) recur across models, so the same operation runs in many configurations; a single sweep over the request-dependent dimensions can serve them all. We present Dooly, which exploits this structure to achieve configuration-agnostic, redundancy-aware profiling. Dooly performs a single inference pass, labels each input dimension with its origin via taint propagation, and selectively profiles only operations absent from its latency database; stateful operations such as attention are isolated by reusing the serving engine's own initialization code, eliminating manual instrumentation. It builds latency regression models based on the database, which becomes a drop-in backend for existing simulators. Across two GPU platforms, three attention backends, and diverse model architectures, Dooly achieves simulation accuracy within 5% MAPE for TTFT and 8% for TPOT while reducing profiling GPU-hours by 56.4% across 12 models compared to the existing profiling approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Dooly：面向LLM推理模拟的配置无关且冗余感知的配置分析
选择最优的LLM推理配置需评估硬件、服务引擎、注意力后端及模型架构，因无单一配置能适用于所有负载。基于配置的模拟器是标准工具，但其操作集硬编码于特定配置，且每次重新配置均需从头重新配置，导致探索成本过高。此成本源于缺乏结构理解：每个操作的输入维度均由模型配置或请求决定。许多模型配置值（如头大小、层数）在不同模型中重复出现，因此同一操作可在多种配置下运行；只需对请求依赖维度进行一次遍历即可覆盖所有操作。我们提出Dooly，利用此结构实现配置无关且冗余感知的配置分析。Dooly仅执行一次推理，通过污点传播标记每个输入维度的来源，并仅对未存在于延迟数据库中的操作进行选择性配置；状态性操作（如注意力）通过复用服务引擎自身的初始化代码进行隔离，无需手动插入代码。Dooly基于数据库构建延迟回归模型，该模型可作为现有模拟器的即插即用后端。在两个GPU平台、三种注意力后端及多种模型架构下，Dooly在TTFT和TPOT上的模拟精度误差分别控制在5%和8%以内，相比现有配置分析方法，对12个模型的GPU小时数节省达56.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of profiling LLM inference configurations across diverse hardware and model setups, Dooly introduces a configuration-agnostic, redundancy-aware profiling method that avoids re-profiling identical operations. It performs a single inference pass, uses taint propagation to track input dimension origins, and profiles only operations not in its latency database, while isolating stateful operations like attention via reuse of the serving engine’s initialization. Experimental results across two GPU platforms, three attention backends, and multiple model architectures show Dooly achieves simulation accuracy within 5% MAPE for TTFT and 8% for TPOT, while reducing profiling GPU-hours by 56.4% across 12 models compared to existing methods.
为降低在不同硬件和模型配置下对大语言模型推理配置进行性能分析的成本，Dooly提出一种配置无关、冗余感知的分析方法，通过污点传播追踪输入维度来源，避免重复分析相同操作。通过单次推理和复用服务引擎初始化代码隔离状态化操作，Dooly仅对未在延迟数据库中的操作进行分析，再基于数据库构建回归模型以支持精确模拟。在两个GPU平台、三种注意力后端及多种模型架构上测试，Dooly在TTFT和TPOT上的预测误差分别控制在5%和8%以内，同时将12个模型的分析GPU时长减少56.4%。
Where's the Plan? Locating Latent Planning in Language Models with Lightweight Mechanistic Interventions
Authors: Nicole Ma, Nick Rui
First: 2026-05-08T16:44:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:44:21+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 20 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study planning site formation in language models -- where internal representations of structurally-constrained future tokens form during the forward pass, and whether they causally drive generation. Using rhyming-couplet completion as a clean test of forward-looking constraint, we apply two lightweight methods (linear probing and activation patching) across Qwen3, Gemma-3, and Llama-3 at more than ten scales. Probing shows that future-rhyme information is linearly decodable at the line boundary, with signal that strengthens with scale in all three families. Activation patching reveals that only Gemma-3-27B causally relies on this encoding, exhibiting a handoff in which the causal driver migrates from the rhyme word to the line boundary around layer 30. Every other model we test conditions on the rhyme word throughout generation, with near-zero causal effect at the line boundary despite strong probe signal. We localize the Gemma-3-27B handoff to five attention heads through two-stage path patching that recover ~90% of the rhyme-routing capacity at the newline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计划在哪里？通过轻量级机制干预定位语言模型中的潜在规划
我们研究语言模型中规划位置的形成——即在前向传播过程中，结构约束的未来词的内部表征如何形成，以及它们是否因果驱动生成。我们以押韵对句补全作为前向约束的干净测试，对Qwen3、Gemma-3和Llama-3在十多个规模上应用两种轻量方法（线性探测和激活修补）。探测显示，未来押韵信息在行边界处可线性解码，且在三个模型家族中随规模增大而增强。激活修补揭示仅Gemma-3-27B依赖此编码，表现出一种交接：因果驱动因素在第30层左右从押韵词迁移到行边界。我们测试的其他模型在整个生成过程中始终依赖押韵词，尽管探测信号强，但在行边界处因果效应接近零。通过两阶段路径修补，我们将Gemma-3-27B的交接定位到五个注意力头，恢复了约90%的押韵路由能力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether language models form and rely on latent planning representations during generation, specifically examining how future-token constraints like rhyming couplets are encoded and causally utilized. Using linear probing and activation patching across Qwen3, Gemma-3, and Llama-3 at multiple scales, the authors find that future-rhyme information is decodable at line boundaries and scales with model size. However, only Gemma-3-27B shows causal dependence on this encoding, with a shift in causal responsibility from the rhyme word to the line boundary around layer 30; other models condition on the rhyme word throughout generation despite strong probe signals. The handoff in Gemma-3-27B is localized to five attention heads using two-stage path patching, recovering approximately 90% of the rhyme-routing capacity.
本研究探讨语言模型在生成过程中是否形成并依赖于潜在的规划表征，尤其关注在结构约束（如押韵对句）下未来词元的编码方式。通过在多个模型（Qwen3、Gemma-3、Llama-3）不同规模上应用线性探测与激活修补方法，作者发现未来押韵信息在行边界处可线性解码，且随模型规模增强。但因果干预显示，仅Gemma-3-27B实际依赖该编码驱动生成，其因果责任在第30层左右从押韵词转移至行边界。其他模型虽在探测中显示强信号，但在生成过程中始终依赖押韵词，行边界处因果效应接近零。Gemma-3-27B的转移机制通过两阶段路径修补定位至五个注意力头，恢复约90%的押韵路由能力。
GLiGuard: Schema-Conditioned Classification for LLM Safeguard
Authors: Urchade Zaratiana, Mary Newhauser, George Hurn-Maloney, Ash Lewis
First: 2026-05-08T16:44:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:44:07+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Ensuring safe, policy-compliant outputs from large language models requires real-time content moderation that can scale across multiple safety dimensions. However, state-of-the-art guardrail models rely on autoregressive decoders with 7B--27B parameters, reformulating what is fundamentally a classification problem as sequential text generation, a design choice that incurs high latency and scales poorly to multi-aspect evaluation. In this work, we introduce \textbf{GLiGuard}, a 0.3B-parameter schema-conditioned bidirectional encoder adapted from GLiNER2 for LLM content moderation. The key idea is to encode task definitions and label semantics directly into the input sequence as structured token schemas, enabling simultaneous evaluation of prompt safety, response safety, refusal detection, 14 fine-grained harm categories, and 11 jailbreak strategies in a single non-autoregressive forward pass. This schema-conditioned design lets supported task and label blocks be composed directly in the input schema at inference time. Across nine established safety benchmarks, GLiGuard achieves F1 scores competitive with 7B--27B decoder-based guards despite being 23--90$\times$ smaller, while delivering up to 16$\times$ higher throughput and 17$\times$ lower latency. These results suggest that compact bidirectional encoders can approach the accuracy of much larger guard models while drastically reducing inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GLiGuard：面向LLM安全防护的模式条件分类
确保大型语言模型输出安全、符合政策要求，需要能够跨多个安全维度扩展的实时内容审核。然而，当前最先进的防护模型依赖于7B至27B参数的自回归解码器，将本质上是分类问题的审核任务重新表述为序列文本生成，这种设计选择导致高延迟且难以扩展至多方面评估。本文提出\textbf{GLiGuard}，一种从GLiNER2迁移而来的0.3B参数模式条件双向编码器，专用于LLM内容审核。核心思想是将任务定义和标签语义直接编码为输入序列中的结构化令牌模式，从而在单次非自回归前向传递中同时评估提示安全、响应安全、拒绝检测、14种细粒度危害类别和11种 jailbreak 策略。这种模式条件设计允许在推理时直接在输入模式中组合支持的任务和标签块。在九个主流安全基准测试中，GLiGuard 尽管参数规模仅为7B至27B解码器模型的23至90倍，却实现了相当的F1分数，同时吞吐量提升最高达16倍，延迟降低最高达17倍。这些结果表明，紧凑的双向编码器可在显著降低推理成本的同时，接近大型防护模型的准确率。代码与模型见 https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiGuard。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency of large autoregressive decoders in real-time LLM content moderation, GLiGuard introduces a 0.3B-parameter bidirectional encoder adapted from GLiNER2, which encodes safety task definitions and label semantics as structured token schemas in the input. This schema-conditioned design enables simultaneous evaluation of multiple safety dimensions—including prompt safety, response safety, refusal detection, 14 harm categories, and 11 jailbreak strategies—in a single non-autoregressive forward pass. Evaluated across nine safety benchmarks, GLiGuard matches or exceeds the F1 scores of 7B–27B decoder-based models while being 23–90 times smaller, achieving up to 16 times higher throughput and 17 times lower latency, demonstrating that compact encoders can deliver competitive safety performance with drastically reduced inference cost.
为解决大语言模型实时内容审核中大型自回归解码器效率低下的问题，本文提出GLiGuard，一种0.3B参数的双向编码器，通过将任务定义和标签语义结构化编码为输入模式，实现对多个安全维度（包括提示安全、响应安全、拒绝检测、14类危害类别和11种越狱策略）的同步评估。在九个安全基准测试中，GLiGuard在F1分数上与7B–27B解码器模型相当，但模型规模小23–90倍，吞吐量高16倍，延迟低17倍。
Susceptibilities and Patterning: A Primer on Linear Response in Bayesian Learning
Authors: Chris Elliott, Daniel Murfet
First: 2026-05-08T16:43:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:43:48+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 3 figures, comments welcome!
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
These notes introduce the theory of susceptibilities as developed in [arXiv:2504.18274, arXiv:2601.12703] for interpreting neural networks. The susceptibility of an observable $φ$ to a data perturbation is defined as a derivative of a posterior expectation, which by the fluctuation--dissipation theorem equals a posterior covariance. Different choices of $φ$ yield different objects: per-sample losses give the influence matrix (the Bayesian influence function of [arXiv:2509.26544]), while component-localized observables give the structural susceptibility matrix that pairs model components with data patterns. The susceptibility matrix is (up to a factor of $nβ$) the Jacobian of the map from data distributions to structural coordinates; its pseudo-inverse provides a linearized solution to the patterning problem of [arXiv:2601.13548]: finding data perturbations that produce a desired structural change. We motivate the theory from its statistical-mechanical foundations, then give a detailed exposition of susceptibilities, their empirical estimators, and their connection to the geometry of the loss landscape.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可变性与模式化：贝叶斯学习中线性响应理论入门
本文档介绍了在[arXiv:2504.18274, arXiv:2601.12703]中发展起来的可变性理论，用于解释神经网络。可观测量φ对数据扰动的可变性定义为后验期望的导数，根据涨落-耗散定理，其等于后验协方差。不同选择的φ会产生不同对象：每样本损失给出影响矩阵（即[arXiv:2509.26544]中的贝叶斯影响函数），而组件局部可观测量则给出结构可变性矩阵，将模型组件与数据模式配对。可变性矩阵（乘以因子nβ）是数据分布到结构坐标映射的雅可比矩阵；其伪逆提供对[arXiv:2601.13548]中模式化问题的线性化解：寻找能产生期望结构变化的数据扰动。我们从统计力学基础出发阐述该理论，随后详细讨论可变性的内容、经验估计量及其与损失景观几何结构的联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how neural networks respond to data perturbations, this work introduces susceptibilities as a linear response framework rooted in Bayesian learning and statistical mechanics. The susceptibility of an observable φ to data perturbations is defined as the derivative of its posterior expectation, which by the fluctuation-dissipation theorem equals the posterior covariance. Different φ choices yield distinct matrices: per-sample losses produce the influence matrix, while localized observables yield the structural susceptibility matrix that maps model components to data patterns. Empirically, the susceptibility matrix approximates the Jacobian of the map from data distributions to structural coordinates, and its pseudo-inverse enables linearized solutions to the patterning problem—finding perturbations that induce desired structural changes in the model.
为理解神经网络对数据扰动的响应，本文引入基于统计物理的可变性理论，将可观测量对数据扰动的导数定义为后验期望的导数，其值等于后验协方差。方法构建两类关键矩阵：基于单样本损失的影响力矩阵和基于组件局部可观测量的结构可变性矩阵，后者将数据模式映射到模型结构变化。实验表明，可变性矩阵的伪逆可实现结构变化的线性化控制，通过最小数据扰动实现期望的模型结构修改，有效解决模式化问题。
Ask Patients with Patience: Enabling LLMs for Human-Centric Medical Dialogue with Grounded Reasoning
Authors: Jiayuan Zhu, Jiazhen Pan, Yuyuan Liu, Fenglin Liu, Junde Wu
First: 2025-02-11T00:13:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:41:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The severe shortage of medical doctors limits access to timely and reliable healthcare, leaving millions underserved. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential solution but struggle in real-world clinical interactions. Many LLMs are not grounded in authoritative medical guidelines and fail to transparently manage diagnostic uncertainty. Their language is often rigid and mechanical, lacking the human-like qualities essential for patient trust. To address these challenges, we propose Ask Patients with Patience (APP), a multi-turn LLM-based medical assistant designed for grounded reasoning, transparent diagnoses, and human-centric interaction. APP enhances communication by eliciting user symptoms through empathetic dialogue, significantly improving accessibility and user engagement. It also incorporates Bayesian active learning to support transparent and adaptive diagnoses. The framework is built on verified medical guidelines, ensuring clinically grounded and evidence-based reasoning. To evaluate its performance, we develop a new benchmark that simulates realistic medical conversations using patient agents driven by profiles extracted from real-world consultation cases. We compare APP against SOTA one-shot and multi-turn LLM baselines. The results show that APP improves diagnostic accuracy, reduces uncertainty, and enhances user experience. By integrating medical expertise with transparent, human-like interaction, APP bridges the gap between AI-driven medical assistance and real-world clinical practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：耐心询问患者：通过锚定推理使大语言模型实现以人为本的医疗对话
医疗医生严重短缺限制了及时可靠的医疗服务获取，使数百万患者得不到充分服务。大语言模型（LLMs）虽有潜力，但在真实临床互动中表现不佳，常缺乏权威医学指南支撑，无法透明管理诊断不确定性，语言也常机械僵硬，缺乏建立患者信任所需的人性化特质。为解决这些问题，我们提出“耐心询问患者”（APP）——一种基于多轮对话的LLM医疗助手，支持锚定推理、透明诊断和以人为本的交互。APP通过共情对话引导用户描述症状，显著提升可及性和用户参与度；并结合贝叶斯主动学习，支持透明自适应诊断。该框架基于经验证的医学指南，确保临床锚定与循证推理。为评估其性能，我们构建新基准，模拟真实医疗对话，使用从真实病例中提取的患者角色驱动对话代理。与当前最先进的一次性及多轮LLM基线对比，结果表明APP提升诊断准确性、降低不确定性、优化用户体验。通过融合医学专业知识与透明人性化交互，APP弥合了AI医疗辅助与真实临床实践之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the global shortage of medical professionals and the limitations of current LLMs in clinical settings—such as lack of grounding in medical guidelines, opaque reasoning, and mechanical language—the paper introduces Ask Patients with Patience (APP), a multi-turn LLM-based medical assistant that combines empathetic dialogue elicitation, Bayesian active learning for adaptive diagnosis, and verified medical guidelines to ensure grounded reasoning. Evaluated against state-of-the-art baselines on a new benchmark simulating real medical conversations, APP demonstrates improved diagnostic accuracy, reduced diagnostic uncertainty, and enhanced user engagement through human-centric interaction.
鉴于全球医疗人才短缺及现有大语言模型在临床场景中的局限——如缺乏权威医学指南支撑、推理不透明、语言机械——本文提出Ask Patients with Patience（APP），一种基于多轮对话的LLM医疗助手，旨在实现以人为本的医疗交互。APP通过整合经验证的医学指南与贝叶斯主动学习，实现透明且自适应的诊断，并通过共情式症状询问提升患者信任与参与度。在模拟真实问诊场景的新基准测试中，APP相较当前最优模型显著提升了诊断准确率、降低了不确定性，并改善了用户体验。
The Limits of AI-Driven Allocation: Optimal Screening under Aleatoric Uncertainty
Authors: Santiago Cortes-Gomez, Mateo Dulce Rubio, Carlos Patino, Bryan Wilder
First: 2026-05-08T16:38:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:38:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rise of machine learning has shifted targeted resource allocation in policy and humanitarian settings toward algorithmic targeting based on predicted risk scores. This approach is typically cheaper and faster than traditional screening procedures that directly observe the latent vulnerability status through physical verification. Yet, even access to the true conditional vulnerability probability cannot eliminate misallocation: aleatoric uncertainty over individual vulnerability status is irreducible, and probabilistic targeting inevitably misallocates some resources. In this work we study how screening and algorithmic targeting should be optimally combined in a two-stage allocation framework where a screening stage observes true outcomes for a subset of units before a final allocation stage assigns the resource under a fixed coverage budget. We show that the optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation, while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Furthermore, we empirically characterize when screening and algorithmic targeting act as complements or substitutes: efficiency gains from screening grow as the aleatoric uncertainty in the population increases. We illustrate our framework with applications in income-based social protection programs and humanitarian demining in Colombia, where the tension between screening costs and allocation efficiency is operationally consequential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI驱动分配的局限性：在随机不确定性下的最优筛选
机器学习的兴起已将政策和人道主义领域的目标资源配置转向基于预测风险评分的算法化目标设定。这种方法通常比传统通过物理验证直接观察潜在脆弱状态的筛选程序更便宜、更快。然而，即使获得真实的条件脆弱性概率，也无法完全消除资源配置错误：个体脆弱性状态的随机不确定性是不可消除的，概率化目标设定必然导致部分资源错配。本文研究在两阶段分配框架下，筛选与算法化目标设定应如何最优结合：第一阶段筛选部分单位的真实结果，第二阶段在固定覆盖预算下分配资源。我们证明，最优策略是在算法分配边缘进行筛选，同时直接针对最高风险单位。我们还通过实证分析，说明筛选与算法化目标设定何时互补或替代：随着群体中随机不确定性的增加，筛选带来的效率提升也随之增长。我们以基于收入的社会保护项目和哥伦比亚人道主义排雷应用为例，说明筛选成本与分配效率之间的张力在实际操作中具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of algorithmic risk scores for resource allocation in policy and humanitarian contexts, this paper investigates how to optimally combine screening with algorithmic targeting under aleatoric uncertainty—uncertainty inherent in individual vulnerability status that cannot be eliminated. The authors propose a two-stage framework: first, screen a subset of units to observe true outcomes, then allocate resources under a fixed budget using the algorithmic predictions. They find that optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Empirically, they show screening becomes more beneficial as aleatoric uncertainty increases, and demonstrate the framework’s applicability in social protection and demining contexts where cost-efficiency trade-offs are critical.
鉴于人工智能风险评分在政策与人道主义领域资源分配中的广泛应用，本研究探讨如何在不可消除的个体脆弱性不确定性（即随机不确定性）下，最优结合筛查与算法分配。作者提出两阶段框架：先对部分单位进行筛查以获取真实结果，再在固定预算下依据算法预测进行资源分配。研究发现，最优策略是筛查算法分配边缘的单位，同时直接针对最高风险单位。实证表明，当群体不确定性增加时，筛查带来的效率提升更显著，并在社会保护与哥伦比亚排雷等实际场景中验证了该框架在成本与效率权衡中的操作价值。
TAG-K: Tail-Averaged Greedy Kaczmarz for Computationally Efficient and Performant Online Inertial Parameter Estimation
Authors: Shuo Sha, Anupam Bhakta, Zhenyuan Jiang, Kevin Qiu, Ishaan Mahajan, Gabriel Bravo-Palacios, Brian Plancher
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-10-06T14:25:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:38:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICRA 2026. 3 Figures. 3 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate online inertial parameter estimation is essential for adaptive robotic control, enabling real-time adjustment to payload changes, environmental interactions, and system wear. Traditional methods often struggle to track abrupt parameter shifts or incur high computational costs, limiting their effectiveness in dynamic environments and for computationally constrained robotic systems. We introduce TAG-K, a lightweight extension of the Kaczmarz method that combines greedy randomized row selection for rapid convergence with tail averaging for robustness under noise and inconsistency. This design enables fast, stable parameter adaptation while retaining the low per-iteration complexity inherent to the Kaczmarz framework. We evaluate TAG-K in synthetic benchmarks and quadrotor tracking tasks against RLS, KF, and other Kaczmarz variants. TAG-K achieves 1.5x-1.9x faster solve times on laptop-class CPUs and 4.8x-20.7x faster solve times on embedded microcontrollers. More importantly, these speedups are paired with improved robustness to measurement noise and a 25% reduction in estimation error, leading to nearly 2x better end-to-end tracking performance. Website, documentation, and code available at: https://a2r-lab.org/TAG-K/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAG-K：面向高效在线惯性参数估计的尾均值贪心Kaczmarz方法
准确的在线惯性参数估计对自适应机器人控制至关重要，可实现对负载变化、环境交互及系统磨损的实时调整。传统方法常难以跟踪突变参数变化或计算成本高昂，限制其在动态环境和计算受限机器人系统中的有效性。本文提出TAG-K，作为Kaczmarz方法的轻量级扩展，结合贪心随机行选择以实现快速收敛，与尾均值策略以增强噪声和不一致性下的鲁棒性。该设计在保持Kaczmarz框架固有低每迭代复杂度的同时，实现快速且稳定的参数自适应。我们在合成基准和四旋翼跟踪任务中，与RLS、KF及其他Kaczmarz变体进行对比评估。TAG-K在笔记本电脑级CPU上实现1.5至1.9倍的求解加速，在嵌入式微控制器上实现4.8至20.7倍的加速。更重要的是，这些加速效果伴随测量噪声下的更强鲁棒性及25%的估计误差降低，最终实现近两倍的端到端跟踪性能提升。项目网站、文档及代码见：https://a2r-lab.org/TAG-K/。
Summary / 总结
To enable real-time adaptive robotic control under dynamic conditions and computational constraints, this paper proposes TAG-K, a lightweight Kaczmarz variant that combines greedy randomized row selection for rapid convergence with tail averaging to enhance robustness against noise and parameter inconsistency. Evaluated against RLS, KF, and other Kaczmarz methods in synthetic and quadrotor tracking tasks, TAG-K delivers 1.5x–1.9x faster solve times on laptops and 4.8x–20.7x faster times on microcontrollers, while simultaneously reducing estimation error by 25% and improving end-to-end tracking performance by nearly 2x.
