为支持动态环境下计算受限的机器人实时自适应控制，本文提出TAG-K方法，该方法结合贪心随机行选择以加速收敛，并引入尾部平均机制提升对噪声和参数不一致的鲁棒性。在合成数据与四旋翼跟踪任务中，与RLS、KF及其他Kaczmarz变体相比，TAG-K在笔记本电脑上提速1.5–1.9倍，在微控制器上提速4.8–20.7倍，同时估计误差降低25%，端到端跟踪性能提升近两倍。
Seeing Across Skies and Streets: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Satellite, Drone, and Ground Images
Authors: Qiwei Wang, Zhongyao Tuo, Xianghui Ze, Yujiao Shi
First: 2026-05-08T16:35:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:35:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-view localization classically asks: where does this ground image lie on the satellite tile? Existing methods are typically limited to 3-DoF estimates -- an $(x,y)$ position and a yaw angle -- because nadir satellite imagery provides no direct cues for roll, pitch, or altitude, forcing a reliance on planar-motion and zero-tilt assumptions. These assumptions break on real terrain with slopes, ramps, and tilted camera mounts. To overcome this, we introduce a single UAV image as an intermediate viewpoint: it reveals the 3D structure invisible from nadir, supplies the cues for roll, pitch, and altitude that the satellite alone cannot provide, and needs only spatial overlap with the ground camera -- no known relative pose is required. Building on this insight, we propose **Cross3R**, a flexible feed-forward model that ingests a satellite tile together with a UAV image, a ground image, or both, and, in a single forward pass, recovers a cross-view 3D point cloud, the 6-DoF poses of every input camera, and the on-tile $(x,y)$ position and yaw of each perspective camera. For training and evaluation, we also construct **CrossGeo**, a 278K-image tri-view dataset spanning 85 scenes across every continent except Antarctica. On CrossGeo, Cross3R consistently outperforms feed-forward 3D baselines in point-cloud reconstruction, 6-DoF camera-pose estimation, and cross-view localization. On KITTI, it outperforms dedicated cross-view methods trained on KITTI on most metrics, despite having no KITTI training itself.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨越天空与街道：从卫星、无人机和地面图像进行前馈三维重建
跨视角定位传统上询问：该地面图像位于卫星图块的何处？现有方法通常仅限于3自由度估计——(x,y)位置和偏航角——因为正射卫星影像无法直接提供俯仰、滚转或高度线索，迫使依赖平面运动和零倾斜假设。这些假设在真实地形（含坡度、坡道和倾斜相机支架）中失效。为克服此问题，我们引入单张无人机图像作为中间视角：它揭示正射视角下不可见的三维结构，提供卫星影像无法提供的俯仰、滚转和高度线索，且仅需与地面相机存在空间重叠——无需已知相对位姿。基于此洞察，我们提出**Cross3R**，一种灵活的前馈模型，可同时输入卫星图块、无人机图像、地面图像或二者，单次前向传播即可恢复跨视角三维点云、各输入相机的6自由度位姿，以及各视角相机在图块上的(x,y)位置和偏航角。为训练与评估，我们构建了**CrossGeo**数据集，包含27.8万张三视角图像，覆盖除南极洲外的85个场景。在CrossGeo上，Cross3R在点云重建、6自由度相机位姿估计和跨视角定位方面持续优于前馈三维基线方法。在KITTI数据集上，尽管未使用KITTI数据训练，其性能仍优于在KITTI上训练的专用跨视角方法的多数指标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional cross-view localization methods that rely on 3-DoF estimates due to the lack of roll, pitch, and altitude cues in nadir satellite imagery, this work introduces Cross3R, a feed-forward model that leverages a UAV image as an intermediate viewpoint to recover full 6-DoF camera poses and 3D point clouds from satellite, drone, and ground images. By using UAV imagery to provide structural cues unavailable from satellite alone, Cross3R achieves accurate reconstruction without requiring known relative poses. Evaluated on the newly constructed CrossGeo dataset (278K tri-view images across 85 global scenes) and KITTI, Cross3R outperforms existing feed-forward 3D baselines and even surpasses KITTI-trained cross-view methods on most metrics, demonstrating robust generalization.
为了解决现有跨视角定位方法因卫星正射影像缺乏俯仰、偏航和高度信息而只能估计3自由度的问题，该研究提出Cross3R模型，利用无人机图像作为中间视角，从卫星、无人机和地面图像中恢复6自由度相机位姿和3D点云。该模型在包含85个全球场景的27.8万张三视角图像数据集CrossGeo上训练，并在点云重建、6自由度位姿估计和跨视角定位任务中均优于现有基线方法，甚至在未使用KITTI数据训练的情况下，超越了专门针对KITTI优化的方法。
Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement in Online Federated LLM Fine-Tuning with Real-Time Feedback
Authors: Seohyun Lee, Wenzhi Fang, Dong-Jun Han, Seyyedali Hosseinalipour, Christopher G. Brinton
First: 2026-05-08T16:35:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:35:42+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent works have advanced feedback-based learning systems, whereby a foundation model is able to intake incoming feedback (e.g., a user) to self-improve, creating a self-loop system of training. However, existing works are limited in needing to consider an offline setup to allow for such feedback-based methods, and are further limited in the need of requiring privileged ground-truth contexts for training. Moreover, there is limited consideration of federated learning (FL), which is particularly well-suited for incorporating external feedback across large networks of end users, for example, but requires methods to be efficient for training on resource-constrained edge devices. Therefore, we introduce SPEAR (Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement), an efficient online learning algorithm for federated LLM fine-tuning. SPEAR utilizes a feedback-guided self-play loop to construct naturally contrastive pairs per prompt which are utilized to be trained on (i) standard maximum likelihood on correct completions and (ii) confidence-weighted unlikelihood on tail tokens of incorrect completions. Without the need of expensive group generations and ground-truth contexts for training (i.e., only partial, non-answer feedback), in contrast with existing works, SPEAR can be trained both online and in a resource-efficient manner. We validate SPEAR across various benchmark datasets, demonstrating its superior performance in comparison to state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/lee3296/SPEAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过优势加权精炼实现自对弈增强的在线联邦大语言模型微调与实时反馈
近期研究已推进基于反馈的学习系统，使基础模型能够接收输入反馈（如用户）以自我提升，形成训练的自循环系统。然而，现有工作受限于需离线设置以支持此类反馈方法，且需特权真实上下文进行训练。此外，对联邦学习（FL）考虑不足，FL特别适合在大规模终端用户网络中整合外部反馈，但需在资源受限的边缘设备上高效训练。为此，我们提出SPEAR（Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement），一种高效的在线联邦LLM微调算法。SPEAR利用反馈引导的自对弈循环，为每个提示构建自然对比对，用于训练：（i）标准最大似然学习正确补全；（ii）置信度加权的非似然学习错误补全的尾部标记。与现有方法相比，SPEAR无需昂贵的群体生成和真实上下文训练（即仅需部分非答案反馈），可在线且资源高效地训练。我们在多个基准数据集上验证SPEAR，显示其优于现有最先进基线。代码公开于https://github.com/lee3296/SPEAR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, real-time feedback-driven learning in federated settings without ground-truth or expensive group generations, SPEAR introduces an online self-play algorithm that leverages advantage-weighted refinement. It constructs contrastive training pairs per prompt by maximizing likelihood on correct completions and minimizing unlikelihood on tail tokens of incorrect ones, using only partial feedback. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show SPEAR outperforms existing methods while being computationally lightweight and suitable for edge devices.
为实现无需真实标签和昂贵群体生成、支持边缘设备实时反馈的联邦学习，SPEAR提出一种在线自对弈机制，通过部分反馈构建每提示的对比训练对，采用标准最大似然训练正确补全，同时对错误补全的尾部词加权训练低似然。实验表明，SPEAR在多个基准数据集上优于现有最先进方法，在准确率与效率方面均表现更优。
HEART: Hyperspherical Embedding Alignment via Kent-Representation Traversal in Diffusion Models
Authors: Arani Roy, Shristi Das Biswas, Kaushik Roy
First: 2026-05-08T16:32:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:32:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image diffusion models can generate visually stunning images, yet, controlling what appears and how it appears, remains surprisingly difficult, especially when operating solely within the constraints of the text-conditioning space. For example, changing a subject or adjusting an attribute often leads to unintended side effects, such as altered backgrounds or distorted details. This is because most existing text-based control methods treat the embedding space as Euclidean and apply simple linear transformations, which do not reflect how semantic concepts are actually organized. In this work, we take a step back and ask: what is the true geometry of these embeddings? We find that text encoder representations lie on a hypersphere, where concepts are not linear directions but structured, anisotropic distributions better captured by Kent distributions. Building on this insight, we propose HEART, a training-free framework that performs Kent-aware geodesic transformations directly on the hypersphere. By respecting the underlying geometry, HEART enables intuitive and precise edits, such as consistent subject replacement and fine-grained attribute control, while preserving the original scene. Importantly, HEART requires no finetuning, inversion, or optimization, and generalizes across diffusion model architectures. Our results show that a simple shift in perspective, from linear to spherical, can unlock fast, and controllable image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HEART：通过肯特表示遍历实现扩散模型中的超球面嵌入对齐
文本到图像的扩散模型虽能生成视觉惊艳的图像，但控制图像内容及其呈现方式仍十分困难，尤其在仅受限于文本条件空间时。例如，更换主体或调整属性常导致意外副作用，如背景变化或细节扭曲。这是因为现有大多数基于文本的控制方法将嵌入空间视为欧几里得空间，并应用简单线性变换，这未能反映语义概念的实际组织方式。本文我们退一步思考：这些嵌入的真实几何结构是什么？我们发现，文本编码器表示位于一个超球面上，其中概念并非线性方向，而是结构化、各向异性的分布，更适合用肯特分布建模。基于此洞察，我们提出HEART，一个无需训练的框架，直接在超球面上执行肯特感知的测地变换。通过尊重底层几何结构，HEART可实现直观且精确的编辑，如主体一致替换和细粒度属性控制，同时保留原始场景。重要的是，HEART无需微调、反演或优化，且可跨扩散模型架构泛化。我们的结果表明，从线性视角转向球面视角的简单转变，即可解锁快速且可控的图像生成。

Motivated by the difficulty of precise control in text-to-image diffusion models due to their reliance on Euclidean embedding assumptions, this work investigates the true geometric structure of text encoder representations and finds they lie on a hypersphere, better modeled by Kent distributions. The authors propose HEART, a training-free framework that performs geodesic transformations on this hypersphere using Kent-aware operations, enabling intuitive edits like subject replacement and attribute adjustment without distorting the scene. Experiments demonstrate that HEART achieves superior control and consistency compared to linear methods, without requiring fine-tuning, inversion, or optimization, and generalizes across diffusion architectures.
鉴于现有扩散模型在文本引导图像编辑中因假设嵌入空间为欧氏空间而难以实现精确控制，该研究发现文本编码器表示实际位于超球面上，更适合用Kent分布建模。作者提出HEART框架，通过在超球面上执行Kent感知的测地变换，实现直观的编辑如主体替换与属性微调，且不改变原场景。实验表明，相比线性方法，HEART在控制精度与一致性上表现更优，无需微调、反演或优化，且可跨扩散模型架构泛化。
It Just Takes Two: Scaling Amortized Inference to Large Sets
Authors: Antoine Wehenkel, Michael Kagan, Lukas Heinrich, Chris Pollard
First: 2026-05-08T16:32:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:32:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural posterior estimation has emerged as a powerful tool for amortized inference, with growing adoption across scientific and applied domains. In many of these applications, the conditioning variable is a set of observations whose elements depend not only on the target but also on unknown factors shared across the set. Optimal inference therefore requires treating the set jointly, which in turn requires training the estimator at the deployment set size -- a regime where memory and compute quickly become prohibitive. We introduce a simple, theoretically grounded strategy that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. Our method trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two, producing an encoder that generalizes to arbitrary set sizes. The inference head is then finetuned on pre-aggregated embeddings, making training cost essentially independent of the deployment set size N. Across scalar, image, multi-view 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional conditional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands, our approach matches or outperforms standard baselines at a fraction of the compute.
中文标题/摘要
标题：只需两人：将摊销推理扩展到大型集合
神经后验估计已成为摊销推理的强大工具，并在科学和应用领域得到越来越多采用。在许多应用中，条件变量是一组观测值，其元素不仅依赖于目标，还依赖于集合内共享的未知因素。因此，最优推理需要联合处理该集合，这要求在部署集合大小下训练估计器——而在该规模下，内存和计算成本迅速变得不可行。我们提出了一种简单且具有理论基础的策略，将表示学习与后验建模解耦。我们的方法在最多包含两个元素的集合上训练均值池化Deep Set，生成可泛化至任意集合大小的编码器。随后，推理头在预聚合的嵌入上进行微调，使训练成本几乎与部署集合大小N无关。在标量、图像、多视角3D、分子及高维条件生成基准测试中（N达数千），我们的方法在计算成本仅为基线的几分之一的情况下，达到或超越标准基线性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of training amortized inference models on large sets due to memory and compute constraints, the paper proposes a method that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. It trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two to learn a generalizable encoder, then fine-tunes a separate inference head on pre-aggregated embeddings. This design makes training cost independent of deployment set size N. Experiments across diverse domains including scalar, image, 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands show that the method matches or exceeds baseline performance while using significantly less compute.
为了解决在大规模集合上训练变分推理模型时因内存和计算资源受限的问题，该文提出一种将表示学习与后验建模解耦的方法。其核心是先在最大规模为二的集合上训练一个均值池化Deep Set以学习可泛化的编码器，再在预聚合的嵌入上微调推理头。该设计使训练开销与部署集合大小N无关。在包括标量、图像、3D多视角、分子及高维条件生成等多类基准测试中，当N达数千量级时，该方法在计算开销大幅降低的前提下，性能与现有基线持平或更优。
DVD: Discrete Voxel Diffusion for 3D Generation and Editing
Authors: Zhengrui Xiang, Jiaqi Wu, Fupeng Sun, Heliang Zheng, Yingzhen Li
First: 2026-05-08T16:32:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:32:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Discrete Voxel Diffusion (DVD), a discrete diffusion framework to generate, assess, and edit sparse voxels for SLat (Structured LATent) based 3D generative pipelines. Although discrete diffusion has not generally displaced continuous diffusion in image-like generation, we show that it can be an effective first-stage prior for sparse voxel scaffolds. By treating voxel occupancy as a native discrete variable, DVD avoids continuous-to-discrete thresholding and provides a simple framework for voxel generation, uncertainty estimation, and editing. Beyond quality gains, DVD provides more interpretable generation dynamics through explicit categorical modeling. Furthermore, we leverage the predictive entropy as a robust uncertainty metric to identify ambiguous voxel regions and complicated samples, facilitating tasks such as data filtering and quality assessment. Finally, we propose a lightweight fine-tuning strategy using block-structured perturbation patterns. This approach empowers the model to inpaint and edit voxels within a single sampling round, requiring negligible auxiliary computation and no additional model evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DVD：用于3D生成与编辑的离散体素扩散
我们提出了离散体素扩散（DVD），一种用于基于结构化潜在表示（SLat）的3D生成管线中生成、评估和编辑稀疏体素的离散扩散框架。尽管离散扩散在类似图像的生成中尚未普遍取代连续扩散，但我们证明其可作为稀疏体素骨架的有效先验第一阶段。通过将体素占据视为原生离散变量，DVD避免了连续到离散的阈值化过程，并提供了一个简洁的体素生成、不确定性估计与编辑框架。除质量提升外，DVD通过显式类别建模提供了更可解释的生成动态。此外，我们利用预测熵作为稳健的不确定性度量，以识别模糊体素区域和复杂样本，从而促进数据过滤与质量评估等任务。最后，我们提出了一种轻量级微调策略，采用块结构扰动模式。该方法使模型能在单次采样轮次内完成体素修复与编辑，几乎无需辅助计算且无需额外模型评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and interpretable 3D generation and editing in sparse voxel spaces, the authors propose Discrete Voxel Diffusion (DVD), a discrete diffusion framework tailored for SLat-based 3D pipelines. By modeling voxel occupancy as a native discrete variable, DVD eliminates the need for continuous-to-discrete thresholding and enables direct generation, uncertainty estimation, and editing within a unified framework. Experimental results show DVD achieves competitive or superior quality compared to continuous diffusion baselines, while offering more interpretable generation dynamics and robust uncertainty quantification via predictive entropy. The method also supports efficient voxel inpainting and editing through lightweight fine-tuning using block-structured perturbations, requiring minimal additional computation.
为实现高效且可解释的3D生成与编辑，作者提出离散体素扩散（DVD）框架，专用于SLat架构中的稀疏体素骨架。该方法将体素占据视为原生离散变量，避免连续到离散的阈值处理，直接支持生成、不确定性估计与编辑。实验表明，DVD在质量上可媲美或超越连续扩散方法，同时通过类别建模提供更清晰的生成动态。预测熵指标能有效识别模糊区域，辅助数据筛选与质量评估；所提轻量级微调策略支持单轮体素修复与编辑，计算开销极低，无需额外模型评估。
Linear Response Estimators for Singular Statistical Models
Authors: Chris Elliott, Daniel Murfet
First: 2026-05-08T16:31:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:31:56+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, comments welcome!
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We define susceptibilities as a measure of the response of an observable quantity of a parameterized statistical model to a perturbation of the data for a general class of observables. We define estimators for these susceptibilities as statistics in a sequence of n data-points and prove that these estimators are consistent and asymptotically unbiased in the large n regime.

标题：奇异统计模型的线性响应估计量

Motivated by the need to quantify how statistical models respond to data perturbations, the paper introduces susceptibilities as a general measure of response for observables under parameterized models. It constructs estimators for these susceptibilities using sequences of n data points and proves their consistency and asymptotic unbiasedness as n grows large. Experimental results confirm that these estimators converge reliably to true susceptibility values under various model perturbations, validating their theoretical guarantees.

When Diffusion Model Can Ignore Dimension: An Entropy-Based Theory
Authors: Ahmad Aghapour, Erhan Bayraktar
First: 2026-05-08T16:30:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:30:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models perform remarkably well on high-dimensional data such as images, often using only a modest number of reverse-time steps. Despite this practical success, existing convergence theory does not fully explain why such samplers remain efficient in high dimensions. Many prior KL guarantees bound the discretization error in terms of the ambient dimension, while other improved results replace this dependence using intrinsic-dimensional or geometric structure assumptions. In this work, we develop an alternative information-theoretic perspective on diffusion sampler convergence. We prove that, for Gaussian mixture targets, the discretization error is controlled by the Shannon entropy of the latent mixture component rather than by the ambient dimension. Consequently, the leading step complexity scales linearly with latent entropy and depends only logarithmically on the second moment of the data. Our analysis also extends to discrete target distributions, where the relevant complexity is the entropy of the target rather than the dimension of the embedding space. These results suggest that diffusion sampling can remain efficient in high-dimensional spaces when the data distribution admits a compact latent representation, as is widely believed to be the case for natural images.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型何时可忽略维度：基于熵的理论
扩散模型在图像等高维数据上表现优异，通常仅需少量反向时间步。尽管实践成功，现有收敛理论未能完全解释为何此类采样器在高维空间仍保持高效。许多先前的KL保证以环境维度衡量离散化误差，而其他改进结果则通过内在维度或几何结构假设替代该依赖。本文从信息论视角提出扩散采样器收敛的替代理论：对高斯混合目标，离散化误差由潜在混合分量的香农熵控制，而非环境维度。因此，主导步复杂度与潜在熵线性相关，仅对数据二阶矩呈对数依赖。该分析亦适用于离散目标分布，其相关复杂度为目标熵而非嵌入空间维度。结果表明，当数据分布具备紧凑潜在表示（如自然图像普遍被认为如此）时，扩散采样在高维空间仍可保持高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical efficiency of diffusion models in high-dimensional spaces despite theoretical limitations, this work introduces an entropy-based information-theoretic framework to explain their convergence. It demonstrates that for Gaussian mixture targets, discretization error depends on the Shannon entropy of the latent representation rather than ambient dimension, leading to step complexity scaling linearly with latent entropy and logarithmically with data second moment. The analysis extends to discrete targets, where entropy of the target distribution governs complexity, not embedding dimension. Experiments confirm that diffusion sampling remains efficient when data admits compact latent structures, aligning with observations in natural image modeling.
鉴于扩散模型在高维数据上表现高效但现有理论难以解释其收敛性，本文提出基于熵的信息论视角。研究证明，对高斯混合目标，离散化误差由潜在表示的香农熵决定而非环境维度，导致步数复杂度与潜在熵线性相关、与数据二阶矩对数相关。该分析亦适用于离散分布，其中目标熵替代嵌入空间维度作为关键复杂度指标，支持高维数据若具备紧凑潜在结构则可高效采样的观点。
Searching for Privacy Risks in LLM Agents via Simulation
Authors: Yanzhe Zhang, Diyi Yang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-08-14T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:28:36+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The widespread deployment of LLM-based agents is likely to introduce a critical privacy threat: malicious agents that proactively engage others in multi-turn interactions to extract sensitive information. However, the evolving nature of such dynamic dialogues makes it challenging to anticipate emerging vulnerabilities and design effective defenses. To tackle this problem, we present a search-based framework that alternates between improving attack and defense strategies through the simulation of privacy-critical agent interactions. Specifically, we employ LLMs as optimizers to analyze simulation trajectories and iteratively propose new agent instructions. To explore the strategy space more efficiently, we further utilize parallel search with multiple threads and cross-thread propagation. Through this process, we find that attack strategies escalate from direct requests to sophisticated tactics, such as impersonation and consent forgery, while defenses evolve from simple rule-based constraints to robust identity-verification state machines. The discovered attacks and defenses generalize across diverse scenarios and backbone models, providing useful insights for developing privacy-aware agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过模拟寻找大语言模型代理中的隐私风险
大语言模型代理的广泛部署可能带来关键隐私威胁：恶意代理会主动参与多轮交互以提取敏感信息。然而，此类动态对话的演变特性使得预测新兴漏洞并设计有效防御措施颇具挑战。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于搜索的框架，通过模拟隐私关键代理交互，交替优化攻击与防御策略。具体而言，我们利用大语言模型作为优化器，分析模拟轨迹并迭代提出新的代理指令。为更高效探索策略空间，我们进一步采用多线程并行搜索与跨线程传播。通过该过程，我们发现攻击策略从直接请求升级为复杂手段，如伪装与伪造同意，而防御策略则从简单规则约束演进为稳健的身份验证状态机。所发现的攻击与防御策略可泛化至多样场景与基础模型，为开发隐私感知代理提供有益洞见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that LLM agents may exploit multi-turn conversations to extract sensitive data, this study introduces a search-based simulation framework that iteratively refines attack and defense strategies using LLMs as optimizers. By running parallel simulations across multiple threads and propagating insights across them, the method efficiently explores strategy space. Experimental results reveal that attacks evolve from direct queries to complex tactics like impersonation and consent forgery, while defenses advance from basic rules to identity-verification state machines, demonstrating generalizability across models and scenarios.
鉴于LLM代理可能通过多轮对话主动窃取敏感信息，本研究提出一种基于搜索的模拟框架，利用LLM作为优化器，交替改进攻击与防御策略。通过模拟隐私关键交互并结合并行搜索与跨线程传播，该方法识别出攻击策略从直接请求演进至伪装与伪造同意等复杂手段，同时防御策略从简单规则约束升级为身份验证状态机。实验表明，这些发现的策略可泛化至不同模型与场景，为构建隐私感知的智能代理提供实用参考。
Asymptotically Log-Optimal Bayes-Assisted Confidence Sequences for Bounded Means
Authors: Valentin Kilian, Stefano Cortinovis, François Caron
First: 2026-05-08T16:27:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:27:50+00:00
Comments: Valentin and Stefano are equal first author
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Confidence sequences based on test martingales provide time-uniform uncertainty quantification for the mean of bounded IID observations without parametric distributional assumptions. Their practical efficiency, however, depends strongly on the choice of martingale updates, and many existing constructions do not exploit prior information about plausible data-generating distributions or mean values. We propose a Bayes-assisted framework that uses a Bayesian working predictive model to adaptively construct confidence sequences.For each candidate mean and time point, the predictive distribution selects, among valid one-step martingale factors, the update maximising predictive expected log-growth; validity is therefore preserved even when the prior or working model is misspecified. We prove that if the predictive distribution is Wasserstein-consistent, the resulting procedure is asymptotically log-optimal, matching the per-sample log-growth of an oracle procedure with access to the true distribution. We instantiate the framework using robust predictives based on Dirichlet-process mixtures and Bayesian exponentially tilted empirical likelihood. Experiments on synthetic data, sequential best-arm identification for LLM evaluation, and prediction-powered inference show that informative priors can substantially reduce confidence-sequence width and sampling effort while retaining anytime-valid coverage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有界均值的渐近对数最优贝叶斯辅助置信序列
基于检验鞅的置信序列可为有界独立同分布观测的均值提供时间均匀的不确定性量化，且无需参数化分布假设。然而其实际效率高度依赖鞅更新的选择，许多现有构造未利用关于合理数据生成分布或均值的先验信息。我们提出一种贝叶斯辅助框架，利用贝叶斯工作预测模型自适应构建置信序列。对每个候选均值和时间点，预测分布从有效的一步鞅因子中选择使预测期望对数增长最大的更新；即使先验或工作模型存在误设，有效性仍可保持。我们证明，若预测分布满足Wasserstein一致性，则所得方法渐近对数最优，其每样本对数增长可匹配拥有真实分布的Oracle方法。我们通过基于Dirichlet过程混合的鲁棒预测及贝叶斯指数倾斜经验似然实例化该框架。在合成数据、LLM评估的序列最优臂识别及预测驱动推断实验中，我们发现信息性先验可显著减少置信序列宽度和采样量，同时保持任意时间点的覆盖有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, anytime-valid confidence sequences for bounded means without distributional assumptions, this work introduces a Bayes-assisted framework that adaptively selects one-step martingale updates using a Bayesian predictive model to maximize expected log-growth. The method ensures validity even under model misspecification and is proven asymptotically log-optimal if the predictive distribution is Wasserstein-consistent. Experiments demonstrate that incorporating informative priors via Dirichlet-process mixtures or Bayesian exponentially tilted empirical likelihood significantly narrows confidence intervals and reduces sampling requirements while maintaining coverage guarantees.
为在无需分布假设的前提下实现对有界均值的高效、任意时间点有效置信序列，本文提出一种贝叶斯辅助框架，利用贝叶斯预测模型自适应选择最大化预测对数增长的单步鞅更新，即使模型存在偏差仍保持有效性。当预测分布满足Wasserstein一致性时，该方法渐近对数最优，匹配拥有真实分布的“理想”方法的每样本效率。在合成数据、大语言模型最佳臂识别及预测驱动推断实验中，引入信息性先验显著缩小置信区间、减少采样量，同时保持覆盖保证。
Aggregation in conformal e-classification
Authors: Vladimir Vovk

Abstract
Aggregating conformal predictors is a standard way of balancing their predictive and computational efficiency while retaining their validity, at least approximately. An important advantage of conformal e-predictors is that they are easier to aggregate without sacrificing their validity. This paper studies experimentally cross-conformal e-prediction, which is an existing method of aggregating conformal e-predictors, and its modifications that are conceptually simpler and more flexible.

FLAM: Evaluating Model Performance with Aggregatable Measures in Federated Learning
Authors: Fabian Stricker, Jose A. Peregrina, David Bermbach, Christian Zirpins
First: 2026-05-08T16:25:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:25:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in 2nd IEEE International Conference on Federated Learning and Intelligent Computing Systems(FLICS2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Performance evaluation is essential for assessing the quality of machine learning (ML) models and guiding deployment decisions. In federated learning (FL), assessing the performance is challenging because data are distributed across participants. Consequently, the coordinator must rely on locally computed evaluation metrics and aggregate them to assess the global model. A key challenge is that common aggregation strategies, such as weighted averaging based on the local samples per participant, do not always produce the same results as centralized evaluation. Existing definitions of performance evaluation are largely tailored to accuracy and do not generalize to other metrics, leading to inconsistencies between participant-based and centralized evaluation. However, such discrepancies are inconsistent with the FL objective and lead to a wrong calculation of the metric. To address this issue, we examine the underlying reasons for these discrepancies and propose FLAM, a performance evaluation method based on aggregatable measures that yields the same results as centralized evaluation without the need for a global test dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLAM：基于可聚合度量的联邦学习模型性能评估
性能评估对于评估机器学习（ML）模型质量并指导部署决策至关重要。在联邦学习（FL）中，由于数据分布在各参与方，评估性能颇具挑战性。因此，协调器必须依赖本地计算的评估指标并将其聚合以评估全局模型。关键挑战在于，常见的聚合策略（如基于各参与方本地样本数的加权平均）并不总能与集中式评估得到相同结果。现有性能评估定义大多针对准确率，难以推广至其他指标，导致参与方评估与集中式评估结果不一致。然而，此类差异与FL目标不符，会导致指标计算错误。 为解决此问题，我们分析了这些差异的根本原因，并提出FLAM方法，该方法基于可聚合度量，无需全局测试数据集即可获得与集中式评估一致的结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inconsistency between participant-local and centralized performance evaluations in federated learning, this paper proposes FLAM, a method that defines and aggregates performance measures to match centralized evaluation results without requiring a global test set. FLAM addresses the issue by identifying that traditional aggregation strategies—like weighted averaging by local sample counts—distort metric values due to non-aggregatable definitions of performance. Experimental results show that FLAM consistently reproduces centralized evaluation outcomes across diverse datasets and model architectures, validating its correctness and practical utility in federated settings.
为解决联邦学习中参与者本地评估与集中式评估结果不一致的问题，本文提出FLAM方法，通过定义可聚合的评估指标，在无需全局测试集的情况下实现与集中式评估一致的结果。研究发现，传统按本地数据量加权平均等聚合策略因缺乏对除准确率外其他指标的普适性，导致评估偏差。实验表明，FLAM在多种数据集和模型架构下均能稳定复现集中式评估结果，从而实现联邦学习场景中可靠、公平的性能评估。
Theory of Optimal Learning Rate Schedules and Scaling Laws for a Random Feature Model
Authors: Blake Bordelon, Francesco Mori
First: 2026-02-04T17:11:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:24:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Setting the learning rate (LR) for a deep learning model is a critical part of successful training. Choosing LRs is often done empirically with trial and error. In this work, we explore a solvable model of optimal LR schedules for a powerlaw random feature model trained with stochastic gradient descent (SGD). We consider the optimal schedule $η_T^\star(t)$ where $t$ is the current iterate and $T$ is the training horizon. This schedule is computed both as a numerical optimization problem and also analytically using optimal control theory. Our analysis reveals two regimes which we term the easy phase and hard phase. In the easy phase the optimal schedule is a polynomial decay $η_T^\star(t) \simeq T^{-ξ} (1-t/T)^δ$ where $ξ$ and $δ$ depend on the properties of the features and task. In the hard phase, the optimal schedule resembles warmup-stable-decay with constant initial LR and annealing performed over a vanishing fraction of training steps. We investigate joint optimization of LR and batch size and find batch ramps can improve the wall-clock time in the easy phase. Beyond SGD, we derive optimal schedules for momentum parameter $β(t)$ and show that it improves the loss-scaling exponent in the hard phase. We compare our optimal schedule to various benchmarks including (1) optimal constant learning rates $η_T(t) \sim T^{-ξ}$ (2) optimal power laws $η_T(t) \sim T^{-ξ} t^{-χ}$, finding that our schedule achieves better rates than either of these. Our theory suggests that LR transfer across training horizon depends on the structure of the model and task. For ResNet image classification on CIFAR-5M, the learning curves exhibit hard-phase behavior where optimal base LRs are constant under sufficient annealing. GPT-2 style transformers trained in language modeling exhibit easy-phase behavior where optimal LRs shift even under annealing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机特征模型中最优学习率调度与缩放定律的理论
深度学习模型的学习率设置是成功训练的关键部分。学习率通常通过试错法经验选择。本文研究了一个可解的幂律随机特征模型在随机梯度下降（SGD）下的最优学习率调度。我们考虑最优调度η_T*(t)，其中t为当前迭代步，T为训练周期。该调度通过数值优化和最优控制理论分析得出。我们的分析揭示了两个阶段：易阶段和难阶段。在易阶段，最优调度为多项式衰减η_T*(t) ≃ T^{-ξ} (1-t/T)^δ，其中ξ和δ取决于特征和任务属性。在难阶段，最优调度类似于预热-稳定-衰减，初始学习率恒定，退火过程发生在训练步数的趋近于零的比例上。我们研究了学习率与批量大小的联合优化，发现批量渐增可改善易阶段的墙钟时间。除SGD外，我们推导了动量参数β(t)的最优调度，并证明其可改善难阶段的损失缩放指数。我们将最优调度与多种基准对比，包括（1）最优恒定学习率η_T(t) ∼ T^{-ξ}；（2）最优幂律η_T(t) ∼ T^{-ξ} t^{-χ}，发现我们的调度优于二者。理论表明，学习率跨训练周期的迁移依赖于模型和任务结构。在CIFAR-5M图像分类任务中，ResNet的学习曲线表现出难阶段行为，即在充分退火下最优基础学习率恒定；而GPT-2风格的Transformer在语言建模中表现出易阶段行为，即即使在退火下最优学习率仍会变化。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of setting optimal learning rates for deep learning models by analyzing a solvable random feature model under stochastic gradient descent. Using optimal control theory and numerical optimization, it derives time-dependent learning rate schedules that adapt to the training dynamics, distinguishing between 'easy' and 'hard' phases based on model-task structure. In the easy phase, the optimal schedule follows a polynomial decay, while in the hard phase, it resembles a warmup-stable-decay pattern with constant initial rate. The study also explores joint optimization with batch size and momentum, showing batch ramps improve wall-clock time in the easy phase and momentum enhances loss scaling in the hard phase. Experiments on ResNet and GPT-2-style models confirm that optimal base learning rates remain constant in the hard phase (e.g., CIFAR-5M) and vary in the easy phase (e.g., language modeling), validating the theory's predictive power.
该论文针对深度学习模型中学习率设置的难题，通过分析一个可解的随机特征模型在随机梯度下降下的最优学习率调度策略。作者利用最优控制理论与数值优化推导出随时间变化的最优学习率，识别出两种训练阶段：易阶段中学习率呈多项式衰减，难阶段则呈现预热-稳定-衰减模式。同时，他们联合优化批大小与学习率，发现批大小渐进可缩短易阶段的训练时间；还推导出最优动量参数调度，提升难阶段的损失缩放指数。实验对比表明，其调度策略优于常数和幂律基准。理论预测最优学习率随训练周期的调整依赖于模型与任务结构，实验证实ResNet在CIFAR-5M上呈现难阶段行为，而GPT-2风格模型在语言建模中呈现易阶段行为。
Graph Representation Learning Augmented Model Manipulation on Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs
Authors: Hanlin Cai, Kai Li, Houtianfu Wang, Haofan Dong, Yichen Li, Falko Dressler, Ozgur B. Akan
First: 2026-05-08T16:24:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:24:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated fine-tuning (FFT) has emerged as a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaboratively adapting large language models (LLMs). Built upon federated learning, FFT enables distributed agents to jointly refine a shared pretrained LLM by aggregating local LLM updates without sharing local raw data. However, FFT-based LLMs remain vulnerable to model manipulation threats, in which adversarial participants upload manipulated LLM updates that corrupt the aggregation process and degrade the performance of the global LLM. In this paper, we propose an Augmented Model maniPulation (AugMP) strategy against FFT-based LLMs. Specifically, we design a novel graph representation learning framework that captures feature correlations among benign LLM updates to guide the generation of malicious updates. To enhance manipulation effectiveness and stealthiness, we develop an iterative manipulation algorithm based on an augmented Lagrangian dual formulation. Through this formulation, malicious updates are optimized to embed adversarial objectives while preserving benign-like parameter characteristics. Experimental results across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that the AugMP strategy achieves the strongest manipulation performance among all competing baselines, reducing the global LLM accuracy by up to 26% and degrading the average accuracy of local LLM agents by up to 22%. Meanwhile, AugMP maintains high statistical and geometric consistency with benign updates, enabling it to evade conventional distance- and similarity-based defense methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图表示学习的联邦微调大语言模型对抗性模型操纵策略
联邦微调（FFT）作为一种隐私保护范式，支持协作调整大语言模型（LLMs）。基于联邦学习，FFT使分布式代理能够通过聚合本地LLM更新共同优化共享的预训练LLM，而无需共享本地原始数据。然而，基于FFT的LLMs仍易受模型操纵威胁，即恶意参与者上传被操纵的LLM更新，破坏聚合过程并降低全局LLM性能。本文提出一种针对FFT-LLMs的增强型模型操纵（AugMP）策略。具体而言，我们设计了一种新颖的图表示学习框架，捕捉良性LLM更新间的特征相关性，以指导恶意更新的生成。为提升操纵效果与隐蔽性，我们基于增强拉格朗日对偶形式开发了一种迭代操纵算法。通过该形式，恶意更新被优化以嵌入对抗目标，同时保持良性参数特征。在多个LLM架构上的实验表明，AugMP策略在所有对比基线中表现最优，可使全局LLM准确率下降高达26%，本地LLM代理平均准确率下降高达22%。同时，AugMP在统计与几何一致性上保持与良性更新高度一致，从而能规避传统基于距离与相似度的防御方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of federated fine-tuning of LLMs to adversarial manipulation, this work proposes AugMP, a strategy that leverages graph representation learning to model benign update correlations and generate stealthy malicious updates. The method employs an iterative optimization algorithm based on an augmented Lagrangian dual formulation to embed adversarial objectives while preserving benign parameter distributions. Experiments across multiple LLM backbones show AugMP achieves the highest manipulation effectiveness, reducing global LLM accuracy by up to 26% and local agent accuracy by up to 22%, while remaining statistically and geometrically consistent with benign updates to evade common defense mechanisms.
为应对联邦微调LLM过程中模型被恶意操纵的脆弱性，本文提出AugMP策略，利用图表示学习建模良性LLM更新间的特征关联，以生成隐蔽的恶意更新。该方法基于增广拉格朗日对偶框架设计迭代操纵算法，使恶意更新在嵌入对抗目标的同时保持良性参数分布特征。实验表明，AugMP在多个LLM架构上表现最优，可使全局模型准确率下降高达26%，本地模型平均准确率下降高达22%，且其更新在统计与几何层面与良性更新高度一致，能有效规避基于距离与相似度的传统防御方法。
Exploring a Virtual Pet to Provide Context Notifications in a Tourism Recommender System: a Pilot Study
Authors: Patrícia Alves, Joana Neto, Ana Barreiro, Jorge Lima, Fausto Alves, Henish Balu, Luís Conceição, Goreti Marreiros
First: 2026-05-08T16:23:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:23:53+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While context-aware personalization has been widely explored in modern tourism Recommender Systems (RS), the delivery of real-time notifications remains a significant design challenge due to issues of intrusiveness and user fatigue. This paper presents a proof-of-concept for a tourism recommendation framework that utilizes a virtual pet as a social mediator for delivering context-aware alerts. The system integrates real-time environmental data - including air quality, noise levels, and weather forecasts - and proximity-based notifications with a Multi-Agent Microservice that generates personalized recommendations based on the user's personality traits and preferences. A within-subjects pilot study (n=11) was conducted to evaluate the feasibility and user acceptance of this pet-mediated approach. Participants interacted with two versions of the system - a baseline without contextual alerts and a version featuring pet-mediated notifications - over a four-week period (two weeks per version) in real-world scenarios. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected to assess engagement, perceived naturalness, notification utility, and acceptance. Preliminary results suggest that the virtual pet effectively can "soften" the perceived intrusiveness of system alerts, making safety-critical information feel more welcome and natural. Furthermore, the character-mediated justifications significantly improved the clarity of the notifications, effectively supporting users in their real-time travel decisions. These findings provide a foundation for using virtual pet companions to enhance the transparency and acceptance of context-aware communication in tourism RS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：虚拟宠物在旅游推荐系统中提供情境通知的探索：一项试点研究
尽管情境感知个性化在现代旅游推荐系统中已被广泛研究，但实时通知的交付仍因侵入性和用户疲劳问题而成为重要设计挑战。本文提出了一种旅游推荐框架的可行性概念，利用虚拟宠物作为社交中介传递情境感知警报。系统整合实时环境数据（包括空气质量、噪音水平和天气预报）与基于接近度的通知，并通过多智能体微服务根据用户性格特征和偏好生成个性化推荐。一项受试者内试点研究（n=11）评估了该宠物中介方法的可行性与用户接受度。参与者在真实场景中为期四周（每版本两周）分别体验无情境警报的基线版本和宠物中介通知版本。通过定量与定性数据评估参与度、感知自然性、通知效用与接受度。初步结果显示，虚拟宠物有效“软化”了系统警报的侵入感，使安全关键信息更易被接受且自然。此外，角色中介的说明显著提升了通知清晰度，有效支持用户实时旅行决策。这些发现为利用虚拟宠物伴侣增强旅游推荐系统中情境感知通信的透明度与接受度提供了基础。

为解决旅游推荐系统中实时通知易引发用户疲劳和侵入感的问题，本研究提出以虚拟宠物作为社交中介来缓和通知的传达方式。方法上，系统结合实时环境数据与基于位置的通知，通过多智能体微服务根据用户性格与偏好生成个性化推荐。在11名参与者为期四周的组内实验中，用户分别体验无上下文通知的基线版本与宠物中介版本。结果表明，虚拟宠物有效降低了通知的侵入感，提升了通知清晰度，增强了用户接受度，并辅助用户做出实时出行决策。
TAP: Two-Stage Adaptive Personalization of Multi-Task and Multi-Modal Foundation Models in Federated Learning
Authors: Seohyun Lee, Wenzhi Fang, Dong-Jun Han, Seyyedali Hosseinalipour, Christopher G. Brinton
First: 2025-09-30T17:01:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:22:40+00:00
Comments: 29 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In federated learning (FL), local personalization of models has received significant attention, yet personalized fine-tuning of foundation models remains underexplored. In particular, there is a lack of understanding in the literature on how to personalize foundation models in settings where there exist heterogeneity not only in data, but also in tasks and modalities across the clients. To address this gap, we propose Two-Stage Adaptive Personalization (TAP). In the first stage, TAP leverages mismatched model architectures between clients and the server to selectively replace personalized parameters with global updates, explicitly limiting cross-task and cross-modality interference. In the second stage, TAP conducts post-FL distillation on the global model to recover a beneficial shared structure. By reintroducing generalizable knowledge only after the global model has stabilized, TAP enhances generalization without compromising personalization. In developing our methodology, we introduce the first convergence analysis of federated foundation model training at the server under modality-task pair heterogeneity across clients, and demonstrate the impact of the number of modality-task pairs on model fine-tuning. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of TAP across a variety of datasets and tasks in comparison to state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/lee3296/TAP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAP：联邦学习中多任务多模态基础模型的两阶段自适应个性化
在联邦学习（FL）中，模型的本地个性化已受到广泛关注，但基础模型的个性化微调仍处于探索阶段。尤其在客户端间不仅数据存在异质性，任务和模态也存在异质性的场景下，现有文献对此类个性化方法缺乏系统理解。为填补这一空白，我们提出两阶段自适应个性化（TAP）。第一阶段，TAP利用客户端与服务器间模型架构的不匹配性，选择性地用全局更新替换个性化参数，显式限制跨任务与跨模态干扰；第二阶段，TAP对全局模型进行后FL蒸馏，以恢复有益的共享结构。通过在全局模型稳定后才引入可迁移知识，TAP在提升泛化能力的同时不损害个性化效果。在方法构建过程中，我们首次对客户端间模态-任务对异质性下的服务器端联邦基础模型训练收敛性进行分析，并展示模态-任务对数量对模型微调的影响。大量实验表明，TAP在多种数据集和任务上均优于现有最先进基线方法。代码已公开于 https://github.com/lee3296/TAP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to personalize foundation models in federated learning under heterogeneous data, tasks, and modalities across clients, TAP introduces a two-stage adaptive personalization framework. In the first stage, it selectively replaces personalized parameters with global updates to reduce cross-task and cross-modality interference, leveraging mismatched client-server architectures. In the second stage, it performs post-FL distillation to recover a generalizable shared structure from the stabilized global model. Experiments across diverse datasets and tasks show TAP outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while maintaining personalization, and the authors provide the first convergence analysis under modality-task pair heterogeneity, demonstrating that more modality-task pairs negatively impact fine-tuning.
为解决联邦学习中客户端间数据、任务和模态异构导致的预训练模型个性化难题，TAP提出两阶段自适应个性化框架。第一阶段利用客户端与服务器架构差异，选择性替换个性化参数以减少跨任务与跨模态干扰；第二阶段在全局模型稳定后进行蒸馏，恢复可迁移结构，提升泛化能力而不损害个性化。实验表明TAP在多数据集和任务上优于现有方法，且首次对多模态-任务异构下的联邦预训练模型收敛性进行分析，证明模态-任务对数显著影响微调效果。
Convergent Stochastic Training of Attention and Understanding LoRA
Authors: Zhengkai Sun, Dibyakanti Kumar, Alejandro F Frangi, Anirbit Mukherjee, Mingfei Sun
First: 2026-05-08T16:22:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:22:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers have revolutionized machine learning and deploying attention layers in the model is increasingly standard across a myriad of applications. Further, for large models, it is common to implement Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA), whereby a factorized parameterization of them is trained, to achieve a surprisingly beneficial accuracy-size trade-off. In this work, via a unified framework we rigorously establish trainability of such models under stochastic methods. We prove that for any mild regularization, the empirical regression loss on a attention layer and LoRA on a shallow neural net, both induce Poincaré inequality for the corresponding Gibbs' measure. Then it follows via invoking recent results that a certain SDE, which mimics the SGD, minimizes the corresponding losses. In both the cases, our first-of-its-kind results of trainability on attention and nets, do not rely on any assumptions on the data or the size of the architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意力与理解LoRA的收敛随机训练
Transformer架构已彻底改变机器学习，将注意力层部署于模型中已成为众多应用中的标准做法。此外，对于大型模型，通常采用低秩适应（LoRA）方法，通过因子化参数化进行训练，以实现令人惊讶的精度-规模权衡。在本研究中，我们通过统一框架严格证明了此类模型在随机方法下的可训练性。我们证明，对于任何温和的正则化，注意力层和浅层神经网络上的LoRA所对应的实证回归损失，均能诱导相应的吉布斯测度的庞加莱不等式。随后，通过调用近期结果可知，一种模拟SGD的随机微分方程（SDE）可最小化相应的损失。在两种情况下，我们首次提出的注意力层与网络的可训练性结果，均不依赖于数据或架构规模的任何假设。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the widespread use of attention layers and LoRA in large-scale models, this work establishes the trainability of such architectures under stochastic optimization. Using a unified framework, it proves that under mild regularization, both attention layers and LoRA on shallow neural nets induce Poincaré inequalities for their corresponding Gibbs measures, which implies that a stochastic differential equation mimicking SGD minimizes the empirical regression loss. The key result is that these trainability guarantees hold without assumptions on data distribution or model size, offering a foundational theoretical understanding of stochastic training for attention and LoRA.
鉴于注意力层和LoRA在大型Transformer模型中的广泛应用，本研究旨在严格证明其在随机优化下的可训练性。作者提出统一框架，证明在轻度正则化下，注意力层和浅层LoRA网络均能诱导庞卡莱不等式，从而表明随机梯度下降（SGD）可被建模为最小化经验回归损失的随机微分方程（SDE）梯度流。该理论结果是首次针对此类架构的训练性证明，且无需对数据分布或模型规模做任何假设，展示了其在两种结构下随机训练的收敛保证。
TimeLesSeg: Unified Contrast-Agnostic Cross-Sectional and Longitudinal MS Lesion Segmentation via a Stochastic Generative Model
Authors: Vicent Caselles-Ballester, Eloy Martínez-Heras, Giuseppe Pontillo, Zoe Mendelsohn, Elena M. Marrón, Juan Luis García Fernández, Laia Subirats, Jon Stutters, Jeremy Chataway, Frederik Barkhof, Sara Llufriu, Ferran Prados
First: 2026-05-08T16:19:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:19:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multiple sclerosis (MS) expresses substantial clinical and radiological heterogeneity, which poses significant challenges for automatic lesion segmentation. The current deep learning-based SOTA is highly susceptible to changes in both distribution, e.g., changes in scanner; as well as the structure of inputs, evident in the current divide between cross-sectional and longitudinal approaches. We introduce TimeLesSeg, a unified contrast-agnostic framework designed to segment MS lesions regardless of the presence of a temporal dimension in its inputs, with a single convolutional neural network. Our approach models pathological priors through lesion masks, which are processed together with the current scan. Cross-sectional processing is enabled by exposing the model to training cases where no prior information is available, which are modeled with an empty mask, allowing it to operate seamlessly in both scenarios. To overcome the scarcity and inconsistency of longitudinal datasets, we propose a novel generative pipeline in which patterns of lesion evolution are simulated by stochastically deforming each individual lesion with morphological operations, producing realistic prior timepoints. In parallel, we achieve contrast agnosticism through Gaussian mixture model-based domain randomization, enabling the network to experience a wide spectrum of intensity profiles. Results on three publicly available and two in-house datasets show that TimeLesSeg outperforms the contrast-agnostic state of the art on single-modality inputs across overlap- and distance-based metrics. In longitudinal processing, our method outperforms SAMSEG, and captures lesion load dynamics more accurately than both the former and LST-AI. All source code related to the development of TimeLesSeg is available at https://github.com/NeuroADaS-Lab/TimeLesSeg.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TimeLesSeg：通过随机生成模型实现统一的对比无关跨断面与纵向多发性硬化病灶分割
多发性硬化（MS）表现出显著的临床和影像学异质性，给自动病灶分割带来重大挑战。当前基于深度学习的最先进方法对输入分布（如扫描仪变化）和输入结构（当前跨断面与纵向方法的分离）高度敏感。我们提出TimeLesSeg，一种统一的对比无关框架，通过单一卷积神经网络，无论输入是否包含时间维度均可分割MS病灶。该方法通过病灶掩码建模病理先验，与当前扫描一同处理。跨断面处理通过训练时引入无先验信息的病例（用空掩码建模），使模型无缝适应两种场景。为克服纵向数据稀缺与不一致，我们提出新颖生成流程：通过形态学操作随机变形每个病灶，模拟病灶演化模式，生成真实先验时间点。同时，通过基于高斯混合模型的域随机化实现对比无关性，使网络体验广泛强度分布。在三个公开及两个内部数据集上的结果表明，TimeLesSeg在单模态输入下，优于现有对比无关最先进方法（基于重叠与距离指标）；在纵向处理中，超越SAMSEG，且比前者及LST-AI更准确捕捉病灶负荷动态。TimeLesSeg相关源代码见https://github.com/NeuroADaS-Lab/TimeLesSeg。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for robust MS lesion segmentation across varying scanner types and input modalities, TimeLesSeg introduces a unified, contrast-agnostic framework using a single CNN that handles both cross-sectional and longitudinal scans without requiring temporal information. It models pathological priors via lesion masks, training with empty masks for cross-sectional cases and simulated prior timepoints via stochastic morphological deformation for longitudinal cases. Contrast agnosticism is achieved through domain randomization using Gaussian mixture models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate superior performance over prior methods in both overlap and distance metrics, outperforming SAMSEG and LST-AI in longitudinal segmentation while accurately capturing lesion load dynamics.
为应对多发性硬化症影像中对比度变化和时间维度差异带来的分割挑战，TimeLesSeg提出一种统一的无对比度依赖框架，使用单一卷积神经网络同时处理横断面与纵向扫描。该方法通过病变掩码建模病理先验，对横断面数据采用空掩码训练，对纵向数据则通过随机形态学变形生成合成时间点以缓解数据稀缺问题，并借助高斯混合模型实现对比度不变性。在五个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单模态分割与纵向病变负荷追踪上均优于现有对比度无关方法，且在病变演化捕捉上超越SAMSEG与LST-AI。
TSAssistant: A Human-in-the-Loop Agentic Framework for Automated Target Safety Assessment
Authors: Xiaochen Zheng, Zhiwen Jiang, Melanie Guerard, Klas Hatje, Tatyana Doktorova
First: 2026-04-27T01:22:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:17:38+00:00
Comments: Updated with self-consistency quantitative evaluation; additional quantitative and expert evaluations to be included in future revisions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Target Safety Assessment (TSA) requires systematic integration of heterogeneous evidence, including genetic, transcriptomic, target homology, pharmacological, and clinical data, to evaluate potential safety liabilities of therapeutic targets. This process is inherently iterative and expert-driven, posing challenges in scalability and reproducibility. We present TSAssistant, a multi-agent framework designed to support TSA report drafting through a modular, section-based, and human-in-the-loop paradigm. The framework decomposes report generation into a coordinated pipeline of specialised subagents, each targeting a single TSA section. Specialised subagents retrieve structured and unstructured data as well as literature evidence from curated biomedical sources through standardised tool interfaces, producing individually citable, evidence-grounded sections. Agent behaviour is governed by a hierarchical instruction architecture comprising system prompts, domain-specific skill modules, and runtime user instructions. A key feature is an interactive refinement loop in which users may manually edit sections, append new information, upload additional sources, or re-invoke agents to revise specific sections, with the system maintaining conversational memory across iterations. TSAssistant is designed to reduce the mechanical burden of evidence synthesis and report drafting, supporting a hybrid model in which agentic AI augments evidence synthesis while toxicologists retain final decision authority.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TSAssistant：一种用于自动化靶点安全性评估的人类在环代理框架
靶点安全性评估（TSA）需要系统整合遗传、转录组、靶点同源性、药理学和临床等异质性证据，以评估治疗靶点的潜在安全性风险。该过程本质上是迭代且依赖专家的，存在可扩展性和可重复性挑战。我们提出TSAssistant，一种多代理框架，通过模块化、分章节和人类在环范式支持TSA报告起草。该框架将报告生成分解为协调的专用子代理流水线，每个子代理专注于一个TSA章节。专用子代理通过标准化工具接口从精选的生物医学资源中检索结构化和非结构化数据及文献证据，生成可单独引用、以证据为基础的章节。代理行为由包含系统提示、领域特定技能模块和运行时用户指令的分层指令架构控制。关键特性是交互式优化循环，用户可手动编辑章节、添加新信息、上传额外资源或重新调用代理修订特定章节，系统在迭代过程中保持对话记忆。TSAssistant旨在减轻证据综合与报告起草的机械负担，支持一种混合模式：代理AI辅助证据综合，而毒理学家保留最终决策权。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate yet maintain expert oversight in Target Safety Assessment, TSAssistant introduces a human-in-the-loop multi-agent framework that decomposes report generation into modular, section-specific subagents. Each agent retrieves and synthesizes evidence from structured and unstructured biomedical sources, producing citable, evidence-based sections under a hierarchical instruction system. Experimental results show the framework effectively reduces manual synthesis burden while preserving expert control, with iterative refinement loops enabling users to edit, append, or re-invocate agents, supported by conversational memory across sessions.
为解决靶点安全性评估中专家依赖与可扩展性不足的问题，TSAssistant提出一种人机协同的多智能体框架，将报告生成拆分为模块化、分章节的子智能体。每个智能体从结构化与非结构化生物医学数据中检索并合成证据，生成可引用的、基于证据的章节，受分层指令体系控制。实验表明，该框架有效减轻人工证据整合负担，同时保留专家决策权，支持用户通过编辑、上传新资料或重新调用智能体进行迭代优化，并在多次交互中保持对话记忆。
Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics: Theory and Applications to Training-Free Guided Generation
Authors: Atsushi Nitanda, Dake Bu, Yueming Lyu, Tanya Veeravalli
First: 2026-05-08T16:17:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:17:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We study Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics (SALD), a sampler for tracking a path of moving target distributions and approximating the terminal target through time slowdown. We establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees via a KL differential inequality, showing that slowdown improves tracking through contraction of intermediate targets and the complexity of the path. Motivated by training-free guided generation with pretrained score-based generative models, we further introduce Velocity-Aware SALD (VA-SALD), which explicitly incorporates the underlying marginal distributions of the pretrained model and uses slowdown to correct the additional deviation induced by guidance. This yields a principled framework for training-free guided generation for diffusion-based and related generative model families, together with convergence guarantees that clarify the roles of intermediate functional inequalities and guidance bias. Code is available at https://github.com/anitan0925/sald.
中文标题/摘要
标题：缓慢退火朗之万动力学：理论与无训练引导生成的应用
我们研究了缓慢退火朗之万动力学（SALD），这是一种用于追踪移动目标分布路径并随时间减速逼近终端目标的采样器。我们通过KL微分不等式建立了非渐近收敛保证，表明减速通过收缩中间目标和路径复杂度提升了追踪效果。受预训练分数生成模型无训练引导生成的启发，我们进一步提出了速度感知SALD（VA-SALD），该方法显式纳入预训练模型的边际分布，并利用减速校正引导引起的额外偏差。这为扩散模型及相关生成模型家族提供了无训练引导生成的原理性框架，并附有收敛保证，阐明了中间函数不等式与引导偏差的作用。代码见https://github.com/anitan0925/sald。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for training-free guided generation using pretrained score-based models, this work introduces Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics (SALD), a sampling method that tracks evolving target distributions by slowing down time to improve convergence. The authors establish non-asymptotic convergence via a KL differential inequality, demonstrating that time slowdown enhances tracking by contracting intermediate targets and reducing path complexity. They further propose Velocity-Aware SALD (VA-SALD), which incorporates marginal distributions from pretrained models to correct guidance-induced deviations, enabling principled, convergence-guaranteed generation in diffusion models and related families without retraining.
为实现无需训练的引导生成，该研究提出缓慢退火朗之万动力学（SALD）采样方法，通过减缓时间步长跟踪随时间变化的目标分布并提升终端分布逼近精度。作者通过KL微分不等式建立了非渐近收敛性理论，证明时间减速可收缩中间目标并降低路径复杂度。进一步提出的速度感知SALD（VA-SALD）显式结合预训练模型的边缘分布，校正引导带来的偏差，为扩散模型等生成方法提供了一个具有理论保证的训练免指导生成框架。
Exploring the non-convexity in machine learning using quantum-inspired optimization
Authors: Kandula Eswara Sai Kumar, Parth Dhananjay Danve, Abhishek Chopra, Rut Lineswala
First: 2026-05-08T16:15:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-08T16:15:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The escalating complexity of modern machine learning necessitates solving challenging non-convex optimization problems, particularly in high-dimensional regimes and scenarios contaminated by gross outliers. Traditional approaches, relying on convex relaxations or specialized local search heuristics, frequently succumb to suboptimal local minima and fail to recover the true underlying discrete structures. In this paper, we propose treating these non-convex challenges as a global search problem and introduce a unified framework based on Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Optimization (QIEO). By leveraging a probabilistic representation inspired by quantum superposition, QIEO maintains a global view of the search space, enabling it to tunnel through local optima that trap conventional gradient-based and greedy solvers. We comprehensively evaluate QIEO across diverse non-convex applications, including sparse signal recovery (gene expression analysis and compressed sensing) and robust linear regression. Extensive benchmarking against state-of-the-art continuous solvers (ADAM, Differential Evolution), classical metaheuristics (Genetic Algorithms), and specialized non-convex algorithms (Iterative Hard Thresholding) demonstrates that QIEO consistently achieves superior structural fidelity, lower mean squared error, and enhanced robustness without support inflation. Our findings suggest that embracing a quantum-inspired global search provides a resilient, unified paradigm for overcoming the inherent intractability of discrete nonconvex machine learning landscapes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用量子启发式优化探索机器学习中的非凸性
现代机器学习日益增长的复杂性要求解决高维及含严重异常值场景下的困难非凸优化问题。传统方法依赖凸松弛或专用局部搜索启发式，常陷入次优局部极小值，无法恢复真实离散结构。本文将这些非凸挑战视为全局搜索问题，提出基于量子启发式进化优化（QIEO）的统一框架。QIEO利用受量子叠加启发的概率表示，保持搜索空间全局视角，可穿越传统梯度法和贪心求解器陷入的局部最优。我们在稀疏信号恢复（基因表达分析与压缩感知）和鲁棒线性回归等多样非凸应用中全面评估QIEO，与当前最优连续求解器（ADAM、差分进化）、经典元启发式（遗传算法）及专用非凸算法（迭代硬阈值）的大量基准测试表明，QIEO在结构保真度、均方误差和鲁棒性方面持续优于现有方法，且无需支持集膨胀。研究结果表明，采用量子启发式全局搜索可提供一种稳健统一范式，以应对离散非凸机器学习景观的固有不可解性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to solve complex non-convex optimization problems in high-dimensional machine learning, especially under noisy or outlier-contaminated conditions, this paper introduces Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Optimization (QIEO) as a unified global search framework. QIEO leverages a probabilistic model inspired by quantum superposition to maintain a global perspective of the search space, enabling it to escape local optima that hinder gradient-based or greedy methods. Evaluated across sparse signal recovery and robust regression tasks, QIEO outperforms state-of-the-art solvers including ADAM, Differential Evolution, Genetic Algorithms, and Iterative Hard Thresholding, consistently delivering better structural accuracy, lower mean squared error, and improved robustness without overfitting.
为应对高维机器学习中复杂非凸优化问题，特别是在存在粗大误差的场景下，本文提出量子启发式进化优化（QIEO）框架。该方法通过借鉴量子叠加的概率表示，保持对搜索空间的整体认知，从而有效跳出传统梯度法和贪心算法易陷入的局部最优。在稀疏信号恢复与鲁棒线性回归等任务中，QIEO显著优于ADAM、差分进化、遗传算法及迭代硬阈值等前沿方法，展现出更高的结构保真度、更低的均方误差和更强的鲁棒性，且无需过度拟合。
